习题 2.37 处于无限深方势阱中粒子的初始波函数为(方程 (2.22))

$$
\varPsi (x, 0) = A \sin^ {3} \left(\frac {\pi x}{a}\right) \quad (0 \leqslant x \leqslant a),
$$

求出 $A$ 和l $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ 并计算 $\langle x \rangle$ ，作为时间函数能量的期望值是多少?提示 $\sin ^ { n } \theta$ 和$\cos ^ { n } \theta$ 可以通过重复利用三角公式化简为 $\sin \left( m \theta \right)$ 和 $\cos \left( m \theta \right) \left( m = 0 , 1 , 2 , \cdot \cdot \cdot , n \right)$ 的线性组合.

解答利用二角公式 $\sin 3 \theta = 3 \sin \theta - 4 \sin ^ { 3 } \theta  \sin ^ { 3 } \theta = { \frac { 3 } { 4 } } \sin \theta - { \frac { 1 } { 4 } } \sin 3 \theta ,$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi (x,0)=A \sin ^{3}\left(\frac{\pi x}{a}\right)=A\left[\frac{3}{4} \sin \left(\frac{\pi x}{a}\right)-\frac{1}{4} \sin \left(\frac{3\pi x}{a}\right)\right] \\ = A \sqrt {\frac {a}{2}} \left[ \frac {3}{4} \psi_ {1} (x) - \frac {1}{4} \psi_ {3} (x) \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right) = \sqrt { \frac { 2 } { a } } \sin \left( \frac { n \pi x } { a } \right)$ 是一翩翩方势阱能量的本征函数

对波函数归一化

$$
1 = \int_ {0} ^ {a} | \Psi (x, 0) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = | A | ^ {2} \frac {a}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left| \frac {3}{4} \psi_ {1} (x) - \frac {1}{4} \psi_ {3} (x) \right| ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x
$$

$$
A = \sqrt {\frac {1 6}{5 a}}.
$$

(其中利用了本征态之间的正交性. )所以

$$
\Psi (x, 0) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 0}} [ 3 \psi_ {1} (x) - \psi_ {3} (x) ].
$$

在 $t > 0$ 时刻，被函数为

$$
\Psi (x, t) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 0}} \left[ 3 \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {1} t / \hbar} \psi_ {1} (x) - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {3} t / \hbar} \psi_ {3} (x) \right],
$$

其中 $E _ { n } = { \frac { n ^ { 2 } \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } } }$ 是一雄无限深方势阱能量的本征值

$$
\begin{array}{l} | \Psi (x, t) | ^ {2} = \left| \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 0}} \left[ 3 \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {1} t / \hbar} \psi_ {1} (x) - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {3} t / \hbar} \psi_ {3} (x) \right] \right| ^ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{1 0} \left[ 9 \psi_ {1} ^ {2} + \psi_ {3} ^ {2} - 6 \psi_ {1} \psi_ {3} \cos (\omega t) \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\omega \equiv \frac { E _ { 3 } - E _ { 1 } } { \hbar }$

坐标的期望值为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle = \int_ {0} ^ {a} x | \Psi (x, t) | ^ {2} d x \\ = \frac {9}{1 0} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \psi_ {1} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x + \frac {1}{1 0} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \psi_ {3} ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x - \frac {3}{5} \cos (\omega t) \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \psi_ {1} \psi_ {3} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {9}{1 0} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {1} + \frac {1}{1 0} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {3} - \frac {3}{5} \cos (\omega t) \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \psi_ {1} \psi_ {3} \mathrm {d} x. \\ \end{array}
$$

代入

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle_ {n} = \int_ {0} ^ {a} x | \psi_ {n} | ^ {2} d x \\ = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi x}{a}\right) d x \\ = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \frac {1 - \cos \left(2 \frac {n \pi x}{a}\right)}{2} d x \\ = \frac {a}{2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \psi_ {1} \psi_ {3} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \sin \left(\frac {\pi x}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x}{a}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \left[ \cos \left(\frac {2 \pi x}{a}\right) - \cos \left(\frac {4 \pi x}{a}\right) \right] d x \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

最后得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle = \frac {9}{1 0} \langle x \rangle_ {1} + \frac {1}{1 0} \langle x \rangle_ {3} - \frac {3}{5} \cos (\omega t) \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \psi_ {1} \psi_ {3} d x \\ = \frac {a}{2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

# 习题 2.38

(a) 证明:无限深方势阱中粒子的波函数经历一个量子恢复时间 $T = 4 m a ^ { 2 } / ( \pi \hbar )$ 后，恢复到初始形式，即对于任何态(不仅仅限于定态)， $\varPsi \left( x , T \right) = \varPsi \left( x , 0 \right)$ .  
(b) 能量为 $E$ 的粒子在势阱壁之间往返碰撞，其经典恢复时间是多少?

(c) 在什么样的能量下，两种恢复时间相等?①

解答 (时披函数的一般解可以表示为定态解的线性叠加，即

$$
\varPsi \left(x, t\right) = \sum_ {n} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} \left(x\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar}.
$$

其中定态能量为

$$
E _ {n} = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}, \quad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots .
$$

故

$$
E _ {n} T = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} \frac {4 m a ^ {2}}{\pi \hbar} = 2 n ^ {2} \pi \hbar .
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi (x, t + T) = \sum_ {n} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} (t + T) / \hbar} \\ = \sum_ {n} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n ^ {2} \pi} \\ = \sum_ {n} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \\ = \varPsi (x, t). \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 经典粒子在势阱中来回碰撞的速度为

$$
V = \sqrt {\frac {2 E}{m}}.
$$

所以粒子在势阱中运动一个来回的时间为

$$
T = \frac {2 a}{V} = \frac {2 a}{\sqrt {2 E / m}}.
$$

(c) 让经典的恢复周期与量子恢复周期相等，即

$$
\frac {2 a}{\sqrt {2 E / m}} = \frac {4 m a ^ {2}}{\pi \hbar}
$$

$$
E = \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{8 m a ^ {2}} = \frac {E _ {1}}{4}.
$$

即经典能量等于量子基态能量的 时，两个周期相等.

习题 2.39 在习题 2.7(d) 中，通过对方程 (2.21) 中各项求和可以得到能量的期望值，但是我曾提醒你(教材脚注川不要用"老式的方法" $\left. H \right. = \int \varPsi \left( x , 0 \right) ^ { \ast } H \varPsi ( x ,$ $0 ) \mathrm { d } x$ 亚 $\varPsi \left( x , 0 \right)$ )一阶导数的不连续性会使得二阶导数产生问题.实际中，可以用分部积分法来做，但狄拉克 $\delta$ 函数提供了更为简洁的方法来处理这些反常问题.

(a) 计算l/ $\varPsi \left( x , 0 \right)$ 一阶导数(习题 2.7)，计算结果用方程 (2.146) 中定义的阶跃函数。 $\theta \left( x - a / 2 \right)$ 来表示.

(b) 利用习题 2.23(b) 得到的结果，将! $\varPsi \left( x , 0 \right)$ 的二阶导数用 $\delta$ 函数的形式表示出来.  
ω) 求积分 $\int \boldsymbol { \varPsi } \left( \boldsymbol { x } , 0 \right) ^ { \ast } H \boldsymbol { \psi } \left( \boldsymbol { x } , 0 \right) \mathrm { d } \boldsymbol { x } .$ 。并检验其结果与前面得到的结果一致.

解答 (归)

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} \varPsi}{\mathrm {d} x} = A \times \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} 1 & \\ - 1 & \end{array} \right. = \sqrt {\frac {1 2}{a ^ {3}}} \times \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} 1, & 0 \leqslant x <   \frac {a}{2}, \\ - 1, & \frac {a}{2} \leqslant x \leqslant a. \end{array} \right.
$$

在 $x = a / 2$ 处一阶导数有跃变，引入阶跃函数

$$
\theta \left(x - \frac {a}{2}\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & 0 \leqslant x <   \frac {a}{2}, \\ 1, & \frac {a}{2} \leqslant x \leqslant a. \end{array} \right.
$$

所以可以把一阶导数表示为

(b)

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} \Psi}{\mathrm {d} x} = \sqrt {\frac {1 2}{a ^ {3}}} \left[ 1 - 2 \theta \left(x - \frac {a}{2}\right) \right].
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \Psi}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} = - \sqrt {\frac {1 2}{a ^ {3}}} \times 2 \delta \left(x - \frac {a}{2}\right) = - \frac {4 \sqrt {3}}{a \sqrt {a}} \delta \left(x - \frac {a}{2}\right).
$$

(c)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle = \int_ {0} ^ {a} \Psi^ {*} (x, 0) \left(- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}}\right) \Psi (x, 0) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {4 \sqrt {3}}{a \sqrt {a}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \Psi^ {*} (x, 0) \delta \left(x - \frac {a}{2}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {4 \sqrt {3}}{a \sqrt {a}} \Psi^ {*} \left(\frac {a}{2}, 0\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {4 \sqrt {3}}{a \sqrt {a}} \sqrt {\frac {1 2}{a ^ {3}}} \frac {a}{2} = \frac {6 \hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

这与用 $\langle H \rangle = \sum _ { n } | c _ { n } | ^ { 2 } E _ { n }$ 得到的结果是一致的，说明只要小心处理一阶导数的跃变就可以得到正确结果.

还可以用另一种分区域积分的方法，而无需考虑一阶导数在 $x = a / 2$ 处的跃变.

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle = \int_ {0} ^ {a} \Psi^ {*} (x, 0) \left(\frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m}\right) \Psi (x, 0) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a} [ \hat {p} \Psi (x, 0) ] ^ {*} [ \hat {p} \Psi (x, 0) ] \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} [ \hat {p} \Psi (x, 0) ] ^ {*} [ \hat {p} \Psi (x, 0) ] d x + \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {a / 2} ^ {a} [ \hat {p} \Psi (x, 0) ] ^ {*} [ \hat {p} \Psi (x, 0) ] d x \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} \left[ - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \Psi (x , 0)}{\mathrm {d} x} \right] ^ {*} \left[ - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \Psi (x , 0)}{\mathrm {d} x} \right] \mathrm {d} x \\ + \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {a / 2} ^ {a} \left[ - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \Psi (x , 0)}{\mathrm {d} x} \right] ^ {*} \left[ - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \Psi (x , 0)}{\mathrm {d} x} \right] \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} (- \mathrm {i} \hbar A) ^ {*} (- \mathrm {i} \hbar A) \mathrm {d} x + \frac {1}{2 m} \int_ {a / 2} ^ {a} [ - \mathrm {i} \hbar (- A) ] ^ {*} [ - \mathrm {i} \hbar (- A) ] \mathrm {d} x \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {6 \hbar^ {2}}{m a ^ {2}}.
$$

(这里利用了动量是厄米算符的性质. )

串串习题 2.40 处在谐振子势(方程 (2.44)) 中质量为 $m$ 的粒子，从初态开始演化:

$$
\varPsi (x, 0) = A \left(1 - 2 \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}} x\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x ^ {2}},
$$

其中 $A$ 为某一常数.

(a) 根据谐振子的定态，确定 和该态的展开系数C $c _ { n }$   
(b) 对粒子的能量进行测量，能得到哪些结果?对应的几率是多少?能量的期望值是多少?  
(c) 经过一段时间 $T$ 后的波函数是

$$
\varPsi (x, T) = B \left(1 + 2 \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}} x\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x ^ {2}},
$$

其中 $B$ 为常数. $T$ 的最小可能值是多少?

解答 (司用记号

$$
\xi \equiv \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}} x, \quad \alpha \equiv \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4},
$$

故该初始波函数可以写为

$$
\Psi (x, 0) = A (1 - 2 \xi) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2} = A (1 - 4 \xi + 4 \xi^ {2}) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2}.
$$

可以看出它是谐振子能量本征函数 $\psi _ { 0 } , \psi _ { 1 } , \psi _ { 2 }$ 的结性叠加态

$$
\psi_ {0} (x) = \alpha \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2}, \quad \psi_ {1} (x) = \sqrt {2} \alpha \xi \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2}, \quad \psi_ {2} (x) = \frac {\alpha}{\sqrt {2}} (2 \xi^ {2} - 1) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2},
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi (x, 0) = c _ {0} \psi_ {0} + c _ {1} \psi_ {1} + c _ {2} \psi_ {2} \\ = c _ {0} \alpha \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2} + c _ {1} \sqrt {2} \alpha \xi \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2} + c _ {2} \frac {\alpha}{\sqrt {2}} \left(2 \xi^ {2} - 1\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2} \\ = \alpha \left(c _ {0} - \frac {c _ {2}}{\sqrt {2}} + \sqrt {2} c _ {1} \xi + \sqrt {2} c _ {2} \xi^ {2}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \xi^ {2} / 2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此我们有

$$
\sqrt {2} c _ {2} \alpha = 4 A \rightarrow c _ {2} = 2 \sqrt {2} \frac {A}{\alpha},
$$

$$
\sqrt {2} c _ {1} \alpha = - 4 A \rightarrow c _ {1} = - 2 \sqrt {2} \frac {A}{\alpha},
$$

$$
\alpha c _ {0} - \alpha \frac {c _ {2}}{\sqrt {2}} = A \rightarrow c _ {0} = 3 \frac {A}{\alpha}.
$$

归一化

$$
1 = \left| c _ {0} \right| ^ {2} + \left| c _ {1} \right| ^ {2} + \left| c _ {2} \right| ^ {2} = {\frac {2 5}{\alpha^ {2}}} \left| A \right| ^ {2},   \text {得}   A = {\frac {\alpha}{5}}.
$$

所以

$$
c _ {0} = \frac {3}{5}, \quad c _ {1} = - \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{5}, \quad c _ {2} = \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{5}.
$$

(b) 测量可以得到的能量为 $E _ { 0 } = \frac { 1 } { 2 } \hbar \omega , E _ { 1 } = \frac { 3 } { 2 } \hbar \omega$ __ 和-' $E _ { 2 } = \frac { 5 } { 2 } \hbar \omega$ 各自出现的概率为| $\left| c _ { 0 } \right| ^ { 2 } =$ 言， 11 ${ \frac { 9 } { 2 5 } } , | c _ { 1 } | ^ { 2 } = { \frac { 8 } { 2 5 } }$ 和| $| c _ { 2 } | ^ { 2 } = \frac { 8 } { 2 5 }$

能量的期望值为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle = \sum_ {n} | c _ {n} | ^ {2} E _ {n} = E _ {0} | c _ {0} | ^ {2} + E _ {1} | c _ {1} | ^ {2} + E _ {2} | c _ {2} | ^ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{2} \hbar \omega \frac {9}{2 5} + \frac {3}{2} \hbar \omega \frac {8}{2 5} + \frac {5}{2} \hbar \omega \frac {8}{2 5} \\ = \frac {7 3}{5 0} \hbar \omega . \\ \end{array}
$$

(c) $t > 0$ 时，被函数为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi (x, t) = c _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {0} t / \hbar} \psi_ {0} + c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {1} t / \hbar} \psi_ {1} + c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {2} t / \hbar} \psi_ {2} \\ = \frac {3}{5} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t / 2} \psi_ {0} - \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{5} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 3 \omega t / 2} \psi_ {1} + \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{5} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 5 \omega t / 2} \psi_ {2} \\ = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t / 2} \left(\frac {3}{5} \psi_ {0} - \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{5} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \psi_ {1} + \frac {2 \sqrt {2}}{5} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 \omega t} \psi_ {2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

在时间 $T$ 时，要想得到本题所要求的形式，中间一项必须变号，而最后一项不变号，即要求 $\mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } \omega T } =$ -1 ，产 = ，显然需要 T= T=3 $- 1 , \mathrm { e } ^ { - 2 \mathrm { i } \omega T } = 1$ $\omega T = \pi  T = { \frac { \pi } { \omega } }$

习题 2.41 求半谐振子势所允许的能级，

$$
V \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} (1 / 2)   m \omega^ {2} x ^ {2}, & x > 0, \\ \infty , & x <   0. \end{array} \right.
$$

(例如，这种情况代表一个只能被拉仲而不能被压缩的弹簧.)提示 本题需要仔细思考，具体计算很少.

解答定态薛定诲方程在 $x > 0$ 区域与谐振子的方程元全一样，但是在 $x = 0$ 处波函数必须为零，所以可以从谐振子的本征函数中选出满足在 $x = 0$ 处的能量本征函数，显然 $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ 为奇函数时满足我们的要求，而 $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ 为偶函数时不满足要求.所以半谐振子势的解是谐振子解中 $n = 1 , 3 , 5 , \cdots$ 的那些解能量本征值为

$$
E _ {n} = \left(n + \frac {1}{2}\right) \hbar \omega , \quad n = 1, 3, 5, \dots .
$$

基态为 $n = 1$ 的态，这比谐振子基态能量高 hw.

串串串习题 2.42 在习题 2.21 中，已经对自由粒子静态高斯波包进行了分析.现在对传播中的高斯波包求解同样问题，其初始波函数为

$$
\varPsi (x, 0) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x ^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} l x},
$$

其中 $l$ 是一个实的常数.

[建议从 $\varphi \left( k \right)$ 求 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ 的过程中，在做积分前先做变量替换 $u \equiv k - l . ]$ 部

分答案:

$$
\varPsi \left(x, t\right) = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {1}{\gamma} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a (x - \hbar l t / m) ^ {2} / \gamma^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} l (x - \hbar l t / (2 m))},
$$

其中 $\gamma \equiv \sqrt { 1 + 2 \mathrm { i } a \hbar t / m }$ 注意到 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ 具有一个高斯"波包"调制一列正弦行波结构.波包的速度是多少?行波传播的速度是多少?

解答 (时归一化波函数

$$
1 = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} | \Psi (x, 0) | ^ {2} d x = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} e ^ {- 2 a x ^ {2}} d x = | A | ^ {2} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{2 a}}
$$

$$
A = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4}.
$$

(b) 这个初始被函数可以用自由粒子的能量本征函数(尽管它不是物理上可实现的状态，但是它们的叠加可以是归一化的态)的叠加表示，即

$$
\varPsi (x,0) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \phi (k) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \mathrm {d} k,
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \phi (k) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \Psi (x, 0) e ^ {- i k x} d x \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \left(\frac {a}{2 \pi}\right) \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x ^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} l x} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \left(\frac {a}{2 \pi}\right) \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x ^ {2} - \mathrm {i} (k - l) x} \mathrm {d} x. \\ \end{array}
$$

将指数配平方，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} - a x ^ {2} - \mathrm {i} (k - l) x = - a \left[ x ^ {2} + 2 \frac {\mathrm {i}}{2 a} (k - l) x + \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} (k - l)}{2 a} \right] ^ {2} - \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} (k - l)}{2 a} \right] ^ {2} \right] \\ = - a \left[ x + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{2 a} (k - l) \right] ^ {2} - \frac {(k - l) ^ {2}}{4 a}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \phi (k) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (k - l) ^ {2} / (4 a)} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a [ x + \mathrm {i} (k - l) / (2 a) ] ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (k - l) ^ {2} / (4 a)} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{a}} \\ = \frac {1}{(2 \pi a) ^ {1 / 4}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (k - l) ^ {2} / (4 a)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$t > 0$ 时的波函数为

$$
\Psi (x, t) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi} (2 \pi a) ^ {1 / 4}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (k - l) ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} [ k x - \hbar k ^ {2} t / (2 m) ]} \mathrm {d} k.
$$

首先对指数 $k$ 配平方，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {(k - l) ^ {2}}{4 a} + \mathrm {i} \left(k x - \frac {\hbar k ^ {2} t}{2 m}\right) = - \frac {l ^ {2}}{4 a} - \left[ \left(\frac {1}{4 a} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\hbar t}{2 m}\right) k ^ {2} - \left(\mathrm {i} x + \frac {l}{2 a}\right) k \right] \\ = - \frac {l ^ {2}}{4 a} + \frac {1}{4} \left(\mathrm {i} x + \frac {l}{2 a}\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {1}{4 a} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\hbar t}{2 m}\right) ^ {- 1} \\ - \left(\frac {1}{4 a} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\hbar t}{2 m}\right) \left[ k - \frac {1}{2} \left(\mathrm {i} x + \frac {l}{2 a}\right) \left(\frac {1}{4 a} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\hbar t}{2 m}\right) ^ {- 1} \right] ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

积分，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Psi \left(x, t\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi} \left(2 \pi a\right) ^ {1 / 4}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {a [ \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ] ^ {2} / (1 + 2 \mathrm {i} a \hbar t / m)} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{1 / (4 a) + \mathrm {i} \hbar t / (2 m)}} \\ = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {a [ \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ] ^ {2} / (1 + 2 \mathrm {i} a \hbar t / m)}}{\sqrt {1 + 2 \mathrm {i} \hbar a t / m}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(c) 令。 $\theta \equiv 2 \hbar a t / m$ 所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} | \Psi (x, t) | ^ {2} = \left| \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {a [ \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ] ^ {2} / (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta)}}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} \theta}} \right| ^ {2} \\ = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {a [ \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ] ^ {2} / (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta)} \mathrm {e} ^ {a [ - \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ] ^ {2} / (1 - \mathrm {i} \theta)}}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} \theta} \sqrt {1 - \mathrm {i} \theta}} \\ = \left(\frac {2}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \sqrt {\frac {a}{1 + \theta^ {2}}} \exp \left[ - \frac {2 a}{1 + \theta^ {2}} \left(x - \frac {\theta l}{2 a}\right) ^ {2} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

令 四三 $w \equiv \sqrt { \frac { a } { 1 + \theta ^ { 2 } } }$ 言，则

$$
| \varPsi (x, t) | ^ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} w \exp \left[ - 2 w ^ {2} \left(x - \frac {\theta l}{2 a}\right) ^ {2} \right].
$$

(d)坐标期望值

$$
\langle x \rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x | \Psi (x, t) | ^ {2} d x = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x w \exp \left[ - 2 w ^ {2} \left(x - \frac {\theta l}{2 a}\right) ^ {2} \right] d x.
$$

令y=x $y \equiv x - \frac { \theta l } { 2 a } = x - \frac { \hbar l } { m } t = x - v t _ { \mathrm { : } }$ ，其中 $v = { \frac { \hbar l } { m } }$ 是被包的群速度，则

$$
\langle x \rangle = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} (y + v t) w \exp (- 2 w ^ {2} y ^ {2}) \mathrm {d} y = v t.
$$

(其中第一例被积函数是奇函机分为零第三个积 $\int _ { - \infty } ^ { \infty } \exp \left( - 2 w ^ { 2 } y ^ { 2 } \right) \mathrm { d } y = \sqrt { \frac { \pi } { 2 w ^ { 2 } } } . )$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} (y + v t) ^ {2} w \exp \left(- 2 w ^ {2} y ^ {2}\right) d y \\ = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(y ^ {2} + 2 y v t + v ^ {2} t ^ {2}\right) w \exp \left(- 2 w ^ {2} y ^ {2}\right) d y \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} w \frac {1}{2 (2 w ^ {2})} \sqrt {\frac {\pi}{2 w ^ {2}}} + 0 + (v t) ^ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{4 w ^ {2}} + (v t) ^ {2}. \\ \langle p \rangle = m \frac {\mathrm {d} \langle x \rangle}{\mathrm {d} x} = m v = \hbar l. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\left\langle p ^ {2} \right\rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \Psi^ {*} (x, t) \hat {p} ^ {2} \Psi (x, t) d x = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} [ \hat {p} \Psi (x, t) ] ^ {*} [ \hat {p} \Psi (x, t) ] d x.
$$

由前面求出的 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ ，得

$$
\hat {p} \Psi (x, t) = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} \Psi}{\mathrm {d} t} = \frac {2 \hbar a [ \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ]}{1 + \mathrm {i} \theta} \Psi .
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle p ^ {2} \right\rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left[ \frac {2 \hbar a [ \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ]}{1 + \mathrm {i} \theta} \Psi \right] ^ {*} \left[ \frac {2 \hbar a [ \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ]}{1 + \mathrm {i} \theta} \Psi \right] \mathrm {d} x \\ = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left[ \frac {2 \hbar a [ - \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ]}{1 - \mathrm {i} \theta} \Psi^ {*} \right] \left[ \frac {2 \hbar a [ \mathrm {i} x + l / (2 a) ]}{1 + \mathrm {i} \theta} \Psi \right] \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {(2 \hbar a) ^ {2}}{1 + \theta^ {2}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} [ x ^ {2} + [ l / (2 a) ] ^ {2} ] \Psi^ {*} \Psi d x \\ = \frac {\left(2 \hbar a\right) ^ {2}}{1 + \theta^ {2}} \left[ \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle + [ l / (2 a) ] ^ {2} \right] = \frac {\left(2 \hbar a\right) ^ {2}}{1 + \theta^ {2}} \left[ \frac {1}{4 w ^ {2}} + (v t) ^ {2} + [ l / (2 a) ] ^ {2} \right] \\ = \hbar^ {2} \left[ \frac {a ^ {2}}{w ^ {2} (1 + \theta^ {2})} + \frac {(2 a v t) ^ {2} + l ^ {2}}{1 + \theta^ {2}} \right] \\ = \hbar^ {2} \left[ a + \frac {(2 a \hbar l t / m) ^ {2} + l ^ {2}}{1 + (2 a \hbar t / m) ^ {2}} \right] = \hbar^ {2} \left(a + l ^ {2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma_ {x} = \sqrt {\left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle - \left\langle x \right\rangle^ {2}} = \sqrt {\frac {1}{4 w ^ {2}} + (v t) ^ {2} - (v t) ^ {2}} = \frac {1}{2 w}, \\ \sigma_ {p} = \sqrt {\langle p ^ {2} \rangle - \langle p \rangle^ {2}} = \sqrt {\hbar^ {2} a + (\hbar l) ^ {2} - (\hbar l) ^ {2}} = \hbar \sqrt {a}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(e) 动量与坐标满足不确定关系

$$
\sigma_ {x} \sigma_ {p} = \frac {\hbar \sqrt {a}}{2 w} = \frac {\hbar \sqrt {a}}{2 \sqrt {a} / (1 + \theta^ {2})} = \frac {\hbar}{2} \left[ 1 + (2 \hbar a t / m) ^ {2} \right] \geqslant \frac {\hbar}{2}.
$$

串串习题 2.43 在原点对称的无限深方势阱的中间存在一个 $\delta$ 函数势垒，

$$
V \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \alpha \delta \left(x\right), & - a <   x <   + a, \\ \infty , & | x | \geqslant a. \end{array} \right.
$$

求解定态薛定海方程.分别处理波函数为偶和奇波函数的情况.没必要花时间去归一化这些波函数.求出能量的允许值(必要时可用作图法).与没有 $\delta$ 函数存在时的情况相比，相应的能级有何不同?解释为什么奇函数解不受 $\delta$ 函数的影响.讨论$\alpha  0$ 和 $\alpha \to \infty$ 两种极限情况.

解答 对偶函数解 $\psi \left( x \right) = \psi \left( - x \right)$ ，在 $0 < x < a$ 和 $- a < x < 0$ 两个区域，定态薛定诗方程的解为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} A \sin (k x) + B \cos (k x), & 0 <   x <   a, \\ - A \sin (k x) + B \cos (k x), & - a <   x <   0, \end{array} \right.
$$

其中 $k \equiv \frac { \sqrt { 2 m E } } { \hbar }$ h

在 $x = 0$ 处波函数连续己经满足 $B = B$ $\delta$ 函数势引起波函数导数不连续，由方程(1.128)

$$
k A + k A = \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} B
$$

$$
B = \frac {\hbar^ {2} k}{m \alpha} A.
$$

在 $x = a$ 处的边界条件给出

$$
A \left[ \sin (k a) + \frac {\hbar^ {2} k}{m \alpha} \cos (k a) \right] = 0.
$$

由此得到能级满足的方程为

$$
\tan (k a) = - \frac {\hbar^ {2} k}{m \alpha}.
$$

数值求解该超越方程(图 2.12) 可以得到解.

从图 2.12 中可以看出，解得的 $k a$ 值略大于

$$
n \pi / 2, \quad n = 1, 2, 3, \dots ,
$$

而且随着 $n$ 的增加越来越靠近 $\frac { n \pi } { 2 }$ 所以能量本征值为

$$
E _ {n} \geqslant \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m (2 a) ^ {2}}.
$$

![](images/1e8a3970b71e533b037b729058dac297fa22db4a3578051ad2bc8e8ca670f433.jpg)  
2.12

此式右边为阱宽为 $2 a$ 的无限深方势阱的能量本征值，所以 $\delta$ 势在势阱中心存在的情况下 能量本征值比没有时略有增加.当 $\delta$ 势的强度减弱 $\overset { \cdot } { \alpha }$ 减小)时，图中直线变

得更加倾斜 $E _ { n }$ 将更加接近于阱宽为 $2 a$ 的无限深方势阱的能量本征值.当 $\delta$ 势的强度增加 $\overset { \cdot } { \alpha }$ 增 大)时，图中直线将变得比较水平 $k a$ 将接近 $n \pi , E _ { n }$ 将接近阱宽为 $a$ 的无限深方势阱的能量本征值 $\frac { n ^ { 2 } \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } } . \alpha  \infty$ ， $E _ { n } = { \frac { n ^ { 2 } \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } } }$ 心势垒把势阱分割成两个孤立的阱宽为 $a$ 的无限深方势阱 ~mαa

奇函数解满足 $\psi \left( x \right) = - \psi \left( - x \right)$ ，在 $0 < x < a$ 和 $- a < x < 0$ 两个区域，定态薛定诗方程的解为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} A \sin (k x) + B \cos (k x), & 0 <   x <   a, \\ A \sin (k x) - B \cos (k x), & - a <   x <   0. \end{array} \right.
$$

在 $x = 0$ 处波函数连续要求 $B = 0$ $\delta$ 函数势引起)，波函数导数跃变给出 $k A - k A = 0$ (自然满足).

在 $x = a$ 处边界条件给出 $A \sin ( k a ) = 0$

由此得到能级满足的方程为

$$
k a = \frac {n \pi}{2}, \quad n = 2, 4, 6, \dots ,
$$

即

$$
E _ {n} = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m (2 a) ^ {2}}, \quad n = 2, 4, 6, \dots .
$$

这正是阱宽为 $2 a$ 的无限深方势阱中 $n$ 为偶数的能量本征值，所以 $\delta$ 势在势阱中心存在的情况下，对奇函数解能量本征值没有影响.这是因为波函数在势阱中心为零，所以感受不到此处 $\delta$ 势的影响

习题 2.44 如果两个(或更多)定态薛定评方程的不同解具有同一个能量 EF些态是简井的例如:自由粒子态是双重简并的一一一个解代表向右运动，另一个解代表向左运动.但从未遇到过可归一化的简并解，这并非偶然.证明如下定理:一维情况下( $( - \infty < x < \infty )$ ， (2)不存在简并束缚态.提示 假设存在两个解 $\psi _ { 1 }$ 和 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 具有同样的能量 $E$ ，将 $\psi _ { 1 }$ 满足的薛定诗方程乘以 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 将 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 满足的薛定海方程乘以$\psi _ { 1 }$ ，然后两式相减，证明: $( \psi _ { 2 } \mathrm { d } \psi _ { 1 } / \mathrm { d } x - \psi _ { 1 } \mathrm { d } \psi _ { 2 } / \mathrm { d } x )$ 是个常数.利用归一化解在 $\pm \infty$ 处应满足 $\psi \to 0$ 的事实，证明这个常数事实上为军.从而得出结 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 如是 $\psi _ { 1 }$ 乘以一个常数因子，因此两个解是相同的.

证明 设 $\psi _ { 1 } , \psi _ { 2 }$ 是定态薛定诗方程具有同一个能量 $E$ 的不同的解，即

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {1}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + V \psi_ {1} = E \psi_ {1}, \\ - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + V \psi_ {2} = E \psi_ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

第一个式子乘以 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 第二个式子乘以 $\psi _ { 1 }$ 两式相减，得

$$
\psi_ {2} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {1}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} - \psi_ {1} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} = 0,
$$

进一步得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} \left(\psi_ {2} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {1}}{\mathrm {d} x} - \psi_ {1} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) = 0.
$$

这表明

$$
\psi_ {2} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {1}}{\mathrm {d} x} - \psi_ {1} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x} = K (\text {常 数}).
$$

考虑到 $x \to \infty$ 时 $\psi _ { 1 }  0 , \psi _ { 2 }  0$ (束缚态归一化的要求)，所以 $K = 0$ 因此

$$
\frac {1}{\psi_ {1}} \frac {d \psi_ {1}}{\mathrm {d} x} = \frac {1}{\psi_ {2}} \frac {d \psi_ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x},
$$

得

$$
\ln \psi_ {1} = \ln \psi_ {2} + \text {常 数}, \text {即} \psi_ {1} = \text {常 数} \times \psi_ {2}.
$$

即这两个解仅相差一个常数，归一化后，它们仅相差一个相因子，因此它们代表同一个物理态.

习题 在本题中，你将证明一维势中定态的节点数目总是随能量的增加而增加.③对于给定的势 $V \left( x \right)$ 考虑定态薛定海方程的两个解(实数归一化的解， $\psi _ { n }$ 和$\psi _ { m , \ l }$ ，能量满足 $E _ { n } > E _ { m }$ .

(a) 证明:

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {m}}{\mathrm {d} x} \psi_ {n} - \psi_ {m} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {n}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) = \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} \left(E _ {n} - E _ {m}\right) \psi_ {m} \psi_ {n}.
$$

(b) 设 $x _ { 1 }$ 和 $x _ { 2 }$ 是波函数 $\psi _ { m } \left( x \right)$ 的邻近两个节点.证明:

$$
\psi_ {m} ^ {\prime} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) - \psi_ {m} ^ {\prime} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) = \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} (E _ {n} - E _ {m}) \int_ {x _ {1}} ^ {x _ {2}} \psi_ {m} \psi_ {n} \mathrm {d} x.
$$

(c) 如果 $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ 在 $x _ { 1 }$ 和 $x _ { 2 }$ 之间不存在节点，那么在此区间内都有相同的符号.证明 (b) 将导致一个矛盾.因此，在 $\psi _ { m } \left( x \right)$ 的每对节点之间， $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ 至少有一个节点，且节点的数量随能量的增加而增加.

证明 (a) 处于某一本征态 $\psi _ { n }$ 的薛定海方程为

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {n}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + V \psi_ {n} = E _ {n} \psi_ {n}.
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {n}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} = - \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} (E _ {n} - V) \psi_ {n}. \tag {1}
$$

同理

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {m}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} = - \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} (E _ {m} - V) \psi_ {m}. \tag {②}
$$

$\textcircled { \ 1 } \times \psi _ { m } - \textcircled { 2 } \times \psi _ { n }$ 可得，左边为

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {n}}{\mathrm {d} x} \psi_ {m} - \psi_ {n} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {m}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right),
$$

右边为

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} \psi_ {m} (E _ {n} - V) \psi_ {n} + \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} \psi_ {n} (E _ {m} - V) \psi_ {m} \\ = - \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} \left(E _ {n} - E _ {m}\right) \psi_ {m} \psi_ {n}, \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，得证

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {m}}{\mathrm {d} x} \psi_ {n} - \psi_ {m} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {n}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) = \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} \left(E _ {n} - E _ {m}\right) \psi_ {m} \psi_ {n}.
$$

(b) 对(司的结果两边进行积分，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {x _ {1}} ^ {x _ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {m}}{\mathrm {d} x} \psi_ {n} - \psi_ {m} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {n}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \psi_ {m} ^ {\prime} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) - \psi_ {m} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {n} ^ {\prime} (x _ {2}) - \psi_ {m} ^ {\prime} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) + \psi_ {m} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {n} ^ {\prime} (x _ {1}). \\ \end{array}
$$

由于 $x _ { 1 }$ 和 $x _ { 2 }$ 是 $\psi _ { m } \left( x \right)$ 的两个节点，所以 $\psi _ { m } \left( x _ { 1 } \right) = \psi _ { m } \left( x _ { 2 } \right) = 0$ ，因此，上式可化简为

$$
\psi_ {m} ^ {\prime} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) - \psi_ {m} ^ {\prime} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) = \frac {2 m}{\hbar^ {2}} (E _ {n} - E _ {m}) \int_ {x _ {1}} ^ {x _ {2}} \psi_ {m} \psi_ {n} \mathrm {d} x.
$$

(c) 因为 $x _ { 1 }$ 和 $x _ { 2 }$ 是 $\psi _ { m }$ 的两个相邻节点，所以 $\psi _ { m }$ 在此间隔区间内部为正值或者都为负值.假定 $\psi _ { m }$ 为正，那么

$$
\psi_ {m} \left(x\right) \geqslant 0 \quad \left(x _ {1} \leqslant x \leqslant x _ {2}\right), \quad \psi^ {\prime} \left(x _ {1}\right) \geqslant 0   \text {和}     \psi^ {\prime} \left(x _ {2}\right) \leqslant 0.
$$

如果 $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ 在 $x _ { 1 }$ 和 $x _ { 2 }$ Z之间没有节点， $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ 在此间隔内也必有相同的符号(我们同样选择它为正号):

$$
\psi_ {n} (x) \geqslant 0 \quad (x _ {1} \leqslant x \leqslant x _ {2}).
$$

这样的话， $\psi _ { m } ^ { \prime } \left( x _ { 2 } \right) \psi _ { n } \left( x _ { 2 } \right) - \psi _ { m } ^ { \prime } \left( x _ { 1 } \right) \psi _ { n } \left( x _ { 1 } \right) \leqslant 0 .$ ，但是 $\frac { 2 m } { \hbar ^ { 2 } } \left( E _ { n } - E _ { m } \right) \int _ { x _ { 1 } } ^ { x _ { 2 } } \psi _ { m } \psi _ { n } \mathrm { d } x \geqslant 0 .$ 这与中的结论矛盾.所以， $\psi _ { n } \left( x \right)$ 在 $x _ { 1 }$ 和 $x _ { 2 }$ 之间至少存在一个节点.

习题 2.46 假设质量为 $m$ 的小珠子，绕着周长为 $L$ 的圆线环做无摩擦滑动. (这与自由粒子相似，只不过 $\psi ( x + L ) = \psi ( x ) . )$ )求出它的定态(并适当归一化)和相应的能量允许值.注意到对于每个能级 $E _ { n }$ 只有一个例外)都有两个相互独立的解，分别对应顺时针和逆时针运动情况;记为 $\psi _ { n } ^ { + } \left( x \right)$ 和 $\psi _ { n } ^ { - } \left( x \right)$ . 根据习题 .4 中的定理，如何解释此种简井性(为什么在此情况下定理会不成立)?

解答是态薛定诗方程为

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} = E \psi ,
$$

其解为

$$
\psi (x) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x},
$$

其中 $k \equiv \frac { \sqrt { 2 m E } } { \hbar }$

考虑到周期性边界条件

$$
\psi (x + L) = \psi (x),
$$

$$
A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k L} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k L} = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}.
$$

该式对任何 $x$ 都应该成立，比如将, ------.. $x = 0$ . $x = { \frac { \pi } { 2 k } }$ 这两个值分别代入，得--.. . -_....... -,

$$
A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k L} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k L} = A + B,
$$

$$
A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k L} - B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k L} = A - B.
$$

两式相加，得

$$
2 A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k L} = 2 A.
$$

这表明，要么

$\mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k L } = 1$ ~ ，得 $l = 2 n \pi$ , $n = 0 , \pm 1 , \pm 2 , \pm 3 , \cdot \cdot \cdot _ { ; }$

要么 $A = 0$ 但是 $A = 0$ 意味着 $B  { \mathrm { e } } ^ { - \mathrm { i } k L } = B$ 要想得到非零解，只有

$\mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } k L } = 1$ ，得 kl = 2n n=O ，土 ，土 ，…，

会得到同样的结论.所以定态解可以表示为

$$
\psi (x) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}, \quad n = 0, 1, 2, 3, \dots ,
$$

披函数归一化

$$
1 = \int_ {0} ^ {L} | \psi_ {n} | ^ {2} d x = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {L} d x = | A | ^ {2} L
$$

$$
A = \frac {1}{\sqrt {L}}.
$$

能量本征值为

$$
E _ {n} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\frac {2 \pi n}{L}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {2 n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m L ^ {2}}.
$$

显然

$$
\psi_ {n} ^ {+} (x) = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}}
$$

与

$$
\psi_ {n} ^ {-} (x) = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 n \pi x / L}}{\sqrt {L}}
$$

是具有同样能量的本征态，所以是二重筒并的习题 .4 中所证明的定理在这里不成立，是因为 $\psi \left( x \right)$ 在无限远处不为零，由于周期性，我们把 $x$ 限制在一个有限范围，所以我们不能确定习题 2.44 中的常数 $K$ 为零.

**习题 2.47 注意这是一个严格的定性问题一一不允许做任何计算!考虑"双方势阱"势(图 2.13). 假设阱深 $V _ { 0 }$ 和阱宽 $a$ 都固定，且足够大使得可以存在几个束缚态.

(a) 分别画出 (i) $b = 0$ , (ii) $b \approx a$ (iii)$b \gg a$ 这三种情况下的基态波函数 $\psi _ { 1 }$ 和第一激发态波函数 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 草图.  
(b) 定性描述当 $b$ 变化到 $\infty$ ，$\psi _ { 1 }$ 和 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 相对应的能级 $\boldsymbol { \mathrm { ~ E _ { 1 } ~ } }$ 和 $E _ { 2 }$ 的变化趋势.在同一回中画出 $E _ { 1 } \left( b \right)$ 和 $E _ { 2 } \left( b \right)$ .

![](images/2479588fed1cfdad467cfe096561f7c708c7841428123ce740979b4d37d57fbf.jpg)  
2.13 双方势阱

双阱模型是一个基本的一维模型，用来描述双原子分子中电子所受到的势场作用(两个势阱代表两个原子核的吸引力).如果两核被看成是自由移动的，它们将遵循最小能量分布.根据 (b) 中所得结论，电子更趋向于使两个核靠在一起，还是使其分离呢? (当然两个核之间存在排斥力，不过这是另外一个问题.)

解答 (a) (i) $b = 0$ ，变成一个阱宽为 $2 a$ 的有限探方势阱问题.在阱外指数衰减，阱内正弦或余弦振荡(对基态是 $\psi _ { 1 } \propto \cos ( k x )$ 对第一激发态是 $\psi _ { 2 } \propto \sin ( k x )$ ，在 $x = 0$ 处有一个节点).基态是偶函数，第一激发态是奇函数，如圄 所示.

(ii) $b \approx a$ 在 $- b / 2 < x < b / 2$ 区域，基态是双曲余弦，第一激发态是双曲正弦，其余同上，如图2.15 所示.  
(iii) $b \gg a$ 一样，但是由于 $b$ 比较大，在 $- b / 2 < x < b / 2$ 区域披函数值很小，基本与两个孤立有限探方势阱情况一样. $\psi _ { 1 }$ 与 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 能量非常接近(兼并)，它们是两个孤立有限深方势阱基态披函数的线性叠加，一个是偶函数，一个是奇函数，如图 所示.

![](images/b744c47e9108e453a6294a738484c07444e28fd8f5c47d1616d84f607af57072.jpg)

![](images/645da031db6bd8130774086b4b302b4be82eec188615fec420090d28c72205f7.jpg)  
2.14

![](images/f27de2878debade8e5c9a4407835c5ba9fc73b4dd2d1874c6e90b0e540c3e461.jpg)  
基态波函数

![](images/b01fd61237db06ed3eadcf691476f28af7c9b96f7a6b31323192c79ca66ab73d.jpg)  
第一激发态波函数

![](images/afa8f5f8dc07e20c1574528338a714164852ce582cae677e3e98497f087e9060.jpg)  
2.15

![](images/fd4df9631346558b3f2a0d65e6a5ab8ef1e8f1512ab36c996f7d06b2f91fbf88.jpg)  
2.16

(b) $b = 0$ 时，阱宽为 $2 a$ 的有限深方势阱，基态能量和第一激发态能量略小于无限深方势阱的相应能量

![](images/dda28fb8d168b0c53adb7745ce6e1d9076fcf664e2b681f16e6d616f536c603d.jpg)  
2.17

$$
\begin{array}{l} E _ {1} + V _ {0} \approx \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m (2 a) ^ {2}}, \\ E _ {2} + V _ {0} \approx \frac {4 \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m (2 a) ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

当 $b \gg a$ 时， $\psi _ { 1 }$ 与 $\psi _ { 2 }$ 简并，能量略小于阱宽为 $a$ 的无限深方势阱的基态能量

$$
E _ {1} + V _ {0} \approx E _ {2} + V _ {0} \approx \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}.
$$

能量随 $b$ 增加的变化如图 2.17 所示.

在(偶函数的)基态，两个原子越靠近，能量越低，电子倾向于把原子核吸引在一起，有利于原子的结合.而对(奇函数的)第一激发态，两个原子越靠近，能量越高 电子倾向于使原子核分离，不利于原子的结合.

习题 .4 $m$ 的粒子处在如下势场中：

$$
V \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \infty , & x <   0, \\ - 3 2 \hbar^ {2} / (m a) ^ {2}, & 0 \leqslant x \leqslant a, \\ 0, & x > a. \end{array} \right.
$$

(a) 存在多少个束缚态?  
(b) 对最高束缚态能级，粒子在阱外 $( x > a )$ 被发现的几率是多少?答案: 0.542,即便是它被"束缚"在势阱内，它在势阱外被观察到的可能性比势阱内还要大!

解答 (a) 设 $V _ { 0 } \equiv 3 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } / m a ^ { 2 }$ 定态薛定诗方程在三个区域的束缚态 $( E < 0 )$ 解为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & x <   0, \\ A \sin (l x) + B \cos (l x), & 0 \leqslant x \leqslant a, \\ C \mathrm {e} ^ {- \kappa x} + D \mathrm {e} ^ {\kappa x}, & x > a, \end{array} \right.
$$

其中 $l \equiv \frac { \sqrt { 2 m \left( E + V _ { 0 } \right) } } { \hbar } , \kappa \equiv \frac { \sqrt { - 2 m E } } { \hbar } .$ $\kappa \equiv \frac { \sqrt { - 2 m E } } { \hbar }$

由 $x \to \infty$ 时波函数有限的条件，得 $D = 0$ ，由 $x = 0$ 时波函数连续的条件，得 $B = 0$ 所以

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & x <   0, \\ A \sin (l x), & 0 \leqslant x \leqslant a, \\ C \mathrm {e} ^ {- \kappa x}, & x > a. \end{array} \right.
$$

由波函数及其导数在 $x = a$ 处的连续条件，得

$$
A \sin (l a) = F \mathrm {e} ^ {- \kappa a},
$$

$$
l A \cos (l a) = - \kappa F e ^ {- \kappa a}.
$$

两式相除

$$
l \cot (l a) = - \kappa ,
$$

或者

$$
- \cot (l a) = \kappa / l.
$$

$$
l a \equiv z, \quad z _ {0} \equiv \frac {\sqrt {2 m V _ {0}}}{\hbar} a = \frac {\sqrt {2 m \left[ 3 2 \hbar^ {2} / (m a) ^ {2} \right]}}{\hbar} a = 8
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {z _ {0} ^ {2} - z ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}} = \frac {2 m V _ {0} a ^ {2} / \hbar^ {2} - 2 m (E + V _ {0}) a ^ {2} / \hbar^ {2}}{l ^ {2} a ^ {2}} \\ = \frac {- 2 m E / \hbar^ {2}}{l ^ {2}} = \frac {\kappa^ {2}}{l ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
- \cot z = \sqrt {z _ {0} ^ {2} / z ^ {2} - 1} = \sqrt {6 4 / z ^ {2} - 1}.
$$

数值求解该超越方程(如图 18 所示).

由图 18 知存在三个束缚态解，分别对应 $z$ ~出，相应的束缚态能量为

$$
E = \frac {z ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} - V _ {0} = \frac {z ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} - \frac {6 4 \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} <   0, \quad (z <   8).
$$

![](images/f9db011b6e5321f3e76e54bb410c1371a6af07674ddef8012863bb9c0f6a8219.jpg)  
2.18

(b) 粒子处于阱内的几率为

$$
P _ {1} = \int_ {0} ^ {a} | \psi | ^ {2} d x = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \sin^ {2} (l x) d x
$$

$$
= | A | ^ {2} \left[ \frac {a}{2} - \frac {1}{2 k} \sin (l a) \cos (l a) \right].
$$

粒子处于阱外的几率为

$$
P _ {2} = \int_ {a} ^ {\infty} | \psi | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = | C | ^ {2} \int_ {a} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \kappa x} \mathrm {d} x = | C | ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \kappa a}}{2 \kappa} = | A | ^ {2} \frac {\sin^ {2} (l a)}{2 \kappa}.
$$

最后一步利用了波函数在 $x = a$ 处连续的条件，得 $C \mathrm { e } ^ { - \kappa a } = A \sin ( l a )$

这两个几率之和应该等于 (归一化)，即

$$
1 = P _ {1} + P _ {2} = | A | ^ {2} \left[ \frac {a}{2} - \frac {1}{2 l} \sin (l a) \cos (l a) \right] + | A | ^ {2} \frac {\sin^ {2} (l a)}{2 \kappa}.
$$

将 $- \cot ( l a ) = \kappa / l$ 代入上式，得

$$
1 = \frac {1}{2 \kappa} | A | ^ {2} \left[ \kappa a + \cot (l a) \sin (l a) \cos (l a) + \sin^ {2} (l a) \right] = \frac {1}{2 \kappa} | A | ^ {2} (\kappa a + 1).
$$

所以

$$
\left| A \right| ^ {2} = \frac {2 \kappa}{\kappa a + 1}.
$$

粒子处于阱外的儿率可以表示为

$$
P _ {2} = | A | ^ {2} \frac {\sin^ {2} (l a)}{2 \kappa} = \frac {\sin^ {2} (l a)}{\kappa a + 1}.
$$

将 $\sin ^ { 2 } { ( l a ) } = \sin ^ { 2 } { z } = { \frac { 1 } { 1 + \cot ^ { 2 } { z } } } = { \frac { 1 } { 1 + z _ { 0 } ^ { 2 } / z ^ { 2 } - 1 } } = { \frac { z ^ { 2 } } { z _ { 0 } ^ { 2 } } } , \kappa a = { \sqrt { z _ { 0 } ^ { 2 } - z ^ { 2 } } }$ ~2 代入上式，得

$$
P _ {2} = \frac {z ^ {2}}{z _ {0} ^ {2} \left(1 + \sqrt {z _ {0} ^ {2} - z ^ {2}}\right)} = \frac {z ^ {2}}{6 4 \left(1 + \sqrt {6 4 - z ^ {2}}\right)}.
$$

对最高能级的束缚态 $z \approx 7 . 9$ 计算出 $P _ { \mathrm { 2 } } \approx 0 . 5 4$ ，这比处在阱内的几率还要大.

串串串习题 2.49

(a) 证明:

$$
\Psi \left(x, t\right) = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left\{- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left[ x ^ {2} + \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} \left(1 + \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) + \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar t}{m} - 2 x _ {0} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \right] \right\}
$$

满足谐振子势的合时薛定诗方程(方程 (2.44)). 这里 $x _ { 0 }$ 是一个具有长度量纲的任意实数.①

(b) $\left| \psi \left( x , t \right) \right| ^ { 2 }$ ，并描述波包的运动.  
(c) 计算 $\langle x \rangle$ 和( $\langle p \rangle$ ，并检验它们满足埃伦菲斯特定律(方程(1.38)).

解答 (均分别计 $\frac { \partial \psi } { \partial t }$ 。和,- $\frac { \partial ^ { 2 } \psi } { \partial x ^ { 2 } }$

$$
\frac {\partial \psi}{\partial t} = \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}\right) \left[ \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} \left(- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) + \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} - 2 x _ {0} x (- \mathrm {i} \omega) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \right] \psi ,
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial x} = \left[ \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}\right) \left(2 x - 2 x _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) \right] \psi = \left[ \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) \left(x - x _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) \right] \psi . \\ \frac {\partial^ {2} \psi}{\partial x ^ {2}} = \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) \psi + \left[ \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) \left(x - x _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) \right] \frac {\partial \psi}{\partial x} \\ = \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) \psi + \left[ \left(- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) \left(x - x _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) \right] ^ {2} \psi \\ = \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} + \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \left(x ^ {2} - 2 x _ {0} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} + x _ {0} ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) \right] \psi . \\ \end{array}
$$

将上式代入薛定海方程 $\mathrm { i } \hbar \frac { \partial \psi } { \partial t } \ = \ \left( - \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m } \frac { \mathrm { d } ^ { 2 } } { \mathrm { d } x ^ { 2 } } + \frac { 1 } { 2 } m \omega ^ { 2 } x ^ { 2 } \right) \psi$ ，就可以发现 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ 是满足薛定诲方程的.

(b) 披函数的平方

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \Psi (x, t) \right| ^ {2} = \left| \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left\{- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left[ x ^ {2} + \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} \left(1 + \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) + \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar t}{m} - 2 x _ {0} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \right] \right\} \right| ^ {2} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \exp \left\{- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left[ x ^ {2} + \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} (1 + \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t}) + \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar t}{m} - 2 x _ {0} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \right] \right. \\ \left. - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left[ x ^ {2} + \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} \left(1 + \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) - \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar t}{m} - 2 x _ {0} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \right] \right\} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \exp \left\{- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \left[ x ^ {2} - \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} - \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{4} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} + \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) - x _ {0} x \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t} + \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) \right] \right\} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \exp \left\{- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \left[ x ^ {2} - \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} - \frac {x _ {0} ^ {2}}{2} \cos (2 \omega t) - 2 x _ {0} x \cos (\omega t) \right] \right\} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}} \exp \left\{- \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} [ x - x _ {0} \cos (\omega t) ] ^ {2} \right\}. \\ \end{array}
$$

可以看出这是一个形状不变的高斯波包但是波包的中心做振幅为 $a$ 、频率为 $\omega$ 的余弦振荡.

（c）

$$
\langle x \rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x | \psi (x, t) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x \exp \left\{- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} [ x - x _ {0} \cos (\omega t) ] ^ {2} \right\} \mathrm {d} x.
$$

作变量代换，令

$$
\begin{array}{l} y = x - x _ {0} \cos (\omega t), \\ \langle x \rangle = \frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} [ y + x _ {0} \cos (\omega t) ] \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} y ^ {2}\right) d x \\ = x _ {0} \cos (\omega t), \\ \langle p \rangle = m \frac {\mathrm {d} \langle x \rangle}{\mathrm {d} t} = - m x _ {0} \omega \sin (\omega t), \\ \frac {\mathrm {d} \left<   p \right > }{\mathrm {d} t} = m x _ {0} \omega^ {2} \cos \left(\omega t\right), \\ \left\langle - \frac {\partial V}{\partial x} \right\rangle = m \omega^ {2} \left\langle x \right\rangle = m \omega^ {2} x _ {0} \cos (\omega t). \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} \left<   p \right > }{\mathrm {d} t} = \left<   - \frac {\partial V}{\partial x} \right > .
$$

满足埃伦菲斯特定理.

串串习题 2.50 考虑运动的 $\delta$ 函数势阱:

$$
V (x, t) = - \alpha \delta (x - v t),
$$

其中 $v$ 常数)是势阱的运动速度.

(a) 证明:舍时薛定湾方程可以有严格解①

$$
\varPsi \left(x, t\right) = \frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- m \alpha | x - v t | / \hbar^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \left[ \left(E + (1 / 2) m v ^ {2}\right) t - m v x \right] / \hbar},
$$

其中 $E = - m \alpha ^ { 2 } / ( 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } )$ 是静止 $\delta$ 函数的束缚态能.提示 将习题 2.23(b时)所得结果代入上式并验证.

(b) 求此状态下哈密顿量的期望值，并讨论所得结果.

解答 (a)

$$
\frac {\partial \varPsi}{\partial t} = \left[ - \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial t} | x - v t | - \mathrm {i} \frac {\left(E + \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2}\right)}{\hbar} \right] \varPsi ; \quad \frac {\partial}{\partial t} | x - v t | = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} - v, & x - v t > 0, \\ v, & x - v t <   0. \end{array} \right.
$$

利用阶跃函数

$$
\theta \left(z\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 1, & z > 0, \\ 0, & z <   0, \end{array} \right.
$$

得

$$
\frac {\partial | x - v t |}{\partial t} = - v [ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 ].
$$

因此

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar {\frac {\partial \varPsi}{\partial t}} = \left\{\mathrm {i} {\frac {m \alpha}{\hbar}} v [ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 ] + E + {\frac {1}{2}} m v ^ {2} \right\} \varPsi ,
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial x} = \left(- \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} | x - v t | + \frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar}\right) \Psi ;
$$

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial x} | x - v t | = \left\{ \begin{array}{c c} 1, & x - v t > 0 \\ - 1, & x - v t <   0 \end{array} \right\} 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1.
$$

所以

$$
\frac {\partial \varPsi}{\partial x} = \left\{- \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \left[ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 \right] + \frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar} \right\} \varPsi ,
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Psi}{\partial x ^ {2}} = \left\{- \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \left[ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 \right] + \frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar} \right\} ^ {2} \Psi - \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \frac {\partial \theta (x - v t)}{\partial x} \Psi \\ = \left\{- \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \left[ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 \right] + \frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar} \right\} ^ {2} \varPsi - \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \delta (x - v t) \varPsi . \\ \end{array}
$$

将上式代入薛定诗方程 $\mathrm { i } \hbar \frac { \partial \varPsi } { \partial t } = - \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m } \frac { \partial ^ { 2 } \varPsi } { \partial x ^ { 2 } } + \alpha \delta \left( x - v t \right) \varPsi ,$ 即

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Psi}{\partial x ^ {2}} + \alpha \delta (x - v t) \Psi
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left\{- \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} [ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 ] + \frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar} \right\} ^ {2} \Psi \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left\{\left(\frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} [ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 ] ^ {2} - 2 \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} [ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 ] \frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar} + \left(\frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \right\} \Psi \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left\{\left(\frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} - 2 \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} [ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 ] \frac {\mathrm {i} m v}{\hbar} - \left(\frac {m v}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \right\} \Psi \\ = \left\{- \frac {m \alpha}{2 \hbar^ {2}} + \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} + \frac {\mathrm {i} m v \alpha}{\hbar} [ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 ] \right\} \Psi \\ = \left\{- E + \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} + \frac {\mathrm {i} m v \alpha}{\hbar} \left[ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 \right] \right\} \Psi \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 检查披函数是否己归一化

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} | \Psi (x, t) | ^ {2} d x = \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} e ^ {- 2 m \alpha | x - v t | / \hbar^ {2}} d x \\ = 2 \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 m \alpha y / \hbar^ {2}} \mathrm {d} y \\ = 2 \frac {m \omega}{\hbar^ {2}} \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m \alpha} = 1. \\ \end{array}
$$

哈密顿量的期望值为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle H \rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \Psi^ {*} \hat {H} \Psi = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \Psi^ {*} \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left\{\mathrm {i} \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar} v [ 2 \theta (x - v t) - 1 ] + E + \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} \right\} | \Psi | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x \\ = 0 + E + \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} = E + \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

其中利用了 $[ 2 \theta \left( x - v t \right) - 1 ]$ 是奇函数的性质.可以看出这个被函数是以速度 $v$ 拖拽 $\vert x \vert  \vert x - v t \vert \delta$ 势束缚态披函数，所以其能量是束缚态能量 $E$ 加上动能 $m v ^ { 2 } / 2$

牢牢习题 2.51 自由下落.证明:

$$
\varPsi (x, t) = \varPsi_ {0} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}, t\right) \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {m g t}{\hbar} \left(x + \frac {1}{6} g t ^ {2}\right) \right] \tag {2.176}
$$

满足粒子在寻引|力场

$$
V (x) = m g x \tag {2.177}
$$

中的舍时定态薛定谆方程，这里 $\varPsi _ { 0 } \left( x , t \right)$ 为自由高斯波包(方程 求作为时间的函数( $\langle x \rangle$ ，并对结果进行讨论.①

证明方程 (2.111)

$$
\varPsi (x, t) = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {1}{\gamma} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x ^ {2} / \gamma^ {2}},
$$

其中 $\gamma \equiv \sqrt { 1 + 2 \mathrm { i } \hbar a t / m }$ .

对于自由落体运动，将上讲的 $_ x$ 替换为 $x + { \frac { 1 } { 2 } } g t ^ { 2 }$ 则

$$
\varPsi_ {0} = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {1}{\gamma} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} / \gamma^ {2}},
$$

$$
\varPsi (x, t) = \varPsi_ {0} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}, t\right) \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {m g t}{\hbar} \left(x + \frac {1}{6} g t ^ {2}\right) \right].
$$

含时薛定诗方程为如下形式:

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \varPsi}{\partial t} = \left(- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + V\right) \varPsi .
$$

左边对时间求微分，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t} = \left[ \frac {\partial \Psi_ {0}}{\partial t} + \Psi_ {0} \left(- \frac {\mathrm {i} m g}{\hbar}\right) \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) \right] \exp \left[ - \mathrm {i} \frac {m g t}{\hbar} \left(x + \frac {1}{6} g t ^ {2}\right) \right], \\ \frac {\partial \Psi_ {0}}{\partial t} = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \left\{- \frac {1}{\gamma^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm {d} \gamma}{\mathrm {d} t} + \frac {1}{\gamma} \left[ - \frac {2 a}{\gamma^ {2}} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) g t \right. \right. \\ \left. \left. - a \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t\right) ^ {2} \left(- \frac {2}{\gamma^ {3}} \frac {\mathrm {d} \gamma}{\mathrm {d} t}\right) \right] \right\} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} / \gamma^ {2}} \\ = \left[ - \frac {1}{\gamma} \frac {\mathrm {d} \gamma}{\mathrm {d} t} - \frac {2 a g t}{\gamma^ {2}} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) + \frac {2 a}{\gamma^ {3}} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm {d} \gamma}{\mathrm {d} t} \right] \varPsi_ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由 ${ \frac { \mathrm { d } \gamma } { \mathrm { d } t } } = { \frac { 1 } { 2 \gamma } } \left( { \frac { 2 \mathrm { i } a \hbar } { m } } \right) = { \frac { \mathrm { i } a \hbar } { \gamma m } }$ 得

$$
\frac {\partial \Psi_ {0}}{\partial t} = \left[ \frac {- \mathrm {i} a \hbar}{\gamma^ {2} m} - \frac {2 a g t}{\gamma^ {2}} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) + \frac {2 \mathrm {i} a ^ {2} \hbar}{\gamma^ {4} m} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right] \Psi_ {0},
$$

困此，

$$
\frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t} = \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i} a \hbar}{\gamma^ {2} m} - \frac {2 a g t}{\gamma^ {2}} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) + \frac {2 \mathrm {i} a ^ {2} \hbar}{\gamma^ {4} m} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m g}{\hbar} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) \right] \Psi . \qquad ①
$$

求含时薛定诗方程等号右边的部分，需要先求波函数对坐标的二阶微分.

一阶微分为

$$
\frac {\partial \varPsi}{\partial x} = \left(\frac {\partial \varPsi_ {0}}{\partial x} - \varPsi_ {0} \frac {\mathrm {i} m g t}{\hbar}\right) \exp \left[ - \frac {\mathrm {i} m g t}{\hbar} \left(x + \frac {1}{6} g t ^ {2}\right) \right],
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \Psi_ {0}}{\partial x} = - 2 a \left[ \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) \Big / \gamma^ {2} \right] \Psi_ {0},
$$

所以，

$$
\frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial x} = \left[ - 2 a \left(x + \frac {1}{2}\right) g t ^ {2} / \gamma^ {2} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m g t}{\hbar} \right] \Psi .
$$

二阶微分为

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} \Psi}{\partial x ^ {2}} = \left\{- \frac {2 a}{\gamma^ {2}} \Psi + \left[ - 2 a \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) / \gamma^ {2} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m g t}{\hbar} \right] \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial x} \right\}
$$

$$
= \left\{- \frac {2 a}{\gamma^ {2}} + \left[ - 2 a \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) \Bigg / \gamma^ {2} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m g t}{\hbar} \right] ^ {2} \right\} \Psi ,
$$

所以，舍时薛定诗方程右边为

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Psi}{\partial x ^ {2}} + V \Psi = \left\{\frac {\hbar^ {2} a}{m \gamma} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ - 2 a \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) / \gamma^ {2} - \frac {\mathrm {i} m g t}{\hbar} \right] ^ {2} + m g x \right\} \Psi .
$$

将 $\textcircled{1}$ 式乘以 $\mathrm { i } \hbar$ 可得到 $\textcircled{2}$ ，验证1 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ 满足舍时薛定诲方程.

$$
① \quad \times \mathrm {i} \hbar = \left[ \frac {a \hbar^ {2}}{\gamma^ {2} m} - \frac {2 \mathrm {i} a g t \hbar}{\gamma^ {2}} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) - \frac {2 a ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{\gamma^ {4} m} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} + m g \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) \right] \Psi .
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \textcircled {2} = \frac {\hbar^ {2} a}{m \gamma^ {2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ \frac {4 a ^ {2} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2}}{\gamma^ {4}} + \frac {4 a \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {i m} g t}{\gamma^ {2} \hbar} - \frac {(m g t) ^ {2}}{\gamma^ {2} \hbar} - \frac {(m g t) ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}} \right] \Psi \\ + m g x \Psi \\ = \left[ \frac {\hbar^ {2} a}{m \gamma^ {2}} - \frac {2 a ^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{m \gamma^ {4}} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {2 \mathrm {i} a g t \hbar}{\gamma^ {2}} \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) + \frac {1}{2} m g ^ {2} t ^ {2} + m g x \right] \varPsi . \\ \end{array}
$$

因此， $\textcircled { 1 } \times \mathrm { i } \hbar = \textcircled { 2 }$

求 $\left. { x } \right. = \int { x \left| \varPsi \left( x , t \right) \right| ^ { 2 } } \mathrm { d } x$ 首先考察| $\vert \varPsi \left( x , t \right) \vert ^ { 2 }$

$$
| \Psi (x, t) | ^ {2} = | \Psi_ {0} | ^ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {2 a}{\pi}} \frac {1}{| \gamma | ^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a (x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}) ^ {2} \left[ \frac {1}{\gamma^ {2}} + \frac {1}{(\gamma^ {*}) ^ {2}} \right]}.
$$

由 $\frac { 1 } { \gamma ^ { 2 } } + \frac { 1 } { \left( \gamma ^ { * } \right) ^ { 2 } } = \frac { \gamma ^ { 2 } + \left( \gamma ^ { * } \right) ^ { 2 } } { \left| \gamma \right| ^ { 4 } } = \frac { 2 } { \left| \gamma \right| ^ { 4 } }$ 一一一一(γ.)2 和 n'L.I  ' ' _ $y = x + { \frac { 1 } { 2 } } g t ^ { 2 }$ lgt2 _  -r.

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle = \sqrt {\frac {2 a}{\pi}} \frac {1}{| \gamma | ^ {2}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 a \left(x + \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) ^ {2} / | \gamma | ^ {4}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \sqrt {\frac {2 a}{\pi}} \frac {1}{| \gamma | ^ {2}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(y - \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 a y ^ {2} / | \gamma | ^ {4}} \mathrm {d} y \\ = \sqrt {\frac {2 a}{\pi}} \frac {1}{| \gamma | ^ {2}} \left(- \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}\right) \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 a y ^ {2} / | \gamma | ^ {4}} \mathrm {d} y \\ = - \frac {1}{2} g t ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$\langle x \rangle = - { \frac { 1 } { 2 } } g t ^ { 2 }$ jgt2 是经典力学中自由事体运动的精确解，正如我们从埃伦菲斯特定理中所预期的一样

串串串习题 2.52 考虑势能

$$
V (x) = - \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{m} \mathrm {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x),
$$

其中 $a$ 是正的常数'而 "sech" 代表双曲正割函数.

(a) 画图将该势表示出来.   
(b) 验证该势存在基态

$$
\psi_ {0} (x) = A \operatorname {s e c h} (a x),
$$

并求出基态能量.归一化 $\psi _ { 0 }$ 并画图表示.

(c) 证明:对于任何(正的)能量 $E$ 函数

$$
\psi_ {k} (x) = A \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} k - a \tanh  (a x)}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x}
$$

(一般情况下 $k \equiv { \sqrt { 2 m E } } / \hbar )$ 苟同都满足薛定谤方程由于当 $z  - \infty$ 时， $\operatorname { t a n h } z  - 1$ $\psi _ { k } \left( x \right) \approx A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } k x } , x$ 为大的负数，

所以，这表示从左边入射且没有伴生反射波的波(即不存在 $\exp \left( - \mathrm { i } k x \right)$ 项).对正的较大 $x , \psi _ { k } \left( x \right)$ 的渐近形式是什么?对于这个势 $R$ 和 $T$ 是多少?注释这是一个无反射势的著名例子 每一个入射的粒子，不论其能量大小，都能穿越势垒.①

解答 (时所给势能如圄 2.19 所示.

(b)

$$
\operatorname {s e c h} (a x) = \frac {1}{\cosh (a x)} = \frac {2}{\mathrm {e} ^ {a x} + \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x}},
$$

![](images/b92593daba19c61a7ce1e6f0d119e1d434db71418f881836341a06e3062ffcf6.jpg)  
2.19

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} \operatorname {s e c h} (a x) = \frac {- a \sinh (a x)}{\cosh^ {2} (a x)}, \\ \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} \operatorname {s e c h} (a x) = - \frac {a ^ {2}}{\cosh (a x)} + \frac {2 a ^ {2} \sinh^ {2} (a x)}{\cosh^ {3} (a x)} \\ = a ^ {2} \left[ \frac {- \cosh^ {2} (a x) + 2 \sinh^ {2} (a x)}{\cosh^ {3} (a x)} \right] \\ = a ^ {2} \left\{\frac {- \left[ 1 + \sinh^ {2} (a x) \right] + 2 \sinh^ {2} (a x)}{\cosh^ {3} (a x)} \right\} \\ = a ^ {2} \left[ - \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {3} (a x) + \operatorname {s e c h} (a x) \tanh  ^ {2} (a x) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {H} \psi_ {0} (x) = \left[ - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{m} \mathrm {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \right] A \mathrm {s e c h} (a x) \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} A a ^ {2} \left[ \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {3} (a x) - \operatorname {s e c h} (a x) \tanh  ^ {2} (a x) \right] - \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{m} \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) A \operatorname {s e c h} (a x) \\ = \left\{\frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) - \tanh  ^ {2} (a x) \right] - \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{m} \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \right\} A \operatorname {s e c h} (a x) \\ = \left\{\frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) - \tanh  ^ {2} (a x) \right] - \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{m} \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \right\} \psi_ {0} (x) \\ = \left\{- \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ \mathrm {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) + \tanh ^ {2} (a x) \right] \right\} \psi_ {0} (x) \\ = \left\{- \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ \frac {1 + \sinh^ {2} (a x)}{\cosh^ {2} (a x)} \right] \right\} \psi_ {0} (x) \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{2 m} \psi_ {0} (x). \\ \end{array}
$$

因此 $\psi _ { 0 } \left( x \right)$ 是能量本征函数，能量本征值为 $E _ { 0 } = - \hbar ^ { 2 } a ^ { 2 } / ( 2 m )$ .

归一化波函数

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \mathrm {d} x = | A | ^ {2} \frac {1}{a} \tanh  (a x) | _ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \\ = | A | ^ {2} \frac {2}{a} \rightarrow A = \sqrt {\frac {a}{2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

波函数的图形如圄 2.20 所示.

(c)

$$
\psi_ {k} (x) = A \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} k - a \tanh  (a x)}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d} \psi_ {k} (x)}{\mathrm {d} x} = A \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} k - a \tanh  (a x)}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \right] \mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - A \frac {a ^ {2}}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \frac {1}{\cosh^ {2} (a x)} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \\ = \frac {A}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \left\{\left[ \mathrm {i} k - a \tanh  (a x) \right] \mathrm {i} k - a ^ {2} \sec h ^ {2} (a x) \right\} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {k} (x)}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} = A \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i} k - a \tanh (a x)}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \right] \mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} - A \frac {a ^ {2}}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \frac {1}{\cosh^ {2} (a x)} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \\ = \frac {A}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \left\{\left[ \mathrm {i} k - a \tanh  (a x) \right] \mathrm {i} k - a ^ {2} \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \right\} \mathrm {i} k \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \\ + \frac {A}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \left[ - \mathrm {i} k a ^ {2} \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) + 2 a ^ {3} \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \tanh  (a x) \right] \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \\ = \frac {A}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \left\{- k ^ {2} \left[ \mathrm {i} k - a \tanh (a x) \right] - 2 a ^ {2} \mathrm {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \left[ \mathrm {i} k - a \tanh (a x) \right] \right\} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} \\ = - \left[ k ^ {2} + 2 a ^ {2} \operatorname {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \right] \psi_ {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {H} \psi_ {k} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \psi_ {k}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + V (x) \psi_ {k} \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left[ k ^ {2} + 2 a ^ {2} \mathrm {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \right] \psi_ {k} - \frac {\hbar^ {2} a ^ {2}}{m} \mathrm {s e c h} ^ {2} (a x) \psi_ {k} \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2} k ^ {2}}{2 m} \psi_ {k} \\ = E _ {k} \psi_ {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以 $\psi _ { k }$ 是哈密顿能量本征值为 $E _ { k }$ 的本征态(散射态，连续谱).

当 $x \to + \infty$ 时， $\operatorname { t a n h } { ( a x ) }  1$ ，势阱右侧的透射波为

$$
\psi_ {k} (x) \underset {x \rightarrow \infty} {\longrightarrow} A \frac {\mathrm {i} k - a}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} = F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x},
$$

透射系数为

$$
T = \left| \frac {F}{A} \right| ^ {2} = \left| \frac {\mathrm {i} k - a}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \right| ^ {2} = 1,
$$

所以反射系数为零.另外

$$
\psi_ {k} (x) \underset {x \rightarrow - \infty} {\longrightarrow} A \frac {\mathrm {i} k + a}{\mathrm {i} k + a} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x},
$$

只有入射披.

![](images/99834007dc3909e7dee374b74ef0ee2fa9d3fb6c09b59b56627a6e5860c73307.jpg)  
2.20

习题 2.53 散射矩阵.散射理论能够以显而易见的方式推广到任意的定域势(图2.21). 在左边(区域 1) $V \left( x \right) = 0$ ，所以

$$
\psi (x) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}, \text {其 中} k \equiv \frac {\sqrt {2 m E}}{\hbar}. \tag {2.178}
$$

在右边(区域 m) $V \left( x \right)$ 同样为 ，所以

$$
\psi (x) = F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + G \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}. \tag {2.179}
$$

在中间(区域町，当然，在指定具体势之前无法告诉你 $\psi$ 是什么，但是由于薛定诗方程是一个线性二阶微分方程，通解一定具有如下形式:

$$
\psi (x) = C f (x) + D g (x),
$$

其中 $f \left( x \right)$ 和 $g \left( x \right)$ 是两个线性独立的特解.①存在 个边界条件(区域 和区域交汇处两个，区域 和区域皿交汇处两个).利用其中的两个方程可以消去 $C$ 和$D$ 其他两个可以用来求出 $B$ 和 $F .$ 并用 $A$ 和 $G$ 表示:

$$
B = S _ {1 1} A + S _ {1 2} G, \quad F = S _ {2 1} A + S _ {2 2} G.
$$

四个依赖于 $k$ (从而也依赖于 $E$ 的系数 $S _ { i j }$ 组成一个 $2 \times 2$ 矩阵 $s$ ，称为散射矩阵(简写为$ $s .$ 矩阵). $s .$ -矩阵告诉你如何以入射振幅 $A$ 和 $G$ 来表示出射振幅 $B$ 和 $F$ :

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c} B \\ F \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} S _ {1 1} & S _ {1 2} \\ S _ {2 1} & S _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ G \end{array} \right) \tag {2.180}
$$

从左边散射的特定情况下 $G = 0$ 因此，反射系数和透射系数为

$$
R _ {l} = \left. \frac {\left| B \right| ^ {2}}{\left| A \right| ^ {2}} \right| _ {G = 0} = \left| S _ {1 1} \right| ^ {2}, \quad T _ {l} = \left. \frac {\left| F \right| ^ {2}}{\left| A \right| ^ {2}} \right| _ {G = 0} = \left| S _ {2 1} \right| ^ {2}. \tag {2.181}
$$

从右边散射的情况 $A = 0$ ，有

$$
R _ {r} = \left. \frac {\left| F \right| ^ {2}}{\left| G \right| ^ {2}} \right| _ {A = 0} = \left| S _ {2 2} \right| ^ {2}, \quad T _ {r} = \left. \frac {\left| B \right| ^ {2}}{\left| G \right| ^ {2}} \right| _ {A = 0} = \left| S _ {1 2} \right| ^ {2}. \tag {2.182}
$$

![](images/6a5763f46709dbd6a9fdc99cd181bc22a48aa014a8cb1e7d712271e5b4610970.jpg)  
2.21 任意局域势的散射(除了区域 $V \left( x \right) = 0$ )

(a) 构造由一个 $\delta$ 函数势阱(方程 (2.117)) 散射的 $s .$ 矩阵;  
(b) 构造有限深方势阱(方程 (2.148)) 的 $s .$ -矩阵.提示 如果你仔细分析题目的对称性，无需重新计算.

解答 (a) $\delta$ 势阱 $E > 0$ 散射态的解为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}, & x <   0, \\ F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + G \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}, & x \geqslant 0. \end{array} \right.
$$

在 $x = 0$ 处披函数连续及波函数导数跃变给出

$$
F + G = A + B,
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} k (F - G - A + B) = - \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} (A + B).
$$

或者令 $\beta \equiv m \alpha / \hbar ^ { 2 } k$

$$
F - G = A (1 + 2 \mathrm {i} \beta) - B (1 - 2 \mathrm {i} \beta).
$$

消去 $F .$ ，得

$$
B = \frac {1}{1 - \mathrm {i} \beta} (\mathrm {i} \beta A + G).
$$

消去 $B$ ，得

$$
F = \frac {1}{1 - \mathrm {i} \beta} (A + \mathrm {i} \beta G).
$$

用矩阵表示上述关系

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c} B \\ F \end{array} \right) = \frac {1}{1 - \mathrm {i} \beta} \left( \begin{array}{c c} \mathrm {i} \beta & 1 \\ 1 & \mathrm {i} \beta \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ G \end{array} \right).
$$

显然散射矩阵为

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} = \frac {1}{1 - \mathrm {i} \beta} \left( \begin{array}{c c} \mathrm {i} \beta & 1 \\ 1 & \mathrm {i} \beta \end{array} \right).
$$

(b) $V \left( x \right) = V \left( - x \right)$ 偶函数势，从右边入射与从左边入射结果是一样的， 做变换 $x  - x , \mathrm { A } $ $G , B  F , \pmb { \mathscr { s } } .$ 矩阵不变，所以同时有

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c} B \\ F \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} S _ {1 1} & S _ {1 2} \\ S _ {2 1} & S _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ G \end{array} \right),
$$

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c} F \\ B \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} S _ {1 1} & S _ {1 2} \\ S _ {2 1} & S _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} G \\ A \end{array} \right).
$$

故

$$
S _ {1 1} = S _ {2 2}, \quad S _ {1 2} = S _ {2 1}.
$$

(注意 (a) 中的 $\delta$ 也是偶函数势，所以也有这种性质. )

对有限深方势阱 $E > 0$ 散射态，解为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}, & x <   - a, \\ C \sin \left(l x\right) + D \cos \left(l x\right), & - a \leqslant x \leqslant a, \\ F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x}, & a <   x, \end{array} \right.
$$

其中

$$
k = \frac {\sqrt {2 m E}}{\hbar}, \quad l \equiv \frac {\sqrt {2 m (E + V _ {0})}}{\hbar}.
$$

在 $x = \pm a$ 处波函数及其导数连续，有

$$
A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} = - C \sin (l a) + D \cos (l a),
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {i} k \left(A \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} - B \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}\right) = l \left[ C \cos (l a) + D \sin (l a) \right], \\ C \sin (l a) + D \cos (l a) = F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}, \\ l \left[ C \cos (l a) - D \sin (l a) \right] = \mathrm {i} k F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由此解出

$$
\begin{array}{l} F = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a} A}{\cos (2 l a) - \mathrm {i} \frac {(k ^ {2} - l ^ {2})}{2 k l} \sin (2 l a)}, \\ B = \mathrm {i} \frac {\sin (2 l a)}{2 k l} \left(l ^ {2} - k ^ {2}\right) F = \mathrm {i} \frac {\sin (2 l a)}{2 k l} \left(l ^ {2} - k ^ {2}\right) \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 i k a} A}{\cos (2 l a) - \mathrm {i} \frac {(k ^ {2} - l ^ {2})}{2 k l} \sin (2 l a)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
S _ {2 1} = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a}}{\cos (2 l a) - \mathrm {i} \frac {(k ^ {2} - l ^ {2})}{2 k l} \sin (2 l a)} = S _ {1 2},
$$

$$
S _ {2 2} = \frac {\mathrm {i} \sin (2 l a) \left(l ^ {2} - k ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} k a}}{2 k l \cos (2 l a) - \mathrm {i} \left(k ^ {2} - l ^ {2}\right) \sin (2 l a)} = S _ {1 1}.
$$

串串串习题 2.54 传递矩阵.① $s .$ 矩阵(习题 2.53) 给出了出射报幅 $B$ 和 $F$ ) 与入射振幅 (A $G$ 的关系一一方程 (2.180). 为了某些目的利用传递矩阵 $\mathbf { \mathcal { M } }$ 是很方便它可以给出势能右侧波的振幅 $F$ 和 $G$ 同左侧波的振幅 $A$ 和 $B$ 之间的关系:

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c} F \\ G \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} M _ {1 1} & M _ {1 2} \\ M _ {2 1} & M _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ B \end{array} \right). \tag {2.183}
$$

(a) $s .$ -矩阵的矩阵元求出 $\pmb { \mathscr { M } }$ 矩阵的四个矩阵元，反之亦然.用 $\mathbf { \mathcal { M } } \mathbf { \Theta }$ 矩阵的矩阵元表示出岛， $R _ { l } , T _ { l } , R _ { r }$ 和 $T _ { r }$ 方程 (2.181) (2.182)).  
(b) 假设有一个势是由两个孤立部分组成的(图 2.22). 证明:该势的 M-矩阵是两个孤立部分势的 $\mathbf { \mathcal { M } } \mathbf { \mathcal { \cdot } }$ 矩阵的乘积:

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} = \boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} _ {2} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} _ {1}. \tag {2.184}
$$

(显然这个结论可以推广到含有任意多个部分势的情况，这说明了 M-矩阵的有用性.)

(c) 对于在点 $a$ 处的 $\delta$ 函数散射势，构造 M-矩阵:

$$
V (x) = - \alpha \delta (x - a).
$$

(d) 利用 (b) 中的方法，求双 $\delta$ 函数散射的儿1-矩阵

$$
V (x) = - \alpha [ \delta (x + a) + \delta (x - a) ].
$$

该势的透射系数是什么?

![](images/bf9b835825ce41b1c02f86a704d290ccf7277da52e4382601b5cbc13c9c3cec6.jpg)  
含有两个孤立部分的势

解答 (a) 由散射矩阵

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c} B \\ F \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} S _ {1 1} & S _ {1 2} \\ S _ {2 1} & S _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ G \end{array} \right),
$$

得

$$
\begin{array}{l} B = S _ {1 1} A + S _ {1 2} G \rightarrow G = \frac {1}{S _ {1 2}} (B - S _ {1 1} A) = M _ {2 1} A + M _ {2 2} B \\ \rightarrow M _ {2 2} = \frac {1}{S _ {1 2}}, \quad M _ {2 1} = - \frac {S _ {1 1}}{S _ {1 2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} F = S _ {2 1} A + S _ {2 2} G = S _ {2 1} A + \frac {S _ {2 2}}{S _ {1 2}} (B - S _ {1 1} A) \\ = \frac {1}{S _ {1 2}} \left(S _ {1 2} S _ {2 1} - S _ {1 1} S _ {2 2}\right) A + \frac {S _ {2 2}}{S _ {1 2}} B = M _ {1 1} A + M _ {1 2} B \\ \rightarrow M _ {1 1} = \frac {1}{S _ {1 2}} \left(S _ {1 2} S _ {2 1} - S _ {1 1} S _ {2 2}\right) = \frac {- \det  \boldsymbol {S}}{S _ {1 2}}, \quad M _ {1 2} = \frac {S _ {2 2}}{S _ {1 2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} = \frac {1}{S _ {1 2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c} - \det  \boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} & S _ {2 2} \\ S _ {2 1} & 1 \end{array} \right).
$$

再由

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c} F \\ G \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c c} M _ {1 1} & M _ {1 2} \\ M _ {2 1} & M _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ B \end{array} \right),
$$

得

$$
G = M _ {2 1} A + M _ {2 2} B,   \text {得}   B = {\frac {1}{M _ {2 2}}} \left(G - M _ {2 1} A\right) = S _ {1 1} A + S _ {1 2} G,
$$

进一步可得

$$
S _ {1 1} = - \frac {M _ {2 1}}{M _ {2 2}}, \quad S _ {1 2} = \frac {1}{M _ {2 2}}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} F = M _ {1 1} A + M _ {1 2} B = M _ {1 1} A + \frac {M _ {1 2}}{M _ {2 2}} (G - M _ {2 1} A) \\ = \frac {1}{M _ {2 2}} \left(M _ {1 1} M _ {2 2} - M _ {1 2} M _ {2 1}\right) A + \frac {M _ {1 2}}{M _ {2 2}} G = S _ {2 1} A + S _ {2 2} G, \\ \end{array}
$$

得

$$
S _ {2 1} = \frac {1}{M _ {2 2}} \left(M _ {1 1} M _ {2 2} - M _ {1 2} M _ {2 1}\right) = \frac {\det  \mathcal {M}}{M _ {2 2}}, \quad S _ {2 2} = \frac {M _ {1 2}}{M _ {2 2}}.
$$

所以

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {S}} = \frac {1}{M _ {2 2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c} - M _ {2 1} & 1 \\ \det  \boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} & M _ {1 2} \end{array} \right).
$$

(注薛定诗方程的时间反转不变性加上几率守恒要求 $M _ { 2 2 } = M _ { 1 1 } ^ { * } , M _ { 1 2 } = M _ { 2 1 } ^ { * } , \operatorname* { d e t } \mathcal { M } =$ $\operatorname* { d e t } \pmb { \mathcal { M } } = 1$ ，对偶函数势有 $S _ { 1 1 } = S _ { 2 2 } , S _ { 1 2 } = S _ { 2 1 } .$ ,)

在从左边入射 $G = 0$ 情况下，

$$
R _ {l} = \left| \frac {B}{A} \right| ^ {2} = | S _ {1 1} | ^ {2} = \left| \frac {M _ {2 1}}{M _ {2 2}} \right| ^ {2},
$$

$$
T _ {l} = \left| \frac {F}{A} \right| ^ {2} = | S _ {2 1} | ^ {2} = \left| \frac {\det  \boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}}}{M _ {2 2}} \right| ^ {2}.
$$

在从右边入射 $A = 0$ 情况下，

$$
R _ {r} = \left| \frac {F}{G} \right| ^ {2} = | S _ {2 2} | ^ {2} = \left| \frac {M _ {1 2}}{M _ {2 2}} \right| ^ {2},
$$

$$
T _ {r} = \left| \frac {B}{G} \right| ^ {2} = | S _ {1 2} | ^ {2} = \left| \frac {1}{M _ {2 2}} \right| ^ {2}.
$$

(b) 假设势是由两个孤立部分组成，如图 2.23 所示.

![](images/10f8d315312ace32ecbf75b3e5ad2f1dd3d63926bd44e0d57e0ced39f02dfb54.jpg)  
2.23

则有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left( \begin{array}{c} F \\ G \end{array} \right) = \mathcal {M} _ {2} \left( \begin{array}{c} C \\ D \end{array} \right), \quad \left( \begin{array}{c} C \\ D \end{array} \right) = \mathcal {M} _ {1} \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ B \end{array} \right) \\ \Rightarrow \left( \begin{array}{c} F \\ G \end{array} \right) = \mathcal {M} _ {2} \mathcal {M} _ {1} \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ B \end{array} \right) = \mathcal {M} \left( \begin{array}{c} A \\ B \end{array} \right), \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\mathcal {M} = \mathcal {M} _ {2} \mathcal {M} _ {1}.
$$

对于在点 $a$ 处的一个 $\delta$ 函数散射势能.

$$
V (x) = - \alpha \delta (x - a),
$$

定态薛定诲方程的解为

$$
\psi \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}, & x <   a, \\ F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k x} + G \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k x}, & a <   x. \end{array} \right.
$$

其中 $k \equiv \frac { \sqrt { 2 m E } } { \hbar }$

由在 $x = a$ 处波函数连续及其导数跃变有

$$
A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a} = F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} + G \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a},
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} k \left(F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} - G \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a}\right) - \mathrm {i} k \left(A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} - B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a}\right) = - \frac {2 m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \left(A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} k a} + B \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} k a}\right).
$$

整理，得

$$
F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a} + G = A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a} + B,
$$

$$
F \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a} - G = (1 + \mathrm {i} 2 \beta) A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a} - (1 - \mathrm {i} 2 \beta) B, \quad \beta \equiv \frac {m \alpha}{k \hbar^ {2}}.
$$

两式相加，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} F = (1 + \mathrm {i} \beta) A + \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 k a} B = M _ {1 1} A + M _ {1 2} B \\ \rightarrow M _ {1 1} = 1 + \mathrm {i} \beta , \quad M _ {1 2} = \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 k a}. \\ \end{array}
$$

两式相捕，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} G = - \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a} A + (1 - \mathrm {i} \beta) B = M _ {2 1} A + M _ {2 2} B \\ \rightarrow M _ {2 1} = - \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a}, \quad M _ {2 2} = 1 - \mathrm {i} \beta . \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\mathcal {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 + \mathrm {i} \beta & \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 k a} \\ - \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a} & 1 - \mathrm {i} \beta \end{array} \right).
$$

(d) 对双 $\delta$ 势

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}} _ {2} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 + \mathrm {i} \beta & \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 k a} \\ - \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a} & 1 - \mathrm {i} \beta \end{array} \right).
$$

在 $\mathbf { \mathcal { M } } _ { 2 }$ 中做变换 $a  - a$ ，得

$$
\mathcal {M} _ {1} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 + \mathrm {i} \beta & \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 k a} \\ - \mathrm {i} \beta \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 k a} & 1 - \mathrm {i} \beta \end{array} \right).
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {M} = \mathcal {M} _ {2} \mathcal {M} _ {1} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 + 2 \mathrm {i} \beta + \beta^ {2} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 4 k a} - 1\right) & 2 \mathrm {i} \beta \left[ \cos (2 k a) - \beta \sin (2 k a) \right] \\ - 2 \mathrm {i} \beta \left[ \cos (2 k a) - \beta \sin (2 k a) \right] & 1 - 2 \mathrm {i} \beta + \beta^ {2} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 4 k a} - 1\right) \end{array} \right). \\ T = \left\{ \begin{array}{c} T _ {l} = \left| \frac {F}{A} \right| _ {G = 0} ^ {2} = | S _ {2 1} | ^ {2} = \left| \frac {\operatorname* {d e t} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {M}}}{M _ {2 2}} \right| ^ {2} = \left| \frac {1}{M _ {2 2}} \right| ^ {2} \\ T _ {r} = \left| \frac {B}{G} \right| _ {A = 0} ^ {2} = | S _ {1 2} | ^ {2} = \left| \frac {1}{M _ {2 2}} \right| ^ {2} \end{array} \right\} = \left| \frac {1}{M _ {2 2}} \right| ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} T ^ {- 1} = \left| M _ {2 2} \right| ^ {2} = \left[ 1 + 2 \mathrm {i} \beta + \beta^ {2} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 4 k a} - 1\right) \right] \left[ 1 - 2 \mathrm {i} \beta + \beta^ {2} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 4 k a} - 1\right) \right] \\ = (1 + 2 \mathrm {i} \beta) (1 - 2 \mathrm {i} \beta) + (1 + 2 \mathrm {i} \beta) \beta^ {2} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 4 k a} - 1\right) \\ + (1 - 2 \mathrm {i} \beta) \beta^ {2} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 4 k a} - 1\right) + \beta^ {4} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 4 k a} - 1\right) \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 4 k a} - 1\right) \\ = 1 + 2 \beta^ {2} [ 1 + \cos (4 k a) ] + 4 \beta^ {3} \sin (4 k a) + 2 \beta^ {4} [ 1 - \cos (4 k a) ] \\ = 1 + 4 \beta^ {2} [ \cos (2 k a) + \beta \sin (2 k a) ] ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

已--ó 习题 2.57 利用"摇摆狗尾"方法求出无限深方势阱的前四个能级(保留五位有效数字).提示 参考习题 2.55，对微分方程做适当改变.本题需要寻找的条件是 $u \left( 1 \right) = 0$

解答定态薛定 号方程为

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \psi^ {\prime \prime} = E \psi ,
$$

其本征值为己知的

$$
E _ {n} = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}.
$$

令 $a = 1$ 方程可写成

$$
\psi^ {\prime \prime} + (n \pi) ^ {2} \psi = 0.
$$

对于基态 $n = 1$ ，用 替换 $\pi ^ { 2 }$ 对方程的解进行猜测(同样地，将 36, 81 144 分别代入上式，对基态、第 激发态、第 激发态和第三激发态进行猜测求解)然后微调参数使波函数的右端穿过横坐标 $x = 1$ 处.结果保留五位有效数字: 9.8696, 39.478, 88.826, 157.91. (真实能量值为这些数值乘以忐)图 叫图 叫屈情况下的波函数和波函数与横坐标 x=l 附近的交点毗图 2.39 $\frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m a ^ { 2 } }$ $x = 1$ 和图 .4 为第一激发态的情况，图 .41 和图 .4 为第二激发态的情况，图 .4 和图 .4 为第三激发态的情况.
