\frac {1}{1 6} \left(\frac {1}{3} - \frac {5}{4 \pi^ {2}}\right) \geqslant \left(\frac {8}{9 \pi^ {2}}\right) \sin^ {2} (\omega t) + \left(\frac {8}{9 \pi^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \cos^ {2} (\omega t) = \left(\frac {8}{9 \pi^ {2}}\right) ^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{3} - \frac {5}{4 \pi^ {2}} \geqslant \left(\frac {3 2}{9 \pi^ {2}}\right) ^ {2}.
$$

估算一下两边大小

$$
\frac {1}{3} - \frac {5}{4 \pi^ {2}} = 0. 2 0 6 6 8; \quad \left(\frac {3 2}{9 \pi^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} = 0. 1 2 9 7 8.
$$

显然满足能量-时间不确定性原理.

习题 3.21 对习题 .4 中的自由粒子波包和可观测量 $x$ 通过精确计算 $\sigma _ { H } , \sigma _ { x }$ 和$\textrm { d } \langle x \rangle / \mathrm { d } t$ 来验证能量一时间不确定性原理.

解答对题目给的自由粒子披包，由习题 .4

$$
\langle x \rangle = \frac {\hbar l}{m} t, \quad \frac {\mathrm {d} \langle x \rangle}{\mathrm {d} t} = \frac {\hbar l}{m},
$$

$$
\sigma_ {x} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{4 \omega^ {2}} = \frac {1 + \theta^ {2}}{4 a}, \theta \equiv \frac {2 \hbar a t}{m},
$$

$$
\left\langle H \right\rangle = \frac {1}{2 m} \left\langle p ^ {2} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{2 m} \hbar^ {2} \left(a + l ^ {2}\right).
$$

$H = { \frac { p ^ { 2 } } { 2 m } }$ p²为了得到 $\sigma _ { H }$ 需要计算 $\left. H ^ { 2 } \right.$ 对自由粒子， 所以

$$
\left\langle H ^ {2} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{4 m ^ {2}} \left\langle p ^ {4} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{4 m ^ {2}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p ^ {4} | \varPhi (p, t) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} p,
$$

其中

$$
\varPhi (p, t) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p / \hbar} \varPsi (x, t) \mathrm {d} x,
$$

由习题 .4

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPsi (x, t) = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} \theta}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \frac {l ^ {2}}{4 a}} \mathrm {e} ^ {a \left(\mathrm {i} x + \frac {l}{2 a}\right) ^ {2} / (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta)} \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} \theta}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {p l / (2 a \hbar)} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p y / \hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a y ^ {2} / (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta)} \mathrm {d} y \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} \theta}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {p l / (2 a \hbar)} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p y / \hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a y ^ {2} / (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta)} \mathrm {d} y, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPhi \left(p, t\right) = {\frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}}} \left({\frac {2 a}{\pi}}\right) ^ {1 / 4} {\frac {1}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} \theta}}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {a \left(\mathrm {i} x + {\frac {l}{2 a}}\right) ^ {2} / (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta)} \mathrm {d} x \left(\mathrm {令} y \equiv x - {\frac {\mathrm {i} l}{2 a}}\right) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} \theta}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {p l / (2 a \hbar)} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p y / \hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a y ^ {2} / (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta)} \mathrm {d} y \text {(积 分 见 习 题 2 . 2 2)} \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 + \mathrm {i} \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {p l / (2 a \hbar)} \sqrt {\frac {\pi (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta)}{a}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- p ^ {2} (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta) / 4 a \hbar^ {2}} \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\hbar}} \left(\frac {1}{2 a \pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (4 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {p l / (2 a \hbar)} \mathrm {e} ^ {- p ^ {2} (1 + \mathrm {i} \theta) / (4 a \hbar^ {2})}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \varPhi (p, t) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 a \pi}} \frac {1}{\hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- l ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {e} ^ {p l / (a \hbar)} \mathrm {e} ^ {- p ^ {2} / (2 a \hbar^ {2})} \\ = \frac {1}{\hbar \sqrt {2 a \pi}} \mathrm {e} ^ {1 / (2 a) \left(l ^ {2} - 2 p l / \hbar + p ^ {2} / \hbar^ {2}\right)} \\ = \frac {1}{\hbar \sqrt {2 a \pi}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (l - p / \hbar) ^ {2} / (2 a)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\left\langle p ^ {4} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{\hbar \sqrt {2 a \pi}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p ^ {4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (l - p / \hbar) ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {d} p \left(\text {令} \frac {p}{\hbar} - l \equiv z, \mathrm {d} p = \hbar \mathrm {d} z\right)
$$

$$
= \frac {1}{\hbar \sqrt {2 a \pi}} \hbar^ {5} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} (z + l) ^ {4} \mathrm {e} ^ {- z ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {d} z
$$

$$
= \frac {\hbar^ {4}}{\sqrt {2 a \pi}} \left[ \frac {3 (2 a) ^ {2}}{4} \sqrt {2 a \pi} + 6 l ^ {2} \frac {2 a}{2} \sqrt {2 a \pi} + l ^ {4} \sqrt {2 a \pi} \right]
$$

$$
= \frac {\hbar^ {4}}{4 m ^ {2}} \left(3 a ^ {2} + 6 a l ^ {2} + l ^ {4}\right).
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma_ {H} ^ {2} = \left\langle H ^ {2} \right\rangle - \left\langle H \right\rangle^ {2} \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {4}}{4 m ^ {2}} \left(3 a ^ {2} + 6 a l ^ {2} + l ^ {4} - a ^ {2} - 2 a l ^ {2} - l ^ {4}\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {4} a}{2 m ^ {2}} \left(a + 2 l ^ {2}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma_ {H} ^ {2} \sigma_ {x} ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar^ {4} a}{2 m ^ {2}} (a + 2 l ^ {2}) \frac {1}{4 a} \left[ 1 + \left(\frac {2 \hbar a t}{m}\right) ^ {2} \right] \\ \geqslant \frac {\hbar^ {4} l ^ {2}}{4 m ^ {2}} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{4} \left(\frac {\hbar l}{m}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{4} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} \langle x \rangle}{\mathrm {d} t}\right) ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以题目给的自由粒子被包满足能量-时间不确定性原理.

习题 3.22 证明:当所涉及的可观测量为 $x$ 时，能量-时间不确定性原理还原为不确定性原理(习题 3.15).

证明 $Q = x$ 时，能量-时间不确定性原理为 $\sigma _ { H } \sigma _ { x } \geqslant \frac { \hbar } { 2 } \left| \frac { \mathrm { d } \left. x \right. } { \mathrm { d } t } \right|$ JlctJ3.. ，但是( $\langle p \rangle = m { \frac { \mathrm { d } \left. { x } \right. } { \mathrm { d } t } }$ d(x}

$$
\sigma_ {x} \sigma_ {H} \geqslant \frac {\hbar}{2 m} | \langle p \rangle |.
$$

再由

$$
[ x, H ] = \frac {1}{2 m} [ x, p ^ {2} ] = \frac {1}{2 m} (p [ x, p ] + [ x, p ] p) = \frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{m} p
$$

得到不确定性原理，

$$
\sigma_ {x} \sigma_ {H} \geqslant \left| \frac {1}{2 \mathrm {i}} \langle [ x, H ] \rangle \right|.
$$

习题 3.23 证明:投影算符霉事，即 $\hat { P } ^ { 2 } = \hat { P }$ 求 $\hat { P }$ 的本征值，并描述其本征矢量

证明 设 $| \beta \rangle$ 为任意态矢量，投影算符 $\hat { P } = \left| \alpha \right. \left. \alpha \right|$ ，故

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {P} ^ {2} | \beta \rangle = \hat {P} (\hat {P} | \beta \rangle) = \hat {P} (| \alpha \rangle \langle \alpha | \beta \rangle) \\ = | \alpha \rangle \langle \alpha | \alpha \rangle \langle \alpha | \beta \rangle = | \alpha \rangle \langle \alpha | \beta \rangle \\ = \hat {P} | \beta \rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\hat {P} ^ {2} = \hat {P}.
$$

如果 $| \gamma \rangle$ ; $\hat { P }$ 户的本征值 $\lambda$ 的本征矢量，那么

$$
\hat {P} ^ {2} | \gamma \rangle = \lambda \hat {P} | \gamma \rangle = \lambda^ {2} | \gamma \rangle ,
$$

所以 $\lambda ^ { 2 } = \lambda$ 因此 $\hat { P }$ 的本征值是 ，任何一个含有态 $| \alpha \rangle$ 的矢量是 $\hat { P }$ 的本征值为 的本征矢，任何与 $| \alpha \rangle$ 正变的本征矢是 $\hat { P }$ 的本征值为 的本征矢.

习题 3.24 证明:如果算符 $\hat { Q }$ 是厄米的，则在任意正交基内所表示的矩阵元满足$Q _ { m n } = Q _ { n m } ^ { * }$ 也就是说，它相应的矩阵等于其转置共辄.

证明 设有正变基函数 $| m \rangle$ 和 $| n \rangle$ ，从厄米算符定义出发

$$
Q _ {m n} = \langle m | Q n \rangle = \langle Q m \mid n \rangle = \langle n \mid Q m \rangle^ {*} = Q _ {n m} ^ {*}.
$$

习题 3.25 两能级体系的哈密顿量为

$$
\hat {H} = \varepsilon (| 1 \rangle \langle 1 | - | 2 \rangle \langle 2 | + | 1 \rangle \langle 2 | + | 2 \rangle \langle 1 |),
$$

其中 $| 1 \rangle , | 2 \rangle$ 是正交归一基， $\varepsilon$ 为一个具有能量量纲的数值.求出其本征值和本征矢(用 $| 1 \rangle$ 和 $| 2 \rangle$ 的线性叠加表示).用这组基来表 $\hat { H }$ 的矩阵 $\varkappa$ 是什么?

解答相应于这个基表示 $\hat { H }$ 的矩阵 $\mathcal { H }$ 的矩阵元是

$$
\langle 1 | \hat {H} | 1 \rangle = \varepsilon , \quad \langle 1 | \hat {H} | 2 \rangle = \varepsilon , \quad \langle 2 | \hat {H} | 1 \rangle = \varepsilon , \quad \langle 2 | \hat {H} | 2 \rangle = - \varepsilon ,
$$

$$
\mathcal {H} = \varepsilon \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ 1 & - 1 \end{array} \right).
$$

本征方程为

$$
\varepsilon \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ 1 & - 1 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \end{array} \right) = E \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \end{array} \right).
$$

久期方程为

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} \varepsilon - E & \varepsilon \\ \varepsilon & - \varepsilon - E \end{array} \right| = 0,
$$

$$
\left(E ^ {2} - \varepsilon^ {2}\right) - \varepsilon^ {2} = 0,
$$

$$
E _ {\pm} = \pm \sqrt {2} \varepsilon .
$$

把 $E _ { + } = \sqrt { 2 } \varepsilon$ 代入本征方程，得

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ 1 & - 1 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \end{array} \right) = \sqrt {2} \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \end{array} \right),
$$

$$
c _ {1} + c _ {2} = \sqrt {2} c _ {1},
$$

$$
c _ {2} = \left(\sqrt {2} - 1\right) c _ {1},
$$

归一化

$$
\left| c _ {1} \right| ^ {2} + \left| c _ {2} \right| ^ {2} = 1,
$$

$$
\left| c _ {1} \right| ^ {2} \left[ 1 + \left(\sqrt {2} - 1\right) ^ {2} \right] = 1,
$$

$$
c _ {1} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {4 - 2 \sqrt {2}}}.
$$

得到 $c _ { 1 }$ 时不计任意相困子，所以对应 $E _ { + } = \sqrt { 2 } \varepsilon$ 的本征态为

$$
\left| \psi_ {+} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {4 - 2 \sqrt {2}}} \left[ \left| 1 \right\rangle + \left(\sqrt {2} - 1\right) \left| 2 \right\rangle \right].
$$

同理把 $E _ { - } = - \sqrt { 2 } \varepsilon$ 代入本征方程，得

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ 1 & - 1 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \end{array} \right) = - \sqrt {2} \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \end{array} \right),
$$

$$
c _ {1} + c _ {2} = - \sqrt {2} c _ {1},
$$

$$
c _ {2} = - \left(\sqrt {2} + 1\right) c _ {1},
$$

归一化

$$
\left| c _ {1} \right| ^ {2} + \left| c _ {2} \right| ^ {2} = 1,
$$

$$
\left| c _ {1} \right| ^ {2} \left[ 1 + (\sqrt {2} + 1) ^ {2} \right] = 1,
$$

$$
c _ {1} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {4 + 2 \sqrt {2}}}.
$$

所以对应 $E _ { - } = - \sqrt { 2 } \varepsilon$ 的本征态为

$$
\left| \psi_ {-} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {4 + 2 \sqrt {2}}} \left[ \left| 1 \right\rangle - \left(\sqrt {2} + 1\right) \left| 2 \right\rangle \right].
$$

事习题 3.26 考虑由正交归一基 $\left| 1 \right. , \left| 2 \right. , \left| 3 \right.$ 构成的三维矢量空间.右矢 $| \alpha \rangle$ 和 $| \beta \rangle$ 分别为

$$
| \alpha \rangle = \mathrm {i} | 1 \rangle - 2 | 2 \rangle - \mathrm {i} | 3 \rangle , \quad | \beta \rangle = \mathrm {i} | 1 \rangle + 2 | 3 \rangle .
$$

(a) 构造出 $\langle \alpha \vert$ 和 $\langle \beta \vert$ (以对偶基 $\left. 1 \right| , \left. 2 \right| , \left. 3 \right|$ 来表示).  
(b) 求出 $\langle \alpha \vert \beta \rangle$ 和 $\langle \beta | \alpha \rangle$ ，并证实 $ \beta | \alpha  =  \alpha | \beta  ^ { * }$ .  
在这组基中，求出算符 $\hat { A } \equiv \left| \alpha \right. \left. \beta \right|$ 的所有 个矩阵元，并写出矩阵 $\pmb { A }$ 它是厄米矩阵吗?

解答 (a) 构造左矢

$$
\langle \alpha | = - \mathrm {i} \langle 1 | - 2 \langle 2 | + \mathrm {i} \langle 3 |; \quad \langle \beta | = - \mathrm {i} \langle 1 | + 2 \langle 3 |.
$$

(b) 作内积

$$
\langle \alpha | \beta \rangle = (- i \langle 1 | - 2 \langle 2 | + i \langle 3 |) (i | 1 \rangle + 2 | 3 \rangle) = 1 + 2 i,
$$

$$
\langle \beta | \alpha \rangle = (- \mathrm {i} \langle 1 | + 2 \langle 3 |) (\mathrm {i} | 1 \rangle - 2 | 2 \rangle - \mathrm {i} | 3 \rangle) = 1 - 2 \mathrm {i} = \langle \alpha | \beta \rangle^ {*}.
$$

写出矩阵元和矩阵

$$
A _ {1 1} = \langle 1 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 1 \rangle = (\mathrm {i}) (- \mathrm {i}) = 1, A _ {1 2} = \langle 1 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 2 \rangle = (\mathrm {i}) (0) = 0,
$$

$$
A _ {1 3} = \langle 1 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 3 \rangle = (\mathrm {i}) (2) = 2 \mathrm {i}, A _ {2 1} = \langle 2 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 1 \rangle = (- 2) (- \mathrm {i}) = 2 \mathrm {i},
$$

$$
A _ {2 2} = \langle 2 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 2 \rangle = (- 2) (0) = 0, A _ {2 3} = \langle 2 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 3 \rangle = (- 2) (2) = - 4,
$$

$$
A _ {3 1} = \langle 3 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 1 \rangle = (- \mathrm {i}) (- \mathrm {i}) = - 1, A _ {3 2} = \langle 3 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 2 \rangle = (- \mathrm {i}) (0) = 0,
$$

$$
A _ {3 3} = \langle 3 | \alpha \rangle \langle \beta | 3 \rangle = (- \mathrm {i}) (2) = - 2 \mathrm {i}.
$$

得

$$
\mathcal {A} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 1 & 0 & 2 \mathrm {i} \\ 2 \mathrm {i} & 0 & - 4 \\ - 1 & 0 & - 2 \mathrm {i} \end{array} \right),
$$

显然宫不是厄米矩阵.

习题 3.27 算符 $\hat { Q }$ 具有一组完备的正交归一本征矢:

$$
\hat {Q} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle = q _ {n} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle \quad (n = 1,   2,   3,   \dots).
$$

(a) 证明 : $\hat { Q }$ 可以写成它的谱分解形式

$$
\hat {Q} = \sum_ {n} q _ {n} | e _ {n} \rangle \langle e _ {n} |
$$

提示 算符是通过它对所有可能矢量的作用来表征的，因此对于任意矢量 $| \alpha \rangle$ 来说，证明:

$$
\hat {Q} \left| \alpha \right\rangle = \left\{\sum_ {n} q _ {n} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle \left\langle e _ {n} \right| \right\} \left| \alpha \right\rangle .
$$

(b) 定义 $\hat { Q }$ 的函数的另一种方法是通过谱分解:

$$
f \left(\hat {Q}\right) = \sum_ {n} f \left(q _ {n}\right) \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle \left\langle e _ {n} \right|.
$$

证明:对于 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \hat { Q } }$ 的情况，这等同于方程 (3.100).

证明 (a) $| \alpha \rangle$ 为任意态矢量，它可以用 $\{ | e _ { n } \rangle \}$ 展开为 $| \alpha  = \sum _ { n } c _ { n } | e _ { n }  , c _ { n } =  e _ { n } | \alpha $ ，所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {Q} | \alpha \rangle = \sum_ {n} c _ {n} \hat {Q} | e _ {n} \rangle = \sum_ {n} \left\langle e _ {n} \mid \alpha \right\rangle q _ {n} | e _ {n} \rangle = \left(\sum_ {n} q _ {n} | e _ {n} \rangle \left\langle e _ {n} \right|\right) | \alpha \rangle \\ \Rightarrow \hat {Q} = \sum_ {n} ^ {n} q _ {n} | e _ {n} \rangle \langle e _ {n} |. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) ${ \hat { Q } } = \sum _ { n } q _ { n } \left| e _ { n } \right. \left. e _ { n } \right|$ |且 $\hat { Q } \left| e _ { n } \right. = \sum _ { n } q _ { n } \left| e _ { n } \right. \left. e _ { n } \mid e _ { n } \right. = \sum _ { n } q _ { n } \left| e _ { n } \right.$ ，同理

$$
\hat {Q} ^ {2} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle = \hat {Q} \hat {Q} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle = \hat {Q} \sum_ {n} q _ {n} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle = \sum_ {n} q _ {n} \hat {Q} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle = q _ {n} ^ {2} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle ,
$$

所以，

$$
\hat {Q} ^ {m} | e _ {n} \rangle = q _ {n} ^ {m} | e _ {n} \rangle .
$$

由方程 $( 3 . 1 0 0 ) \mathrm { e } ^ { \hat { Q } } \equiv 1 + \hat { Q } + \frac { 1 } { 2 } \hat { Q } ^ { 2 } + \frac { 1 } { 3 ! } \hat { Q } ^ { 3 } + \cdot \cdot \cdot$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {Q}} | \alpha \rangle = \left(1 + \hat {Q} + \frac {1}{2} \hat {Q} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{3 !} \hat {Q} ^ {3} + \dots\right) \sum_ {n} | e _ {n} \rangle \langle e _ {n} | \alpha \rangle \\ = \sum_ {m = 0} \frac {1}{m !} \hat {Q} ^ {m} \sum_ {n} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle \left\langle e _ {n} \mid \alpha \right\rangle \\ = \sum_ {m = 0} \frac {1}{m !} q _ {n} ^ {m} \sum_ {n} | e _ {n} \rangle \langle e _ {n} | \alpha \rangle \\ = \sum_ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {q _ {n}} \left| e _ {n} \right\rangle \left\langle e _ {n} \mid \alpha \right\rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {Q}} = \sum_ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {q _ {n}} | e _ {n} \rangle \langle e _ {n} |.
$$

习题 3.28 $\hat { D } = \mathrm { d } / \mathrm { d } x$ (导数算符).求:

(a) $\left( \sin { \hat { D } } \right) x ^ { 5 }$   
$\left( \frac { 1 } { 1 - \hat { D } / 2 } \right) \cos { ( x ) } .$

解答 (a) 将算符函数进行泰勒展开，使得算符直接作用于后面的函数上

泰勒展开公式

$$
\sin x = x - \frac {1}{3 !} x ^ {3} + \frac {1}{5 !} x ^ {5} - \frac {1}{7} x ^ {7} + \dots ,
$$

$$
\sin \hat {D} = \hat {D} - \frac {1}{3 !} \hat {D} ^ {3} + \frac {1}{5 !} \hat {D} ^ {5} - \frac {1}{7} \hat {D} ^ {7} + \dots ,
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\sin \hat {D}\right) x ^ {5} = \left(\hat {D} - \frac {1}{3 !} \hat {D} ^ {3} + \frac {1}{5 !} \hat {D} ^ {5} - \frac {1}{7} \hat {D} ^ {7} + \dots\right) x ^ {5} \\ = \left(\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} - \frac {1}{3 !} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {3}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {3}} + \frac {1}{5 !} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {5}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {5}} - \frac {1}{7} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {7}}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {7}} + \dots\right) x ^ {5} \\ = 5 x ^ {4} - \frac {1}{3 !} 5 \times 4 \times 3 x ^ {2} + \frac {1}{5 !} 5 \times 4 \times 3 \times 2 \times 1 x ^ {0} \\ = 5 x ^ {4} - 1 0 x ^ {2} + 1. \\ \end{array}
$$

(时泰勒展开 ${ \frac { 1 } { 1 - x } } = 1 + x + x ^ { 2 } + x ^ { 3 } + \cdot \cdot \cdot$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {1}{1 - \hat {D} / 2}\right) \cos x = \left(1 + \frac {\mathrm {d}}{2 \mathrm {d} x} + \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{4 \mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} + \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {3}}{8 \mathrm {d} x ^ {3}} + \dots\right) \cos x \\ = \cos x - \frac {1}{2} \sin x - \frac {1}{4} \cos x + \frac {1}{8} \sin x + \dots \\ = \cos x \left(1 - \frac {1}{4} + \frac {1}{1 6} + \dots\right) - \frac {1}{2} \sin x \left(1 - \frac {1}{4} + \frac {1}{1 6} + \dots\right) \\ = \left(\cos x - \frac {1}{2} \sin x\right) \frac {1}{1 + 1 / 4} \\ = \frac {2}{5} (2 \cos x - \sin x). \\ \end{array}
$$

事*习题 3.29 考虑两个相互不对易算符 $\hat { A }$ 和 $\hat { B } \left( \hat { C } = \left[ \hat { A } , \hat { B } \right] \right)$ 但它们都和它们的对易式对易: $\left[ \hat { A } , \hat { C } \right] = \left[ \hat { B } , \hat { C } \right] = 0 ($ 如 $\hat { x }$ 问) $\hat { p }$ ）

(a) 证明:

$$
\left[ \hat {A} ^ {n}, \hat {B} \right] = n \hat {A} ^ {n - 1} \hat {C}.
$$

提示利用方程 (3.65)，对 $n$ 采用归纳法来证明.

(b) 证明

$$
\left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}}, \hat {B} \right] = \lambda \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \hat {C},
$$

其中 $\lambda$ 为任意复数.提示把 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \lambda \hat { A } }$ 展开为罪级数.

(c) 推导贝克-坎贝尔-事斯多夫公式 ①

$$
\mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {A} + \hat {B}} = \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \hat {C} / 2}.
$$

提示定义函数

$$
\hat {f} (\lambda) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda (\hat {A} + \hat {B})}, \quad \hat {g} (\lambda) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2}.
$$

注释当 $\lambda \ : = \ : 0$ 时，这两个函数相等.证明:它们满足同样的微分方 $\frac { \mathrm { d } \hat { f } } { \mathrm { d } \lambda } =$ $\left( { \hat { A } } + { \hat { B } } \right) { \hat { f } }$ 和 $\frac { \mathrm { d } \hat { g } } { \mathrm { d } \lambda } = \left( \hat { A } + \hat { B } \right) \hat { g }$ 因此，对于所有的 $\lambda$ ，这两个函数本身是相等的②

解答 (功用数学归纳法证明

(1) ~ $n = 1$ 时，有 $\left[ { \hat { A } } , { \hat { B } } \right] = 1 \cdot { \hat { A } } ^ { 1 - 1 } { \hat { C } } = { \hat { C } }$ ，显然成立  
(忱 $n = k$ , $\left[ \hat { A } ^ { k } , \hat { B } \right] = k \hat { A } ^ { k - 1 } \hat { C }$ 成立  
(3) Jl!U $n = k + 1$ 时，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {A} ^ {k + 1}, \hat {B} \right] = [ \hat {A} \hat {A} ^ {k}, \hat {B} ] = \hat {A} [ \hat {A} ^ {k}, \hat {B} ] + [ \hat {A} ^ {k}, \hat {B} ] \hat {A} \\ = \hat {A} k \hat {A} ^ {k - 1} \hat {C} + k \hat {A} ^ {k - 1} \hat {C} \hat {A} = k \hat {A} ^ {k} \hat {C} + k \hat {A} ^ {k - 1} \hat {C} \hat {A}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$\left[ { \hat { A } } , { \hat { C } } \right] = 0$ ，得 $\hat { C } \hat { A } = \hat { A } \hat { C }$ 代入上式，得

$$
k \hat {A} ^ {k} \hat {C} + k \hat {A} ^ {k - 1} \hat {A} \hat {C} = k \hat {A} ^ {k} \hat {C} + k \hat {A} ^ {k} \hat {C} = (k + 1) \hat {A} ^ {k} \hat {C}.
$$

即 $n = k + 1$ 时也成立.

所以，

$$
\left[ \hat {A} ^ {n}, \hat {B} \right] = n \hat {A} ^ {n - 1} \hat {C}.
$$

(b) $\mathrm { e } ^ { \lambda \hat { A } }$ 为算符函数，对于这种类型，需要将函数展开为算符的叠加项(泰勒展开) :

$$
\mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} = 1 + \lambda \hat {A} + \frac {1}{2 !} \lambda \hat {A} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{3 !} \lambda \hat {A} ^ {3} + \dots = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\lambda^ {n} \hat {A} ^ {n}}{n !},
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}}, \hat {B} \right] = \left[ \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\lambda^ {n} \hat {A} ^ {n}}{n !}, \hat {B} \right] = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\lambda^ {n}}{n !} \left[ \hat {A} ^ {n}, \hat {B} \right] \\ = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\lambda^ {n}}{n !} n \hat {A} ^ {n - 1} \hat {C} = \lambda \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {\lambda^ {n - 1}}{(n - 1) !} \hat {A} ^ {n - 1} \hat {C} \\ = \lambda \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \hat {C}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(推导中利用了 (a) 中的结论.)

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \lambda} \left[ \hat {A} (\lambda) \hat {B} (\lambda) \right] = \hat {A} ^ {\prime} (\lambda) \hat {B} (\lambda) + \hat {A} (\lambda) \hat {B} ^ {\prime} (\lambda).
$$

(c) 由于

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} = 1 + \lambda \hat {A} + \frac {1}{2 !} \lambda \hat {A} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{3 !} \lambda \hat {A} ^ {3} + \dots = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\lambda^ {n} \hat {A} ^ {n}}{n !}, \\ \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \lambda} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}}\right) = \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \lambda} \left(\sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\lambda^ {n} \hat {A} ^ {n}}{n !}\right) = \hat {A} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {\lambda^ {n - 1} \hat {A} ^ {n - 1}}{(n - 1) !} \\ = \hat {A} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

根据提示构建两个函数

$$
\hat {f} (\lambda) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda (\hat {A} + \hat {B})}, \quad \hat {g} (\lambda) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2}.
$$

那么

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \lambda} f = (\hat {A} + \hat {B}) f,
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \lambda} g = \hat {A} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \hat {B} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} (- \lambda \hat {C}) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2}.
$$

最好能将其化简为 ${ \frac { \mathrm { d } } { \mathrm { d } \lambda } } f = \left( { \hat { A } } + { \hat { B } } \right) f$ 这样的类型因此，需要将多出的系数提到 指数的前面由$\left[ \mathrm { e } ^ { \lambda \hat { A } } , \hat { B } \right] = \lambda \mathrm { e } ^ { \lambda \hat { A } } \hat { C }$ 得 $\mathrm { e } ^ { \lambda \hat { A } } { \hat { B } } = \lambda \mathrm { e } ^ { \lambda \hat { A } } { \hat { C } } + { \hat { B } } \mathrm { e } ^ { \lambda \hat { A } }$ 并代入等式右边的第二项，且算符 $\hat { C }$ $\hat { A }$ ~ $\hat { B }$ 都对易，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \lambda} g = \hat {A} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \hat {B} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} \left(- \lambda \hat {C}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2} \\ = \hat {A} g + \left(\lambda \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}} \hat {C} + \hat {B} \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {A}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\lambda \hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \lambda^ {2} \hat {C} / 2} - \lambda \hat {C} g \\ = (\hat {A} + \lambda \hat {C} + \hat {B} - \lambda \hat {C}) g \\ = \left(\hat {A} + \hat {B}\right) g. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，对所有的参数 $\lambda$ ，函数 $f$ 和 $g$ 都相等.特别在 $\lambda = 1$ 时，得证

$$
\mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {A} + \hat {B}} = \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {A}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\hat {B}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \hat {C} / 2}.
$$

习题 3.30 利用例题 3.9 中的方法推导出位置空间波函数到能量空间波函数( $\left( c _ { n } \left( t \right) \right)$ 的变换.假设能谱是不连续的，势能不舍时间.

解答 能量本征态用 $| n \rangle$ 表示，能量空间波函数为

$$
c _ {n} (t) = \langle n | S (t) \rangle .
$$

插入坐标表象下的恒等算符

$$
\langle n | \int | x \rangle \langle x | \mathrm {d} x | S (t) \rangle = \int \mathrm {d} x \langle n | x \rangle \langle x | S (t) \rangle .
$$

由 $\langle x | n \rangle$ 为坐标空间的本征态， $\langle x | S \left( t \right) \rangle = \varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ ，所以

$$
c _ {n} (t) = \int \psi_ {n} (x) ^ {*} \Psi (x, t) d x.
$$

# 补充苟l{

*习题 3.31 勒让德多项式.用格拉姆-施密特方法(习题 .4)在区间 $- 1 \leqslant x \leqslant 1$ 内对函数 $1 , x , x ^ { 2 } , x ^ { 3 }$ 进行正交归一化.你可能会认出这些结果一一(除了归一化外)它们是勒让德多项式(习题 2.64 和教材表 4.1).

解答

由 $= 1 , \ \langle e _ { 1 } | e _ { 1 } \rangle = \int _ { - 1 } ^ { 1 } 1 \mathrm { d } x = 2 .$ ld:z; = 2, ~ leD =言， $| e _ { 1 } ^ { \prime } \rangle = \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { 2 } }$

由配 $| e _ { 2 } \rangle = x , \langle e _ { 1 } ^ { \prime } | e _ { 2 } \rangle = { \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { 2 } } } \int _ { - 1 } ^ { 1 } x \mathrm { d } x = 0 , \langle e _ { 2 } | e _ { 2 } \rangle = \int _ { - 1 } ^ { 1 } x \mathrm { d } x = { \frac { 2 } { 3 } }$ 2J -1 $| e _ { 2 } ^ { \prime } \rangle = \sqrt { \frac { 3 } { 2 } } x$

$$
\left| e _ {3} \right\rangle = x ^ {2}; \left\langle e _ {1} ^ {\prime} \right| e _ {3} \rangle = \int_ {- 1} ^ {1} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} x ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{3}; \left\langle e _ {2} ^ {\prime} \right| e _ {3} \rangle = \int_ {- 1} ^ {1} \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2} x ^ {3} \mathrm {d} x = 0;
$$

设

$$
\left| e _ {3} ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = c \left(\left| e _ {3} \right\rangle - \frac {\sqrt {2}}{3} \left| e _ {1} ^ {\prime} \right\rangle\right) = c \left(x ^ {2} - \frac {1}{3}\right),
$$

它与 $| e _ { 1 } ^ { \prime } \rangle$ 及 $| e _ { 2 } ^ { \prime } \rangle$ 正交，归一化

$$
1 = \left\langle e _ {3} ^ {\prime} \mid e _ {3} ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = | c | ^ {2} \int_ {- 1} ^ {1} \left(x ^ {2} - \frac {1}{3}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = | c | ^ {2} \frac {8}{4 5} \rightarrow c = \sqrt {\frac {4 5}{8}},
$$

得

$$
\left| e _ {3} ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = \sqrt {\frac {4 5}{8}} \left(x ^ {2} - \frac {1}{3}\right) = \sqrt {\frac {5}{2}} \left(\frac {3}{2} x ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2}\right),
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| e _ {4} \right\rangle = x ^ {3}; \left\langle e _ {1} ^ {\prime} \mid e _ {4} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \int_ {- 1} ^ {1} x ^ {3} \mathrm {d} x = 0; \left\langle e _ {2} ^ {\prime} \mid e _ {4} \right\rangle = \sqrt {\frac {3}{2}} \int_ {- 1} ^ {1} x ^ {4} \mathrm {d} x = \sqrt {\frac {3}{2}} \frac {2}{5} = \frac {\sqrt {6}}{5}; \\ \left\langle e _ {3} ^ {\prime} \right| e _ {4} \rangle = \sqrt {\frac {5}{2}} \int_ {- 1} ^ {1} \left(\frac {3}{2} x ^ {5} - \frac {1}{2} x ^ {3}\right) \mathrm {d} x = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

设

$$
\left| e _ {4} ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = c \left(\left| e _ {4} \right\rangle - \frac {\sqrt {6}}{5} \left| e _ {2} ^ {\prime} \right\rangle\right) = c \left(x ^ {3} - \frac {3 x}{5}\right),
$$

它与 $| e _ { 1 } ^ { \prime } \rangle , | e _ { 2 } ^ { \prime } \rangle$ 及 $| e _ { 3 } ^ { \prime } \rangle$ 正变，归一化

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle e _ {4} ^ {\prime} \mid e _ {4} ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = \left| c \right| ^ {2} \int_ {- 1} ^ {1} \left(x ^ {3} - \frac {3 x}{5}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = \left| c \right| ^ {2} \frac {8}{1 7 5} \rightarrow c = \sqrt {\frac {1 7 5}{8}} = \frac {5}{2} \sqrt {\frac {7}{2}}, \\ \left| e _ {4} ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = \frac {5}{2} \sqrt {\frac {7}{2}} \left(x ^ {3} - \frac {3 x}{5}\right) = \sqrt {\frac {7}{2}} \left(\frac {5 x ^ {3}}{2} - \frac {3 x}{2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

这样我们构造出了四个相互正交且归一(在区间 $- 1 \leqslant x \leqslant 1$ 的函数 $\left| e _ { 1 } ^ { \prime } \right. , \left| e _ { 2 } ^ { \prime } \right. , \left| e _ { 3 } ^ { \prime } \right. , \left| e _ { 4 } ^ { \prime } \right.$ .

习题 3.32 反厄米(或厄米共辐)算符等于其负厄米共辄:

$$
\hat {Q} ^ {\dagger} = - \hat {Q}.
$$

(a) 证明:反厄米算符的期望值是虚数.   
(b) 证明:反厄米算符的本征值是虚数.  
(c) 证明:属于反厄米算符不同本征值的本征矢是正交的  
(d) 证明 两个厄米算符的对易式是反厄米的.那么两个反厄米算符的对易式 如何?   
(e) 证明 任何一个算符 $\hat { Q }$ 都可以表示为一个厄米算符 $\hat { A }$ 和一个反厄米算符$\hat { B }$ 之和，用 $\hat { Q }$ 和它的共辄算符 $\hat { Q } ^ { \dagger }$ ↑表示出 $\hat { A }$ 和 $\hat { B }$

证明 (a) 期望值

$$
\left\langle \hat {Q} \right\rangle = \left\langle \psi | \hat {Q} \psi \right\rangle = \left\langle \hat {Q} ^ {\dagger} \psi | \psi \right\rangle = - \left\langle \hat {Q} \psi | \psi \right\rangle = - \left\langle \psi | \hat {Q} \psi \right\rangle^ {*} = - \left\langle \hat {Q} \right\rangle^ {*},
$$

所以 $\langle Q \rangle$ 是虚数

(b) $\psi$ 为 $\hat { Q }$ 的本征态，本征值为 $q$

$$
\left\langle \psi \mid \hat {Q} \psi \right\rangle = \langle \psi \mid q \psi \rangle = q \langle \psi \mid \psi \rangle = q.
$$

$$
\left\langle \psi \mid \hat {Q} \psi \right\rangle = \left\langle \hat {Q} ^ {\dagger} \psi \mid \psi \right\rangle = - \left\langle \hat {Q} \psi \mid \psi \right\rangle = - \left\langle \psi \mid \hat {Q} \psi \right\rangle^ {*} = - q ^ {*} \left\langle \psi \mid \psi \right\rangle^ {*} = - q ^ {*}.
$$

则有

$$
q = - q ^ {*}.
$$

所以本征值 $q$ 为虚数.

(c) $| \alpha \rangle$ 和 $| \beta \rangle$ 为 $\hat { Q }$ 的两个本征态，其相应的本征值为 $q _ { \alpha }$ 和 $q _ { \beta }$ ，且 $q _ { \alpha } \neq q _ { \beta }$

$$
Q   | \alpha \rangle = q _ {\alpha}   | \alpha \rangle , \quad Q   | \beta \rangle = q _ {\beta}   | \beta \rangle ,
$$

则

$$
\left\langle \alpha \mid Q \beta \right\rangle = q _ {\beta} \left\langle \alpha \mid \beta \right\rangle .
$$

$$
\langle \alpha   |   Q \beta \rangle = \left\langle Q ^ {\dagger} \alpha   \Big |   \beta \right\rangle = -   \langle Q \alpha   |   \beta \rangle = - q _ {\alpha} ^ {*}   \langle \alpha   |   \beta \rangle = q _ {\alpha}   \langle \alpha   |   \beta \rangle .
$$

(利用到本征值为虚数.)

结合以上两式，得

$$
\left(q _ {\alpha} - q _ {\beta}\right) \langle \alpha | \beta \rangle = 0.
$$

因 $q _ { \alpha } \neq q _ { \beta }$ ，所以

$$
\langle \alpha | \beta \rangle = 0.
$$

即不同本征值对应的本征态正交.

(d) 由 $\left( \hat { P } \hat { Q } \right) ^ { \dagger } = \hat { Q } ^ { \dagger } \hat { P } ^ { \dagger }$ 所以如果户 $\hat { P } = \hat { P } ^ { \dagger }$ 和 $\hat { Q } = \hat { Q } ^ { \dagger }$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {P}, \hat {Q} \right] ^ {\dagger} = \left(\hat {P} \hat {Q} - \hat {Q} \hat {P}\right) ^ {\dagger} = \hat {Q} ^ {\dagger} \hat {P} ^ {\dagger} - \hat {P} ^ {\dagger} \hat {Q} ^ {\dagger} = \hat {Q} \hat {P} - \hat {P} \hat {Q} \\ = - \left[ \hat {P}, \hat {Q} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

如果 $\hat { P } = - \hat { P } ^ { \dagger }$ 和 $\hat { Q } = - \hat { Q } ^ { \dagger }$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left[ \hat {P}, \hat {Q} \right] ^ {\dagger} = \hat {Q} ^ {\dagger} \hat {P} ^ {\dagger} - \hat {P} ^ {\dagger} \hat {Q} ^ {\dagger} \\ = (- \hat {Q}) (- \hat {P}) - (- \hat {P}) (- \hat {Q}) \\ \mathbf {\Sigma} = - \left[ \hat {P}, \hat {Q} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以在两种情况下对易子都是反厄米的.

(e) $\hat { Q } = \hat { A } + \hat { B } , \hat { Q } ^ { \dagger } = \hat { A } ^ { \dagger } + \hat { B } ^ { \dagger } = \hat { A } - \hat { B }$ ，则

$$
\hat {A} = \frac {\hat {Q} + \hat {Q} ^ {\dagger}}{2}, \quad \hat {B} = \frac {\hat {Q} - \hat {Q} ^ {\dagger}}{2}.
$$

习题 3.33 顺序测量.可观测量 $A$ 用算符 $\hat { A }$ 表示，它的两个归一化本征态是 $\psi _ { 1 }$ 和 $\psi _ { 2 }$ ，本征值分别为 $a _ { 1 }$ 和 $a _ { 2 }$ . 算特 $\hat { B }$ 表示可观测量 $B$ 它的两个归一化本征态是$\phi _ { 1 }$ 和 $\phi _ { 2 }$ 本征值分别为 $b _ { 1 }$ 和 $b _ { 2 }$ ，两组本征态之间关系为

$$
\psi_ {1} = (3 \phi_ {1} + 4 \phi_ {2}) / 5, \quad \psi_ {2} = (4 \phi_ {1} - 3 \phi_ {2}) / 5.
$$

(a) 测量可观测量 $A$ 所得结果为 $a _ { 1 }$ 那么在测量之后(瞬时)体系处在什么态?  
(b) 如果再测量 $B$ 可能的结果是什么?它们出现的几率是多少?  
(c) 在恰好测出 $B$ 之后，再次测量 $A$ 那么结果为 $a _ { 1 }$ 的几率是多少? (注意如果已经告诉你测量 $B$ 的结果，答案会完全不同)

解答 (a) 当对体系测量 $\hat { A }$ 得到 $a _ { 1 }$ 时，体系的波函数会明缩为 $\hat { A }$ 本征值为 $a _ { 1 }$ 的本征态 $\psi _ { 1 }$ ，所以在测量之后(瞬时)体系在 $\psi _ { 1 }$ 态.

(b)由 $\psi _ { 1 } = ( 3 \phi _ { 1 } + 4 \phi _ { 2 } ) / 5$ 是 $\hat { B }$ 的本征态 $\phi _ { 1 }$ 和 $\phi _ { 2 }$ 线性叠加，当对 $\psi _ { 1 }$ 态测量 $\hat { B }$ 时，可能得到 $b _ { 1 }$ 或者 $b _ { 2 }$ 得到 $b _ { 1 }$ 的几率为 9/25，得到 $b _ { 2 }$ 的几率为 16/25.  
(c) 如呆在测量 $\hat { B }$ 时得到的结果是 $b _ { 1 }$ ，则波函数拥缩到 $\phi _ { 1 }$ 态(几率为 9/25)，由

$$
\psi_ {1} = (3 \phi_ {1} + 4 \phi_ {2}) / 5, \quad \psi_ {2} = (4 \phi_ {1} - 3 \phi_ {2}) / 5,
$$

可以解出

$$
\phi_ {1} = (3 \psi_ {1} + 4 \psi_ {2}) / 5,
$$

所以再测量 $\hat { A }$ 时，得到 $a _ { 1 }$ 的几率为 9/25.

同理，如果在测量 $\hat { B }$ 时得到的是 $b _ { 2 }$ ，则波函数拥缩到 $\phi _ { 2 }$ 态(几率为 16/25)

$$
\phi_ {2} = (4 \psi_ {1} - 3 \psi_ {2}) / 5,
$$

所以再测量 $\hat { A }$ 时得到 $a _ { 1 }$ 的几率为 16/25.

所以测量 $\hat { B }$ ，再测量 $\hat { A }$ 得到 $a _ { 1 }$ 的几率为

$$
P = \frac {9}{2 5} \cdot \frac {9}{2 5} + \frac {1 6}{2 5} \cdot \frac {1 6}{2 5} = \frac {3 3 7}{6 2 5} = 0. 5 3 9 2.
$$

# 串串串巳. $\bigcirc$ 习题 3.34

(a) 求无限深方势阱第 $n$ 个定态动量空间的波函数 $\varPhi _ { n } \left( p , t \right)$

求几率密度 $\left| \varPhi _ { n } \left( p , t \right) \right| ^ { 2 }$ ，并画出 $n = 1 , n = 2 , n = 5$ 和 $n = 1 0$ 时的几率密度函数.在 $n$ 取很大值时 $p$ 的最可能值是多少?这是你所预期的吗?①将你的结果和习题 3.10 作对比  
(c) 利用 $\boldsymbol { \varPhi } _ { n } \left( \boldsymbol { p } , t \right)$ 计算 $p ^ { 2 }$ 在第 $n$ 个态时的期望值.将你的结果与习题 2.4对比

解害 (a) 一维无限深方势阱的定态波函数和能量本征值为

$$
\varPsi_ {n} \left(x, t\right) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi}{a} x\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar}, 0 <   x <   a; n = 1, 2, 3, \dots ;
$$

$$
E _ {n} = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}.
$$

动量空间的披函数由下式得出:

$$
\varPhi (p, t) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \varPsi (x, t) \mathrm {d} x;
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Phi_ {n} (p, t) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi}{a} x\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar a}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} n \pi x / a} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} n \pi x / a}}{2 \mathrm {i}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar a}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \frac {1}{2 \mathrm {i}} \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (n \pi / a - p / \hbar) x} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (n \pi / a + p / \hbar) x} \right] \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {\pi \hbar a}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \frac {1}{2 \mathrm {i}} \left. \left[ \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (n \pi / a - p / \hbar) x}}{\mathrm {i} (n \pi / a - p / \hbar)} + \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (n \pi / a + p / \hbar) x}}{\mathrm {i} (n \pi / a + p / \hbar)} \right] \right| _ {0} ^ {a} \\ = \frac {- 1}{2 \sqrt {\pi \hbar a}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \left[ \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (n \pi / a - p / \hbar) a} - 1}{n \pi / a - p / \hbar} + \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (n \pi / a + p / \hbar) a} - 1}{n \pi / a + p / \hbar} \right] \\ = - \frac {1}{2} \sqrt {\frac {a}{\pi \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \frac {2 n \pi}{(n \pi) ^ {2} - (a p / \hbar) ^ {2}} \left[ (- 1) ^ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / \hbar} - 1 \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {a \pi}{\hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \frac {n}{(n \pi) ^ {2} - (a p / \hbar) ^ {2}} \left[ 1 - (- 1) ^ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / \hbar} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

注意到

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 - (- 1) ^ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / \hbar} = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / (2 \hbar)} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} a p / (2 \hbar)} - (- 1) ^ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / \hbar} \right] \\ = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / (2 \hbar)} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} a p / (2 \hbar)} + \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / \hbar} \right] = 2 \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / (2 \hbar)} \cos \left(\frac {a p}{2 \hbar}\right), n = 1, 3, 5, \dots \\ \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / (2 \hbar)} \left[ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} a p / (2 \hbar)} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / \hbar} \right] = 2 \mathrm {i} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a p / (2 \hbar)} \sin \left(\frac {a p}{2 \hbar}\right), n = 2, 4, 6, \dots \end{array} \right. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\varPhi_{n}\left(p,t\right)=\sqrt{\frac{a\pi}{\hbar}}\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}E_{n}t/\hbar}\frac{n}{\left(n\pi\right)^{2}-\left(ap/\hbar\right)^{2}}\\ \times\left\{\begin{array}{l}2\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}ap/(2\hbar)}\cos\left(\frac{ap}{2\hbar}\right), n=1,3,5,\cdots\\ 2\mathrm{i}\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}ap/(2\hbar)}\sin\left(\frac{ap}{2\hbar}\right), n=2,4,6,\cdots\end{array}\right.
$$

(b) .对 $n = 1 , n = 2 , n = 5$ 和 $n = 1 0$ 儿率密度函数分别为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \Phi_ {1} (p, t) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {4 a \pi}{\hbar} \frac {\cos^ {2} \left(\frac {a p}{2 \hbar}\right)}{\left[ \pi^ {2} - (a p / \hbar) ^ {2} \right] ^ {2}}, \\ \left| \Phi_ {2} (p, t) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {1 6 a \pi}{\hbar} \frac {\sin^ {2} \left(\frac {a p}{2 \hbar}\right)}{\left[ 4 \pi^ {2} - (a p / \hbar) ^ {2} \right] ^ {2}}, \\ \left| \Phi_ {5} (p, t) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {1 0 0 a \pi}{\hbar} \frac {\cos^ {2} \left(\frac {a p}{2 \hbar}\right)}{\left[ 2 5 \pi^ {2} - (a p / \hbar) ^ {2} \right] ^ {2}}, \\ \left| \Phi_ {1 0} (p, t) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {4 0 0 a \pi}{\hbar} \frac {\sin^ {2} \left(\frac {a p}{2 \hbar}\right)}{\left[ 1 0 0 \pi^ {2} - (a p / \hbar) ^ {2} \right] ^ {2}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

披函数的模平方图如图 $3 . 1 \sim$ ，图 .4所示.

$$
n = 1:
$$

$$
\operatorname {P l o t} \left[ (\cos [ x / 2 ]) ^ {\wedge} / (\text {P i} ^ {\wedge} - x ^ {\wedge} 2) ^ {\wedge} 2, \{x, - 1 0, 1 0 \} \right]
$$

$$
n = 2:
$$

$$
\operatorname {P l o t} \left[ (\sin [ x / 2 ]) ^ {\wedge 2} / (4 * P i ^ {\wedge 2} - x ^ {\wedge 2}) ^ {\wedge 2}, \{x, - 1 6, 1 6 \} \right]
$$

![](images/3cca2cbc2f642525359a4757f20ad2bda8f3af08f20bff3f4d467b59ff8ea22c.jpg)

$$

$$
\text {P l o t} [ (\cos [ x / 2 ]) ^ {\wedge} / (2 5 * \mathrm {P i} ^ {\wedge} 2 - x ^ {\wedge} 2) ^ {\wedge} 2, \{x, - 3 5, 3 5 \} ]
$$
