.4

从几率函数的表达式可以看出，分母为零时动量出现的几率最大，此时 $p = \pm \left( n \pi \hbar / a \right)$ 从物理上来判断，由于 $E _ { n } = n ^ { 2 } \pi ^ { 2 } \hbar ^ { 2 } / 2 m a ^ { 2 }$ ，得 $p = \pm \left( n \pi \hbar / a \right)$ .因为上面作圄令 $a = 1$ 和 $\hbar = 1$ ，所以最大值出现在 $n \pi$ (3.1 6.3, 15.7 30.1); 直观上看，极值点处于 (0 5, 15, 30). $n$ 取值越大时，越接近我们预期的结果.

(c) $x \equiv a p / n \pi \hbar$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle p ^ {2} \right\rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p ^ {2} \left| \Phi_ {n} (p, t) \right| ^ {2} d p \\ = \frac {4 n ^ {2} \pi a}{\hbar} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {p ^ {2}}{\left[ (n \pi) ^ {2} - (a p / \hbar) ^ {2} \right] ^ {2}} \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \cos^ {2} [ a p / (2 \hbar) ] \\ \sin^ {2} [ a p / (2 \hbar) ] \end{array} \right\} \mathrm {d} p \\ = \frac {4 n \hbar^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {2}}{(1 - x ^ {2}) ^ {2}} T _ {n} (x) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {4 n \hbar^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} I _ {n}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
T _ {n} (x) \equiv \left\{ \begin{array}{l} \cos^ {2} \left(n \pi x / 2\right), n = 1, 3, 5, \dots \\ \sin^ {2} \left(n \pi x / 2\right), n = 2, 4, 6, \dots \end{array} \right.,
$$

$$
I _ {n} \equiv \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {2}}{(1 - x ^ {2}) ^ {2}} T _ {n} (x) \mathrm {d} x.
$$

由因式分解得

$$
{\frac {x ^ {2}}{1 - x ^ {2}}} = {\frac {1}{4}} \left[ {\frac {1}{(x - 1) ^ {2}}} + {\frac {1}{(x + 1) ^ {2}}} + {\frac {1}{x - 1}} - {\frac {1}{x + 1}} \right],
$$

$$
I _ {n} = \frac {1}{4} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left[ \frac {1}{(x - 1) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{(x + 1) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{x - 1} - \frac {1}{x + 1} \right] T _ {n} (x) d x.
$$

对 $n$ 为奇数的情况=

$$
\int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{(x \pm 1) ^ {k}} \cos^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{2} x\right) d x = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{y ^ {k}} \cos^ {2} \left[ \frac {n \pi}{2} (y \mp 1) \right] d y = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{y ^ {k}} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{2} y\right) d y,
$$

对 $n$ 为偶数的情况=

$$
\int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{(x \pm 1) ^ {k}} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{2} x\right) \mathrm {d} x = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{y ^ {k}} \sin^ {2} \left[ \frac {n \pi}{2} (y \mp 1) \right] \mathrm {d} y = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{y ^ {k}} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{2} y\right) \mathrm {d} y.
$$

所以在两种情况下都有

$$
I _ {n} = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(\frac {1}{y ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{y}\right) \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{2} y\right) \mathrm {d} y = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{y ^ {2}} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{2} y\right) \mathrm {d} y,
$$

(第三项积分由于被积函数是奇函数，故为零.)所以

$$
I _ {n} = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{y ^ {2}} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{2} y\right) \mathrm {d} y = \frac {n \pi}{4} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\sin^ {2} u}{u ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} u = \frac {n \pi^ {2}}{4},
$$

$$
\left<   p ^ {2} \right> = \frac {4 n \hbar^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} I _ {n} = \frac {4 n \hbar^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \frac {n \pi^ {2}}{4} = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}.
$$

这与坐标空间中所得的计算结果一致(当然它们也必须一样)，

$$
\left\langle p ^ {2} \right\rangle = - \hbar^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \Psi_ {n} (x, t) \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2} \Psi_ {n} (x , t)}{\mathrm {d} x ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {n ^ {2} \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}.
$$

习题 考虑波函数

$$
\varPsi (x, 0) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} { \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 n \lambda}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} 2 \pi x / \lambda},} & {- n \lambda <   x <   n \lambda ,} \\ {0,} & {\text {其 他}.} \end{array} \right.
$$

其中 $n$ 是某个正整数，在区间 $- n \lambda < x < n \lambda$ 上，它是纯正弦函数(波长为 $\lambda$ )，因为振荡没有伸展到无限远处，它的动量仍然有一个分布范围，求出动量空间的波函数 $\varPhi \left( p , 0 \right)$ ，画出 $\left| \varPsi \left( x , 0 \right) \right| ^ { 2 }$ 和 $\left| \phi \left( p , 0 \right) \right| ^ { 2 }$ 的草图，求出峰宽 $w _ { x }$ 和 $w _ { p }$ (主峰两边零点之间的距离).注意当 $n \to \infty$ 时，每一个峰宽如何变化.利用 $w _ { x }$ 和 $w _ { p }$ 估算 $\Delta x$ 和$\Delta p$ 验证不确定性原理是否满足，注意 如果你尝试计算 $\sigma _ { p }$ ，将会感到很意外.你能够分析问题的原因所在吗?

解答 动量二 问波函数

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPhi (p, 0) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \varPsi (x, 0) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {n \pi \hbar \lambda}} \int_ {- n \lambda} ^ {n \lambda} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (2 \pi / \lambda - p / \hbar) x} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {n \pi \hbar \lambda}} \left. \left[ \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (2 \pi / \lambda - p / \hbar) x}}{\mathrm {i} (2 \pi / \lambda - p / \hbar)} \right] \right| _ {- n \lambda} ^ {n \lambda} \\ = \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {n \pi \hbar \lambda}} \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (2 \pi / \lambda - p / \hbar) n \lambda} - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (2 \pi / \lambda - p / \hbar) n \lambda}}{\mathrm {i} (2 \pi / \lambda - p / \hbar)} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar \lambda}{n \pi}} \frac {\sin (n p \lambda / \hbar)}{p \lambda - 2 \pi \hbar}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\left| \varPhi (p, 0) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar \lambda}{n \pi} \frac {\sin^ {2} (n p \lambda / \hbar)}{(p \lambda - 2 \pi \hbar) ^ {2}}, - \infty <   p <   \infty ,
$$

$$
| \varPsi   (x, 0) | ^ {2} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {{ \frac {1}{2 n \lambda},}} & {{- n \lambda <   x <   n \lambda ,}} \\ {{0,}} & {{\text {其 他}.}} \end{array} \right.
$$

它们的图形如图 和图 所示.

![](images/63cfe67ccaf30fcb1217bb905a39e022f486a601458c0c19d56efc3d4b567852.jpg)  
3.5

![](images/cf82df9e0cd2c3d0cf542cab958c11628db063dbf16f53afb50d13ce2194e2b0.jpg)  
3.6

$| \psi | ^ { 2 }$ 的宽度为 $w _ { x } = 2 n \lambda . | \varPhi | ^ { 2 }$ $| \varPhi | ^ { 2 }$ 的最大值在 $2 \pi \hbar / \lambda$ 处(注意此处分母为零，但是分子也为零)，这个最大值两侧的零点出现在 ${ \frac { 2 \pi \hbar } { \lambda } } \left( 1 \pm { \frac { 1 } { 2 n } } \right)$ ，所以 $w _ { p } = { \frac { 2 \pi \hbar } { n \lambda } }$

在这个极限下 (n →∞ "， →∞， $w _ { p }  0 \mathrm { . }$ ，粒子有比较确定的动量，但是坐标非常不确定.

$$
w _ {x} w _ {p} = 2 n \lambda \frac {2 \pi \hbar}{n \lambda} = 4 \pi \hbar > \frac {\hbar}{2}
$$

满足不确定性原理

如呆试图计算 $\sigma _ { p } = \sqrt { \langle p ^ { 2 } \rangle - \langle p \rangle ^ { 2 } }$ ，将会发现 $\langle p \rangle = 0$ ，但是

$$
\left\langle p ^ {2} \right\rangle = \frac {\hbar \lambda}{n \pi} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p ^ {2} \frac {\sin^ {2} (n p \lambda / \hbar)}{(p \lambda - 2 \pi \hbar) ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} p \sim \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \sin^ {2} (n p \lambda / \hbar) \mathrm {d} p \rightarrow \infty ,
$$

出现这个问题的根据在于波函数 $\varPsi \left( x , 0 \right)$ 在瑞点 $\pm n \lambda$ 处是不连续的，这导致 $\hat { p } \varPsi = - \mathrm { i } \hbar d \varPsi / \mathrm { d } x$ 在端点处产生 $\delta$ 函数，而(ll $ \psi | p ^ { 2 } | \psi  =  \hat { p } \varPsi | \hat { p } \varPsi $ ;是 $\delta$ 函数模平方的积分，结果为无限大.一般来讲，如果想要$\sigma _ { p }$ 有限，波函数必须连续.

习题 3.36 假设

$$
\varPsi (x, 0) = \frac {A}{x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}}, (- \infty <   x <   \infty)
$$

其中 $A$ 和 $a$ 是常数.

(a) 通过对 $\varPsi \left( x , 0 \right)$ 归一化确定 $A$ 的值.  
(b) 求出 $\left. { x } \right. , \left. { x ^ { 2 } } \right.$ 和 $\sigma _ { x }$ ( $t = 0$ 时刻).   
(c) 求出动量空间的技函数 $\varPhi \left( p , 0 \right)$ 并验证它是归一化的，  
(d) $\varPhi \left( p , 0 \right)$ 来计算 $\left. p \right. , \left. p ^ { 2 } \right.$ 和 $\sigma _ { p }$ ( $t = 0$ 时刻).   
(e) 验证该态下的海森伯不确定性原理.

解害 (a) 归一化波函数

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = 2 | A | ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = 2 \left| A \right| ^ {2} \frac {1}{2 a ^ {2}} \left[ \frac {1}{x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{a} \arctan (x / a) \right] \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {\infty} \\ = \frac {\pi}{2 a ^ {3}} | A | ^ {2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
n = n ^ {\prime}.
$$

(b) 期望值

$$
\langle x \rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x | \Psi (x, 0) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {x}{(x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}) ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = 0, (\text {被 积 函 数 是 奇 函 数})
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} x ^ {2} | \Psi (x, 0) | ^ {2} d x = | A | ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {x ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}) ^ {2}} d x \\ = \frac {\left| A \right| ^ {2}}{a} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {(x / a) ^ {2}}{\left[ (x / a) ^ {2} + 1 \right] ^ {2}} d (x / a) \\ = \frac {\left| A \right| ^ {2}}{a} \int_ {- \pi / 2} ^ {\pi / 2} \tan^ {2} \theta \cos^ {4} \theta \frac {\mathrm {d} \theta}{\cos^ {2} \theta} = \frac {\left| A \right| ^ {2}}{a} \int_ {- \pi / 2} ^ {\pi / 2} \sin^ {2} \theta \mathrm {d} \theta \\ = \frac {| A | ^ {2}}{a} \frac {\pi}{2} = a ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

运算中用到 $x / a = \tan \theta$ 的代换.所以

$$
\sigma_ {x} = \sqrt {\left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle - \left\langle x \right\rangle^ {2}} = a.
$$

(忡量壁间的波函数为( $\left( { \mathfrak { A } } \xi \equiv { \frac { x } { a } } \right)$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varPhi (p,0) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \varPsi (x,0) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \frac {A}{x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {A}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\cos (p x / \hbar) + \mathrm {i} \sin (p x / \hbar)}{x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {A}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\cos (p x / \hbar)}{x ^ {2} + a ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {A}{a \sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\cos (\frac {p a}{\hbar} \frac {x}{a})}{(x / a) ^ {2} + 1} \mathrm {d} \left(\frac {x}{a}\right) \\ = \frac {2 A}{a \sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\cos \left(\frac {p a}{\hbar} \xi\right)}{\xi^ {2} + 1} \mathrm {d} \xi \\ = \frac {2}{a \sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \sqrt {\frac {2 a ^ {3}}{\pi} \frac {\pi}{2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- | p | a / \hbar} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {a}{\hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- | p | a / \hbar}. \\ \end{array}
$$

计算上式利用了

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\cos (\alpha \xi)}{1 + \xi^ {2}} \mathrm {d} \xi = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \pi \mathrm {e} ^ {- \alpha} / 2, & \alpha > 0, \\ \pi \mathrm {e} ^ {\alpha} / 2, & \alpha <   0. \end{array} \right.
$$

验证归一化

$$
\int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} | \Phi (p, 0) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} p = \frac {a}{\hbar} 2 \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 p a / \hbar} \mathrm {d} p = \frac {2 a}{\hbar} \left(- \frac {\hbar}{2 a} \mathrm {e} ^ {- p a / \hbar} \Big | _ {0} ^ {\infty}\right) = 1.
$$

动量期望值

$$
\langle p \rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p | \Phi (p, 0) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} p = 0, \quad (\text {被 积 函 数 为 奇 函 数})
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left<   p ^ {2} \right> = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p ^ {2} \left| \varPhi (p, 0) \right| ^ {2} \mathrm {d} p = \frac {a}{\hbar} 2 \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} p ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 p a / \hbar} \mathrm {d} p = \frac {2 a}{\hbar} 2 \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 a}\right) ^ {3} \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 a ^ {2}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\sigma_ {p} = \sqrt {\langle p ^ {2} \rangle - \langle p \rangle^ {2}} = \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2} a}.
$$

(e) 将上述结果代入

$$
\sigma_ {x} \sigma_ {p} = a \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2} a} = \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2}} > \frac {\hbar}{2},
$$

故满足不确定性原理.

*习题 3.37 位力定理，利用方程 (3.73) 证明:

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \left<   x p \right> = 2 \left<   T \right> - \left<   x \frac {\partial V}{\partial x} \right > ,
$$

其中 $T$ 是动能 $( H = T + V )$ . 对于定态，上式的左边为 O(为什么? )，所以

$$
2 \left\langle T \right\rangle = \left\langle x \frac {\mathrm {d} V}{\mathrm {d} x} \right\rangle .
$$

这称为位力定理.利用它证明谐振子的定态有 $\langle T \rangle = \langle V \rangle$ 并验证这是否与习题2.11 和习题 2.12 中得到的结果一致.

解答 由力学量期望值随时间演化的公式为

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \left\langle \hat {Q} \right\rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \left[ \hat {H}, \hat {Q} \right] \right\rangle + \left\langle \frac {\partial \hat {Q}}{\partial t} \right\rangle .
$$

算符 $x \hat { p }$ 不显含时间，所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \langle x p \rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \langle [ H, x p ] \rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \langle [ T + V, x p ] \rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m}, x p \right] + [ V, x p ] \right\rangle \\ = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m}, x \right] p + x \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m}, p \right] + [ V, x ] p + x [ V, p ] \right\rangle \\ = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \frac {p}{2 m} [ p, x ] p + [ p, x ] \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + x [ V, p ] \right\rangle \\ = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \frac {p}{2 m} (- \mathrm {i} \hbar) p + (- \mathrm {i} \hbar) \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + x \left(\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} V}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle 2 \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - x \frac {\mathrm {d} V}{\mathrm {d} x} \right\rangle \\ = 2 \left\langle T \right\rangle - \left\langle x \frac {\mathrm {d} V}{\mathrm {d} x} \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

对于定态，所有力学量(不显含时间)的期望值都不随时间变化，即 $\mathrm { d } \langle x p \rangle / \mathrm { d } t = 0$ ，所以 $2 \left. T \right. =$ $\left. x \left( \mathrm { d } V / \mathrm { d } x \right) \right.$ .

对于谐振子， $V = \frac { 1 } { 2 } m \omega ^ { 2 } x ^ { 2 } , \mathrm { d } V / \mathrm { d } x = m \omega ^ { 2 } x$ 所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} 2 \langle T \rangle = \left\langle x \left(\mathrm {d} V / \mathrm {d} x\right) \right\rangle = \left\langle m \omega^ {2} x ^ {2} \right\rangle = 2 \langle V \rangle \\ \Rightarrow \langle T \rangle = \langle V \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

由于

$$
E _ {n} = \langle H \rangle = \langle T \rangle + \langle V \rangle ,
$$

所以对谐振子定态有

$$
\langle T \rangle = \langle V \rangle = \frac {1}{2} E _ {n} = \frac {1}{2} (n + 1 / 2) \hbar \omega .
$$

习题 3.38 在能量一时间不确定性原理中一个有趣的形式 ${ } ^ { \textregistered } \Delta t = \tau / \pi$ ，这里 $\tau$ 是$\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ 演变为与 $\varPsi \left( x , 0 \right)$ 相正交的态所需要的时间.利用对某个(任意的)势场中的两个(正交归一的)定态波函数的线性组合: $\varPsi \left( x , 0 \right) = \left( 1 / \sqrt { 2 } \right) \left[ \psi _ { 1 } \left( x \right) + \psi _ { 2 } \left( x \right) \right]$ 来验证该结论.

解答波函数为 $\varPsi \left( x , t \right) = \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { 2 } } \left[ \psi _ { 1 } \left( x \right) \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } E _ { 1 } t / \hbar } + \psi _ { 2 } \left( x \right) \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } E _ { 2 } t / \hbar } \right] ,$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \Psi (x, t) | \Psi (x, 0) \rangle = 0, \\ \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \Psi^ {*} (x, t) \Psi (x, 0) d x \\ = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left[ \psi_ {1} ^ {*} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {1} t / \hbar} + \psi_ {2} ^ {*} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {2} t / \hbar} \right] [ \psi_ {1} (x) + \psi_ {2} (x) ] \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {1} t / \hbar} | \psi_ {1} | ^ {2} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {2} t / \hbar} | \psi_ {2} | ^ {2} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {1} t / \hbar} \psi_ {1} ^ {*} \psi_ {2} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {2} t / \hbar} \psi_ {2} ^ {*} \psi_ {1}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {1} t / \hbar} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {2} t / \hbar}\right) = 0, \\ \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {2} t / \hbar} = - \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} E _ {1} t / \hbar} \rightarrow \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (E _ {2} - E _ {1}) t / \hbar} = - 1, \\ \left(E _ {2} - E _ {1}\right) t / \hbar = (2 n + 1) \pi , \quad n = 0, 1, 2, 3, \dots . \\ \end{array}
$$

$n = 0$ 对应的时间

$$
\tau = \frac {\hbar \pi}{E _ {2} - E _ {1}}
$$

是两个波函数第一次正变的时间，定义时同的不确定度为

$$
\Delta t = \frac {\tau}{\pi} = \frac {\hbar}{E _ {2} - E _ {1}},
$$

而

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle H ^ {2} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{2} E _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} E _ {2} ^ {2}, \\ \langle H \rangle^ {2} = \left(\frac {1}{2} E _ {1} + \frac {1}{2} E _ {2}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{4} E _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} E _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} E _ {1} E _ {2}, \\ \left\langle H ^ {2} \right\rangle - \left\langle H \right\rangle^ {2} = \frac {1}{4} E _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} E _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} E _ {1} E _ {2} = \frac {1}{4} \left(E _ {2} - E _ {1}\right) ^ {2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\Delta E = \sigma_ {H} = \sqrt {\left\langle H ^ {2} \right\rangle - \left\langle H \right\rangle^ {2}} = \frac {1}{2} \left(E _ {2} - E _ {1}\right),
$$

从而有

$$
\Delta E \Delta t = \frac {1}{2} \hbar ,
$$

得到了所谓的能量-时间不确定性原理.

串串习题 3.39 以谐振子(正交归一的)定态为基(方程 (2.68))，求出矩阵元 $\left. n \right| x \left| n ^ { \prime } \right.$ 和 $\left. n | p | n ^ { \prime } \right.$ .已经在习题 2.12 里计算过矩阵对角元 $( n = n ^ { \prime } )$ ); 用同样方法计算更一般的情况.构造出相应的(无限)矩阵 $_ { x }$ 和 $\mathcal { P }$ 证明:在该基中 $1 / ( 2 m ) \mathcal { P } ^ { 2 } +$ $\left( m \omega ^ { 2 } / 2 \right) \pmb { \mathcal { X } } ^ { 2 } = \pmb { \mathcal { H } }$ 是对角矩阵.它的对角矩阵元是你所预期的那样吗?部分答案如下:

$$
\langle n | x | n ^ {\prime} \rangle = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\sqrt {n} ^ {\prime} \delta_ {n, n ^ {\prime} - 1} + \sqrt {n} \delta_ {n ^ {\prime}, n - 1}\right).
$$

利用产生和理灭算符 解答 $a _ { + } , a _ { - }$ ，以及

$$
a _ {+} | n \rangle = \sqrt {n + 1} | n + 1 \rangle , a _ {-} | n \rangle = \sqrt {n} | n - 1 \rangle ,
$$

$$
x = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} (a _ {+} + a _ {-}); p = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} (a _ {+} - a _ {-}).
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n | x | n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left\langle n | a _ {+} + a _ {-} | n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\left\langle n | a _ {+} | n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle + \left\langle n | a _ {-} | n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left( \right.\sqrt {n ^ {\prime} + 1} \left\langle \right. n \left. \right| n ^ {\prime} + 1 \left. \right\rangle + \sqrt {n} ^ {\prime} \left\langle \right. n \left. \right| n ^ {\prime} - 1 \rangle\left. \right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\sqrt {n} \delta_ {n, n ^ {\prime} + 1} + \sqrt {n ^ {\prime}} \delta_ {n, n ^ {\prime} - 1}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle n | p | n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left\langle n \right| \left(a _ {+} - a _ {-}\right) \left| n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left(\left\langle n | a _ {+} | n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle - \left\langle n | a _ {-} | n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle\right) \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left(\sqrt {n ^ {\prime} + 1} \langle n | n ^ {\prime} + 1 \rangle - \sqrt {n} ^ {\prime} \langle n | n ^ {\prime} - 1 \rangle\right) \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left(\sqrt {n} \delta_ {n, n ^ {\prime} + 1} - \sqrt {n} ^ {\prime} \delta_ {n, n ^ {\prime} - 1}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

所以在占有数表象(能量本征态表象)，坐标与动量的矩阵为

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {X}} = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left( \begin{array}{c c c c c c} 0 & \sqrt {1} & 0 & 0 & 0 \\ \sqrt {1} & 0 & \sqrt {2} & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & \sqrt {2} & 0 & \sqrt {3} & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & \sqrt {3} & 0 & \sqrt {4} \\ 0 & 0 & 0 & \sqrt {4} & 0 \\ & & \dots \end{array} \right),
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {P}} = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \hbar \omega}{2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c c c c c} 0 & - \sqrt {1} & 0 & 0 & 0 \\ \sqrt {1} & 0 & - \sqrt {2} & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & \sqrt {2} & 0 & - \sqrt {3} & 0 & \vdots \\ 0 & 0 & \sqrt {3} & 0 & - \sqrt {4} \\ 0 & 0 & 0 & \sqrt {4} & 0 \\ & & \dots \end{array} \right),
$$

由此得到

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {X}} ^ {2} = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left( \begin{array}{c c c c c c} 1 & 0 & \sqrt {1 \cdot 2} & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 3 & 0 & \sqrt {2 \cdot 3} & 0 \\ \sqrt {1 \cdot 2} & 0 & 5 & 0 & \sqrt {3 \cdot 4} & \vdots \\ 0 & \sqrt {2 \cdot 3} & 0 & 7 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & \sqrt {3 \cdot 4} & 0 & 9 \\ & & \dots \end{array} \right),
$$

$$
\mathcal {P} ^ {2} = - \frac {m \hbar \omega}{2} \left( \begin{array}{c c c c c c} - 1 & 0 & \sqrt {1 \cdot 2} & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & - 3 & 0 & \sqrt {2 \cdot 3} & 0 \\ \sqrt {1 \cdot 2} & 0 & - 5 & 0 & \sqrt {3 \cdot 4} & \vdots \\ 0 & \sqrt {2 \cdot 3} & 0 & - 7 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & \sqrt {3 \cdot 4} & 0 & - 9 \\ & & \dots \end{array} \right).
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {H}} = \frac {1}{2 m} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {P}} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {\mathcal {X}} ^ {2} \\ = - \frac {\hbar \omega}{4} \left( \begin{array}{c c c c c c} & - 1 & 0 & \sqrt {1 \cdot 2} & 0 & 0 \\ & 0 & - 3 & 0 & \sqrt {2 \cdot 3} & 0 \\ & \sqrt {1 \cdot 2} & 0 & - 5 & 0 & \sqrt {3 \cdot 4} \\ & 0 & \sqrt {2 \cdot 3} & 0 & - 7 & 0 \\ & 0 & 0 & \sqrt {3 \cdot 4} & 0 & - 9 \\ & & & \dots \end{array} \right) \\ + \frac {\hbar \omega}{4} \left( \begin{array}{c c c c c c} 1 & 0 & \sqrt {1 \cdot 2} & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 3 & 0 & \sqrt {2 \cdot 3} & 0 \\ \sqrt {1 \cdot 2} & 0 & 5 & 0 & \sqrt {3 \cdot 4} & \vdots \\ 0 & \sqrt {2 \cdot 3} & 0 & 7 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & \sqrt {3 \cdot 4} & 0 & 9 \\ & & \dots & & \end{array} \right) \\ = \frac {\hbar \omega}{2} \left( \begin{array}{c c c c c c} 1 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 3 & 0 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 5 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 0 & 7 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 9 \\ & \dots \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

由此，到刚看出哈密顿算符在它自己的表象中是对角矩阵的(也必须是川角元素为 $\left( n + { \frac { 1 } { 2 } } \right) \hbar \omega$ $( n = 0 , 1 , 2 , 3 , \cdot \cdot \cdot )$ ，刚好是谐振子的能量本征值.

简谐振子势中粒子的波函数一般可写为串串习题 3.40

$$
\varPsi \left(x, t\right) = \sum_ {n} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} \left(x\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar}.
$$

证明 粒子的位置期望值为

$$
\langle x \rangle = C \cos (\omega t - \phi),
$$

其中实常数 $C$ 和 $\phi$ 由下式给出:

$$
C \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \phi} = \sqrt {\frac {2 \hbar}{m \omega}} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \sqrt {n + 1} c _ {n + 1} ^ {*} c _ {n}.
$$

所以，谐振子的位置期望值以经典频率 $\omega$ 振动(正如埃伦菲斯特定理预期的那样;见习题 3.19(b)).示利用方程 作为一个例子，求出习题 中披函数的 $C$ 和 $\phi$ 的值.

证明 粒子坐标期望值为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle = \int \left(\sum_ {n} c _ {n} \psi_ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar}\right) ^ {*} x \left(\sum_ {n ^ {\prime}} c _ {n ^ {\prime}} \psi_ {n ^ {\prime}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n ^ {\prime}} t / \hbar}\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \sum_ {n} \sum_ {n ^ {\prime}} c _ {n} ^ {*} c _ {n ^ {\prime}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \left(E _ {n} - E _ {n ^ {\prime}}\right) t / \hbar} \langle n | x | n ^ {\prime} \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

将方程 (3 4)(州的=~ $1 4 ) \langle n | x | n ^ { \prime } \rangle = \sqrt { \frac { \hbar } { 2 m \omega } } \left( \sqrt { n ^ { \prime } } \delta _ { n , n ^ { \prime } - 1 } + \sqrt { n } \delta _ { n ^ { \prime } , n - 1 } \right)$ 2mw 代入上式，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} c _ {n} ^ {*} c _ {n + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (E _ {n} - E _ {n + 1}) t / \hbar} + \sqrt {n} c _ {n} ^ {*} c _ {n - 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (E _ {n} - E _ {n - 1}) t / \hbar} \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} c _ {n} ^ {*} c _ {n + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t / \hbar} + \sqrt {n} c _ {n} ^ {*} c _ {n - 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t / \hbar} \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} c _ {n} ^ {*} c _ {n + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t / \hbar} + \sqrt {n + 1} c _ {n + 1} ^ {*} c _ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t / \hbar} \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} c _ {n} ^ {*} c _ {n + 1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t / \hbar} \right] + \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} c _ {n + 1} ^ {*} c _ {n} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t / \hbar} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left(C \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \varphi}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t / \hbar} + \frac {1}{2} \left(C \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \varphi}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t / \hbar} = C \cos (\omega t - \varphi). \\ \end{array}
$$

在习题 .4 中， $c _ { 0 } = \frac { 3 } { 5 } , c _ { 1 } = \frac { - 2 \sqrt { 2 } } { 5 }$ "'"和C $c _ { 2 } = { \frac { 2 { \sqrt { 2 } } } { 5 } }$ (其余部为 ，所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} C \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \varphi} = \sqrt {\frac {2 \hbar}{m \omega}} \left(c _ {1} ^ {*} c _ {0} + \sqrt {2} c _ {2} ^ {*} c _ {1}\right) \\ = - \frac {2 8}{2 5} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{m \omega}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

当 $\varphi = \pi$ 时， $C = { \frac { 2 8 } { 2 5 } } { \sqrt { \frac { \hbar } { m \omega } } }$

习题 3.41 谐振子处于这样的态，当对其能量进行测量时，所得结果是 $( 1 / 2 ) \hbar \omega$ 或 $( 3 / 2 ) \hbar \omega$ 并且得到两者的几率相等.在该态下， $\langle p \rangle$ P)最大可能值是多少?假设在$t = 0$ 时刻该可能值最大， $\varPsi \left( x , t \right)$ 是什么?

解答 由题意知波函数为

$$
\varPsi (x, t) = c _ {0} \psi_ {0} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {0} t / \hbar} + c _ {1} \psi_ {1} (x) \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {2} t / \hbar},
$$

$$
E _ {0} = \hbar \omega / 2, E _ {1} = 3 \hbar \omega / 2,
$$

并且

$$
\left| c _ {0} \right| ^ {2} = \left| c _ {1} \right| ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} \Rightarrow c _ {0} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \theta_ {0}}, c _ {1} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \theta_ {1}},
$$

其中 $\theta _ { 0 }$ 和 $\theta _ { 1 }$ 为实数，所以

$$
\Psi \left(x, t\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \left(\omega t / 2 - \theta_ {0}\right)} + \psi_ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \left(3 \omega t / 2 - \theta_ {1}\right)} \right].
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle p \rangle = \langle \Psi | p | \varPsi \rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left[ \langle 0 | \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (\omega t / 2 - \theta_ {0})} + \langle 1 | \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (3 \omega t / 2 - \theta_ {1})} \right] (a _ {+} - a _ {-}) \\ \cdot \left[ | 0 \rangle \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (\omega t / 2 - \theta_ {0})} + | 1 \rangle \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (3 \omega t / 2 - \theta_ {1})} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2} \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left[ \langle 0 | \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (\omega t / 2 - \theta_ {0})} + \langle 1 | \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (3 \omega t / 2 - \theta_ {1})} \right] \\ \cdot \left[ | 1 \rangle \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (\omega t / 2 - \theta_ {0})} + \sqrt {2} | 2 \rangle \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (3 \omega t / 2 - \theta_ {1})} - | 0 \rangle \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (3 \omega t / 2 - \theta_ {1})} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2} \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left[ - \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (\omega t + \theta_ {0} - \theta_ {1})} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} (\omega t + \theta_ {0} - \theta_ {1})} \right] \\ = - \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \sin \left(\omega t + \theta_ {0} - \theta_ {1}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

$\langle p \rangle$ 可能的最大值为 $\sqrt { \frac { \hbar m \omega } { 2 } }$ 和 $t = 0$ 时刻为脯最大姐值'则川 $\theta _ { 0 } - \theta _ { 1 } = 2 n \pi - \frac { \pi } { 2 }$ $n = 1 , \ \theta _ { 0 } = 0 .$ 则0= $\theta _ { 1 } = { \frac { \pi } { 2 } } .$ 因此，

$$
\Psi (x, t) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\psi_ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t / 2} + \psi_ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \pi / 2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 \mathrm {i} \omega t / 2}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\psi_ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t / 2} + \mathrm {i} \psi_ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 3 \mathrm {i} \omega t / 2}\right).
$$

串串*习题 3.42 谐振子的相干态.在谐振子定态中(方程 (2.68))，仅当 $n = 0$ 时的态符合不确定性原理的极限 $( \sigma _ { x } \sigma _ { p } = \hbar / 2 )$ ; 如同在习题 2.12 得到的那样，一般情况下有 $\sigma _ { x } \sigma _ { p } = \left( 2 n + 1 \right) \hbar / 2$ 但是，某些线性叠加(所谓的相干态)也会减小不确定度的乘积.它们是降阶算符的本征函数:①

$$
a _ {-} \left| \alpha \right\rangle = \alpha \left| \alpha \right\rangle ,
$$

(其中本征值 $\alpha$ 可以是任何复数.)

(a) 对态 $| \alpha \rangle$ 计算 $\left. x \right. , \left. x ^ { 2 } \right. , \left. p \right. , \left. p ^ { 2 } \right.$ .提示利用例题 2.5 中的方法，注意 $a _ { + }$ 是 $a _ { - }$ 的厄米共辄算符.不要假定 $\alpha$ 是实数.  
(b) 求 $\sigma _ { x }$ 和 $\sigma _ { p }$ ' 证明 $\sigma _ { x } \sigma _ { p } = \hbar / 2$ .   
(c) 像任何其他的波函数一样，相干态可以利用能量本征态展开

$$
| \alpha \rangle = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} c _ {n} | n \rangle .
$$

证明:展开系数是

$$
c _ {n} = \frac {\alpha^ {n}}{\sqrt {n !}} c _ {0}.
$$

(d) 通过归一化 $| \alpha \rangle$ 确定 $c _ { 0 }$ ·答案: $\exp ( - | \alpha | ^ { 2 } / 2 ) .$   
(e) 引入时间因子

$$
| n \rangle \rightarrow \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} | n \rangle ,
$$

证明: $| \alpha \left( t \right) \rangle$ 仍然是 $a _ { - }$ 的本征态 但本征值是随时间演化的，即

$$
\alpha (t) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \alpha .
$$

因此一个相干态将维持相干，并继续减小不确定积.

(f) 基于 (a) (b) (e) 中得到的结果，求出作为时间函数的 $\langle x \rangle$ 和 $\sigma _ { x }$ .如果把复数 $\alpha$ 写成如下形式将会大有帮助:

$$
\alpha = C \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \phi},
$$

其中 $C$ 和 $\phi$ 是实数.注释在某种意义上，相干态的行为是准经典的.

(g) 基态 $| n = 0 \rangle$ ) 本身是相干态吗?如果是，它的本征值是什么?

解答 (a) 因为 $a _ { + }$ 是 $a _ { - }$ 的厄米共辄算符，所以

$$
\langle \alpha | a _ {+} | \alpha \rangle = \langle \alpha | a _ {-} | \alpha \rangle^ {*} = \langle a _ {-} \alpha | \alpha \rangle = \alpha^ {*}.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle = \langle \alpha | x | \alpha \rangle = \langle \alpha | \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} (\hat {a} _ {+} + \hat {a} _ {-}) | \alpha \rangle \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {+} | \alpha \rangle + \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha \rangle\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\langle \hat {a} _ {-} \alpha | \alpha \rangle + \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha \rangle\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} (\alpha^ {*} + \alpha) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {2 \hbar}{m \omega}} \operatorname {R e} (\alpha), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle = \left\langle \alpha \right| x ^ {2} | \alpha \rangle \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left\langle \alpha \right| \left(\hat {a} _ {+} + \hat {a} _ {-}\right) \left(\hat {a} _ {+} + \hat {a} _ {-}\right) | \alpha \rangle \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {+} \hat {a} _ {+} | \alpha \rangle + \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {+} \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha \rangle + \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {-} \hat {a} _ {+} | \alpha \rangle + \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {-} \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha \rangle\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\alpha^ {*} \alpha^ {*} + \alpha^ {*} \alpha + \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {+} \hat {a} _ {-} + 1 | \alpha \rangle + \alpha \alpha\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\alpha^ {*} \alpha^ {*} + \alpha \alpha + 2 \alpha^ {*} \alpha + 1\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left[ (\alpha^ {*} + \alpha) ^ {2} + 1 \right] \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left[ 4 \mathrm {R e} ^ {2} (\alpha) + 1 \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle \hat {p} \rangle = \langle \alpha | \hat {p} | \alpha \rangle \\ = \langle \alpha | \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} (\hat {a} _ {+} - \hat {a} _ {-}) | \alpha \rangle \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left(\langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {+} | \alpha \rangle - \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha \rangle\right) \\ = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} \left(\langle \hat {a} _ {-} \alpha | \alpha \rangle - \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha \rangle\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} (\alpha^ {*} - \alpha) \\ = \sqrt {2 \hbar m \omega} \mathrm {I m} (\alpha), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle p ^ {2} \right\rangle = \left\langle \alpha \right| p ^ {2} | \alpha \rangle \\ = - \frac {\hbar m \omega}{2} \left\langle \alpha \right| \left(\hat {a} _ {+} - \hat {a} _ {-}\right) \left(\hat {a} _ {+} - \hat {a} _ {-}\right) | \alpha \rangle \\ = - \frac {\hbar m \omega}{2} \left(\langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {+} \hat {a} _ {+} | \alpha \rangle - \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {+} \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha \rangle - \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {-} \hat {a} _ {+} | \alpha \rangle + \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {-} \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha \rangle\right) \\ = - \frac {\hbar m \omega}{2} \left(\alpha^ {*} \alpha^ {*} - \alpha^ {*} \alpha - \langle \alpha | \hat {a} _ {+} \hat {a} _ {-} + 1 | \alpha \rangle + \alpha \alpha\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar m \omega}{2} \left(\alpha^ {*} \alpha^ {*} + \alpha \alpha - 2 \alpha^ {*} \alpha - 1\right) \\ = - \frac {\hbar m \omega}{2} \left[ \left(\alpha^ {*} - \alpha\right) ^ {2} - 1 \right] \\ = \frac {\hbar m \omega}{2} \left[ 4 \mathrm {I m} ^ {2} (\alpha) + 1 \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma_ {x} = \sqrt {\left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle - \left\langle x \right\rangle^ {2}} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left[ 4 \mathrm {R e} ^ {2} (\alpha) + 1 \right] - \frac {2 \hbar}{m \omega} \mathrm {R e} ^ {2} (\alpha)} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma_ {p} = \sqrt {\left\langle p ^ {2} \right\rangle - \left\langle p \right\rangle^ {2}} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2} \left[ 4 \mathrm {I m} ^ {2} (\alpha) + 1 \right] - 2 \hbar m \omega \mathrm {I m} ^ {2} (\alpha)} \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\sigma_ {x} \sigma_ {p} = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \sqrt {\frac {\hbar m \omega}{2}} = \frac {\hbar}{2}.
$$

(c)

$$
| \alpha \rangle = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} c _ {n} | n \rangle , c _ {n} = \langle n | \alpha \rangle , | n \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {n !}} (a _ {+}) ^ {n} | 0 \rangle ,
$$

所以，

$$
c _ {n} = \langle n | \alpha \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {n !}} \left\langle 0 \right| (a _ {-}) ^ {n} | \alpha \rangle = \frac {\alpha^ {n}}{\sqrt {n !}} \left\langle 0 \right| \alpha \rangle = \frac {\alpha^ {n}}{\sqrt {n !}} c _ {0}.
$$

(d) 归一化

$$
1 = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} | c _ {n} | ^ {2} = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{n !} \alpha^ {*} n c _ {0} ^ {*} \alpha^ {n} c _ {0} = | c _ {0} | ^ {2} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\left(| \alpha | ^ {2}\right) ^ {n}}{n !} = | c _ {0} | ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {| \alpha | ^ {2}},
$$

所以

$$
c _ {0} = \mathrm {e} ^ {- | \alpha | ^ {2} / 2}. \quad (\text {不 考 虑 任 意 相 因 子} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \delta})
$$

(e)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {a} _ {-} | \alpha (t) \rangle = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\alpha^ {n}}{\sqrt {n !}} c _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \hat {a} _ {-} | n \rangle \\ = \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {\alpha^ {n}}{\sqrt {(n - 1) !}} c _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} \left| n - 1 \right> \\ = \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\alpha^ {n + 1}}{\sqrt {n !}} c _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n + 1} t / \hbar} | n \rangle \\ = \alpha \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \sum_ {n = 0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\alpha^ {n}}{\sqrt {n !}} c _ {0} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} E _ {n} t / \hbar} | n \rangle \\ = \alpha \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} | \alpha (t) \rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

利用 $E _ { n + 1 } = E _ { n } + \hbar \omega$ 所以， $| \alpha \left( t \right) \rangle$ 仍然是 $a _ { - }$ 的本征态，其本征值为 $\alpha \mathrm { e } ^ { - \mathrm { i } \omega t }$

(。由 (a)

$$
\langle x \rangle = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left[ \alpha^ {*} (t) + \alpha (t) \right],
$$

(e)

$$
\alpha (t) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \alpha ,
$$

因此，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} [ \alpha^ {*} (t) + \alpha (t) ] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\alpha^ {*} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t} + \alpha \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(C \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}} e ^ {i \phi} e ^ {- i \omega t} + C \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}} e ^ {- i \phi} e ^ {i \omega t}\right) \\ = \frac {1}{2} C \left[ e ^ {- i (\omega t - \phi)} + e ^ {i (\omega t - \phi)} \right] \\ = C \cos (\omega t - \phi). \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 川=~ $\sigma _ { x } = { \sqrt { \frac { \hbar } { 2 m \omega } } }$ 标 $\langle x \rangle$ 剧频率振动

(g) 因为 $\hat { a } _ { - } \left| 0 \right. = 0 = 0 \left| 0 \right.$ ，故基态 $| 0 \rangle$ 是 $\hat { a } _ { - }$ 的本征值为 的本征态，所以是相干态.

习题 3.43 扩展的不确定性原理.①广义不确定性原理(方程 (3.62)) 指出

$$
\sigma_ {A} ^ {2} \sigma_ {B} ^ {2} \geqslant \frac {1}{4} \langle C \rangle^ {2},
$$

其中 $\hat { C } \equiv - \mathrm { i } \left[ \hat { A } , \hat { B } \right]$

(a) 证明:它可以拓展为

$$
\sigma_ {A} ^ {2} \sigma_ {B} ^ {2} \geqslant \frac {1}{4} \left(\langle C \rangle^ {2} + \langle D \rangle^ {2}\right),
$$

其中 $\hat { D } \equiv \hat { A } \hat { B } + \hat { B } \hat { A } - 2 \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle$ 提示保留方程 (3.ω) 中的实部项 $\operatorname { R e } \left( z \right)$ .

(b) $B = A$ 时，验证方程 (3.115)(在这种情况下，标准的不确定性原理是平庸的，因为 $\hat { C } = 0$ 遗憾的是，扩展的不确定性原理也没什么帮助).

解答 (均由方程 (3.59)

$$
\sigma_ {A} ^ {2} = \left\langle \left(\hat {A} - \langle A \rangle\right) \Psi \Big | \left(\hat {A} - \langle A \rangle\right) \Psi \right\rangle = \langle f | f \rangle ,
$$

$$
\sigma_ {B} ^ {2} = \left\langle \left(\hat {B} - \langle B \rangle\right) \Psi \Big | \left(\hat {B} - \langle B \rangle\right) \Psi \right\rangle = \langle g | g \rangle ,
$$

$$
\sigma_ {A} ^ {2} \sigma_ {B} ^ {2} = \left\langle f | f \right\rangle \left\langle g | g \right\rangle \geqslant \left| \left\langle f | g \right\rangle \right| ^ {2} \quad (\text {施 瓦 茨 不 等 式})
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle f | g \rangle = \left\langle \left(\hat {A} - \langle A \rangle\right) \Psi \mid \left(\hat {B} - \langle B \rangle\right) \Psi \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \Psi \left| (\hat {A} - \langle A \rangle) (\hat {B} - \langle B \rangle) \Psi \right. \right\rangle \\ = \langle A B \rangle - \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle - \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle + \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle \\ = \langle A B \rangle - \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle , \\ \langle g \mid f \rangle = \langle B A \rangle - \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sigma_ {A} ^ {2} \sigma_ {B} ^ {2} \geqslant | \langle f | g \rangle | ^ {2} = \operatorname {R e} ^ {2} (\langle f | g \rangle) + \operatorname {I m} ^ {2} (\langle f | g \rangle) \\ = \left(\frac {\langle f | g \rangle + \langle f | g \rangle^ {*}}{2}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {\langle f | g \rangle - \langle f | g \rangle^ {*}}{2 \mathrm {i}}\right) ^ {2} \\ = \left(\frac {\langle f | g \rangle + \langle g | f \rangle}{2}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {\langle f | g \rangle - \langle g | f \rangle}{2 \mathrm {i}}\right) ^ {2} \\ = \left(\frac {\langle A B \rangle - \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle + \langle B A \rangle - \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle}{2}\right) ^ {2} \\ + \left(\frac {\langle A B \rangle - \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle - \langle B A \rangle + \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle}{2 \mathrm {i}}\right) ^ {2} \\ = \left(\frac {\langle A B + B A - 2 \langle A \rangle \langle B \rangle \rangle}{2}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\frac {- \mathrm {i} (\langle A B \rangle - \langle B A \rangle)}{2}\right) ^ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{4} \left(\langle D \rangle^ {2} + \langle C \rangle^ {2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

仙)当 $\hat { A } = \hat { B }$ $\hat { C } = 0 , \left. \hat { D } \right. = 2 \left. \left( \hat { A } ^ { 2 } - \left. \hat { A } \right. ^ { 2 } \right) \right. = 2 \sigma _ { A } ^ { 2 } ,$

$$
\sigma_ {A} ^ {2} \sigma_ {A} ^ {2} \geqslant \frac {1}{4} \left(\left\langle \hat {C} \right\rangle^ {2} + \left\langle \hat {D} \right\rangle^ {2}\right) = \sigma_ {A} ^ {4}.
$$

习题 3.44 某三能级体系哈密顿量的矩阵表示为

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {H}} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} a & 0 & b \\ 0 & c & 0 \\ b & 0 & a \end{array} \right),
$$

其中 $a , b$ 和 $c$ 都是实数.

(a) 如果体系的初始态是

$$
| S (0) \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right),
$$

求 $\left| S \left( t \right) \right.$

(b) 如果体系的初始态是

$$
| S (0) \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ 0 \end{array} \right),
$$

求 $| S ( t ) \rangle$

解答首先解久期方程

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \begin{array}{c c c} a - E & 0 & b \\ 0 & c - E & 0 \\ b & 0 & a - E \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ \Rightarrow (a - E) ^ {2} (c - E) - b ^ {2} (c - E) = 0 \\ \Rightarrow E _ {1} = c, \quad E _ {2} = a + b, \quad E _ {3} = a - b, \\ \end{array}
$$

代入本征方程

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c c} a & 0 & b \\ 0 & c & 0 \\ b & 0 & a \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} \alpha \\ \beta \\ \gamma \end{array} \right) = E _ {n} \left( \begin{array}{c} \alpha \\ \beta \\ \gamma \end{array} \right),
$$

得到对应的本征函数为

$$
| E _ {1} \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right), | E _ {2} \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ 1 \end{array} \right), | E _ {3} \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ - 1 \end{array} \right).
$$

所以 $t > 0$ 时的被函数为

$$
| S (t) \rangle = c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} c t / \hbar} | E _ {1} \rangle + c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (a + b) t / \hbar} | E _ {2} \rangle + c _ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (a - b) t / \hbar} | E _ {3} \rangle .
$$

由初始条件

$$
| S (0) \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right) = | E _ {1} \rangle ,
$$

得 $c _ { 1 } = 1 , \ c _ { 2 } = c _ { 3 } = 0$ ，所以

$$
| S (t) \rangle = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} c t / \hbar} | E _ {1} \rangle = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} c t / \hbar} \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right).
$$

(b) 由初始条件

$$
\left| S (0) \right\rangle = c _ {1} \left| E _ {1} \right\rangle + c _ {2} \left| E _ {2} \right\rangle + c _ {3} \left| E _ {3} \right\rangle
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = c _ {1} \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right) + c _ {2} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ 1 \end{array} \right) + c _ {3} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ - 1 \end{array} \right) \\ = \left( \begin{array}{c} (c _ {2} + c _ {3}) / \sqrt {2} \\ c _ {1} \\ (c _ {2} - c _ {3}) / \sqrt {2} \end{array} \right) = \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 0 \\ 1 \end{array} \right), \\ \end{array}
$$

所以 $c _ { 1 } = 0$ ， $c _ { 2 } = - c _ { 3 } = \sqrt { 2 } / 2$ .

含时的态为

$$
\begin{array}{l} | S (t) \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (a + b) t / \hbar} | E _ {2} \rangle - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (a - b) t / \hbar} | E _ {3} \rangle \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (a + b) t / \hbar} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ 1 \end{array} \right) - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} (a - b) t / \hbar} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ - 1 \end{array} \right) \\ = \frac {\mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a t / \hbar}}{2} \left( \begin{array}{c} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} b t / \hbar} - \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} b t / \hbar} \\ 0 \\ \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} b t / \hbar} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} b t / \hbar} \end{array} \right) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} a t / \hbar} \left( \begin{array}{c} - \mathrm {i} \sin (b t) \\ 0 \\ \cos (b t) \end{array} \right). \\ \end{array}
$$

习题 3.45 求以谐振子能量本征态作为基矢展开的位置算符.也就是，按照 $c _ { n } \left( t \right) =$ $\left. n / S \left( t \right) \right.$ 表示出

$$
\langle n | \hat {x} | S (t) \rangle .
$$

提示利用方程 (3.114).

解答利用能量表象的恒等算符 $1 = \sum _ { n } \left| n \right. \left. n \right|$ ，将其插入 $\left. n \right| \hat { x } \left| S \left( t \right) \right.$ 中，得

$$
\langle n | \hat {x} | S (t) \rangle = \left\langle n \right| \hat {x} \left| \sum_ {n ^ {\prime}} \left| n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \right| S (t) \right\rangle = \sum_ {n ^ {\prime}} \left\langle n \right| \hat {x} \left| n ^ {\prime} \right\rangle \left\langle n ^ {\prime} \right| S (t) \rangle .
$$

将 $c _ { n } \left( t \right) = \langle n / S \left( t \right) \rangle$ 和方程 (3.114) 代入上式，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {n ^ {\prime}} C _ {n ^ {\prime}} (t) \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\sqrt {n ^ {\prime}} \delta_ {n, n ^ {\prime} - 1} + \sqrt {n} \delta_ {n ^ {\prime}, n - 1}\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \sum_ {n ^ {\prime}} C _ {n ^ {\prime}} (t) \left(\sqrt {n ^ {\prime}} \delta_ {n, n ^ {\prime} - 1} + \sqrt {n} \delta_ {n ^ {\prime}, n - 1}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

只有在饨 $n ^ { \prime } - 1 = n$ 和 $n ^ { \prime } = n - 1$ 时，括号中的两项才不为 ，因此遍历求和，上式等于

$$
\sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left[ \sqrt {n + 1} C _ {n + 1} (t) + \sqrt {n - 1} C _ {n - 1} (t) \right].
$$

习题 3.46 某三能级体系的哈雷顿量的矩阵表示为

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {H}} = \hbar \omega \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 1 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 2 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 2 \end{array} \right).
$$

另外两个可观测量 $A$ 和 $B$ 的矩阵分别为

$$
\boldsymbol {\mathcal {A}} = \lambda \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 1 & 0 \\ 1 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 2 \end{array} \right), \quad \boldsymbol {\mathcal {B}} = \mu \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 2 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 1 \\ 0 & 1 & 0 \end{array} \right),
$$

其中 $\omega , \lambda$ 和 $\mu$ 都是正实数.

(a) 求出 $\varkappa , A$ 和 $_ { \pmb { \beta } }$ 的本征值，以及归一化的本征函数.  
(b) 假设体系初始态为一般的态

$$
| S \left(0\right) \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \\ c _ {3} \end{array} \right),
$$

其中 $\left| c _ { 1 } \right| ^ { 2 } + \left| c _ { 2 } \right| ^ { 2 } + \left| c _ { 3 } \right| ^ { 2 } = 1$ ，求(在 $t = 0$ 时刻) $\varkappa , A$ 和 $_ { \pmb { \beta } }$ 的期望值.

(c) $\left| S \left( t \right) \right.$ 是什么?如果你(在 $t$ 时刻)对该态的能量进行测量，可能会得到什么样的值?相应几率分别是多少?对可观测量 $A$ 和 $B$ 回答同样的问题.

解答 (a) $\varkappa$ 已经是对角的，所以是在自己的表象中，本征值为对角元，所以

$$
E _ {1} = \hbar \omega , E _ {2} = E _ {3} = 2 \hbar \omega .
$$

对应的本征态为

$$
| E _ {1} \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ 0 \end{array} \right), | E _ {2} \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right), | E _ {3} \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 0 \\ 1 \end{array} \right).
$$

对算符 $\hat { A }$ 解久期方程

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left| \begin{array}{c c c} - a & \lambda & 0 \\ \lambda & - a & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 2 \lambda - a \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ \Rightarrow a _ {1} = 2 \lambda , a _ {2} = \lambda , a _ {3} = - \lambda . \\ \end{array}
$$

本征值分别代入本征方程

$$
\lambda \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 1 & 0 \\ 1 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 2 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} \alpha \\ \beta \\ \lambda \end{array} \right) = a _ {n} \left( \begin{array}{c} \alpha \\ \beta \\ \lambda \end{array} \right),
$$

求出本征函数为

$$
| 2 \lambda \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 0 \\ 1 \end{array} \right), | \lambda \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right), | - \lambda \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ - 1 \\ 0 \end{array} \right).
$$

同样的步骤可以求出 $\hat { B }$ 算符的本征值

$$
b _ {1} = 2 \mu , \quad b _ {2} = \mu , \quad b _ {3} = - \mu ,
$$

对应的本征函数为

$$
| 2 \mu \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} 1 \\ 0 \\ 0 \end{array} \right), | \mu \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \\ 1 \end{array} \right), | - \mu \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c} 0 \\ 1 \\ - 1 \end{array} \right).
$$

(b)

$$
\langle H \rangle = \langle \Im (0) | H | \Im (0) \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} c _ {1} ^ {*} & c _ {2} ^ {*} & c _ {3} ^ {*} \end{array} \right) \hbar \omega \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 1 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 2 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 2 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \\ c _ {3} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
= \hbar \omega \left( \begin{array}{c c c} c _ {1} ^ {*} & c _ {2} ^ {*} & c _ {3} ^ {*} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ 2 c _ {2} \\ 2 c _ {3} \end{array} \right) = \hbar \omega \left(c _ {1} ^ {*} c _ {1} + 2 c _ {2} ^ {*} c _ {2} + 2 c _ {3} ^ {*} c _ {3}\right) = \hbar \omega \left(1 + | c _ {2} | ^ {2} + | c _ {3} | ^ {2}\right),
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle A \rangle = \langle \mathfrak {S} (0) | A | \mathfrak {S} (0) \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} c _ {1} ^ {*} & c _ {2} ^ {*} & c _ {3} ^ {*} \end{array} \right) \lambda \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 0 & 1 & 0 \\ 1 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 2 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \\ c _ {3} \end{array} \right) \\ = \lambda \left( \begin{array}{c c c} c _ {1} ^ {*} & c _ {2} ^ {*} & c _ {3} ^ {*} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {2} \\ c _ {1} \\ 2 c _ {3} \end{array} \right) = \lambda \left(c _ {1} ^ {*} c _ {2} + c _ {2} ^ {*} c _ {1} + 2 c _ {3} ^ {*} c _ {3}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle B \rangle = \langle \Im (0) | B | \Im (0) \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} c _ {1} ^ {*} & c _ {2} ^ {*} & c _ {3} ^ {*} \end{array} \right) \mu \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 2 & 0 & 0 \\ 0 & 0 & 1 \\ 0 & 1 & 0 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \\ c _ {3} \end{array} \right) \\ = \mu \left( \begin{array}{c c c} c _ {1} ^ {*} & c _ {2} ^ {*} & c _ {3} ^ {*} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} 2 c _ {1} \\ c _ {3} \\ c _ {2} \end{array} \right) = \mu \left(2 c _ {1} ^ {*} c _ {2} + c _ {2} ^ {*} c _ {3} + c _ {3} ^ {*} c _ {2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

(c) 由初始条件

$$
| S \left(0\right) \rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \\ c _ {2} \\ c _ {3} \end{array} \right),
$$

所以

$$
| S \left(t\right) \rangle = c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \left| E _ {1} \right\rangle + c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 \omega t} \left| E _ {2} \right\rangle + c _ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} 2 \omega t} \left| E _ {3} \right\rangle = \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \end{array} \right) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {+ \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {2} \\ c _ {3} \end{array} \right).
$$

如果测量该态的能量值，可能得到 $\hbar \omega$ 或 $2 \hbar \omega$ 得到 $\hbar \omega$ 的儿率为 $\left| c _ { 1 } \right| ^ { 2 }$ ，得到 $2 \hbar \omega$ 的儿率为 $\left| c _ { 2 } \right| ^ { 2 } + \left| c _ { 3 } \right| ^ { 2 }$ 把 $\left| S \left( t \right) \right.$ 用算特 $\hat { A }$ 的本征态展开，

$$
| S (t) \rangle = \sum_ {n} A _ {n} | a _ {n} \rangle , \quad A _ {n} = \langle a _ {n} | \Im (t) \rangle ,
$$

展开系数的模平方 $\left| A _ { n } \right| ^ { 2 }$ 即为测量得到本征值 $a _ { n }$ 的儿率.

$$
\begin{array}{l} a _ {1} = 2 \lambda ,   P _ {1} = \left| \left( \begin{array}{c c c} & & \\ 0 & 0 & 1 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \end{array} \right) \right| ^ {2} = | c _ {3} | ^ {2}, \\ a _ {2} = \lambda ,   P _ {2} = \left| \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 1 & 1 & 0 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \end{array} \right) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} \left| c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} + c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \right| ^ {2} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {1}{2} \left(\left| c _ {1} \right| ^ {2} + \left| c _ {2} \right| ^ {2} + c _ {1} ^ {*} c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t} + c _ {2} ^ {*} c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right), \\ a _ {3} = - \lambda ,   P _ {3} = \left| \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left( \begin{array}{c c c} 1 & - 1 & 0 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \end{array} \right) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} \left| c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} - c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \right| ^ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left(\left| c _ {1} \right| ^ {2} + \left| c _ {2} \right| ^ {2} - c _ {1} ^ {*} c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \omega t} - c _ {2} ^ {*} c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t}\right), \\ \left(P _ {1} + P _ {2} + P _ {3} = 1\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

同样，对算符 $\hat { B }$ 有

$$
\begin{array}{l} b _ {1} = 2 \mu , P _ {1} = \left| \left( \begin{array}{c c c} & 1 & 0 & 0 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c} c _ {1} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \\ c _ {3} \mathrm {e} ^ {- 2 \mathrm {i} \omega t} \end{array} \right) \right| ^ {2} = | c _ {1} | ^ {2}, \\ b_{2} = \mu , P_{2} = \left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left( \begin{array}{ccc} & 0 & 1 & 1 \end{array} \right)\left( \begin{array}{c}c_{1}\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\omega t}\\ c_{2}\mathrm{e}^{-2\mathrm{i}\omega t}\\ c_{3}\mathrm{e}^{-2\mathrm{i}\omega t} \end{array} \right)\right|^{2} = \frac{1}{2}\left|c_{2}\mathrm{e}^{-2\mathrm{i}\omega t} + c_{3}\mathrm{e}^{-2\mathrm{i}\omega t}\right|^{2} \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left(\left| c _ {2} \right| ^ {2} + \left| c _ {3} \right| ^ {2} + c _ {2} ^ {*} c _ {3} + c _ {3} ^ {*} c _ {2}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} b_{3} = -\mu , P_{3} = \left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\Bigg( \begin{array}{ccc}0 & 1 & -1 \end{array} \Bigg)\left( \begin{array}{c}c_{1}\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}\omega t}\\ c_{2}\mathrm{e}^{-2\mathrm{i}\omega t}\\ c_{3}\mathrm{e}^{-2\mathrm{i}\omega t} \end{array} \right)\right|^{2} = \frac{1}{2}\Big|c_{2}\mathrm{e}^{-2\mathrm{i}\omega t} - c_{3}\mathrm{e}^{-2\mathrm{i}\omega t}\Big|^{2} \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left(\left| c _ {2} \right| ^ {2} + \left| c _ {3} \right| ^ {2} - c _ {2} ^ {*} c _ {3} - c _ {3} ^ {*} c _ {2}\right), \\ \left(P _ {1} + P _ {2} + P _ {3} = 1\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

事*习题 3.47 超对称性.考虑两个算符

$$
\hat {A} = \mathrm {i} \frac {\hat {p}}{\sqrt {2 m}} + W (x) \text {和} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} = - \mathrm {i} \frac {\hat {p}}{\sqrt {2 m}} + W (x),
$$

$W \left( x \right)$ 为某个函数.通过对两个算符按照不同次序相乘，可以构建两个哈密顿量:

$$
\hat {H} _ {1} \equiv \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \hat {A} = \frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} + V _ {1} (x) \text {和} \hat {H} _ {2} \equiv \hat {A} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} = \frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} + V _ {2} (x);
$$

$V _ { 1 }$ 和 $V _ { 2 }$ 称为超对称伴势 $\hat { H } _ { 1 }$ 和 $\hat { H } _ { 2 }$ 的能量和本征态以有趣的方式联系在一起

(a) 用超势 $W \left( x \right)$ 表示势 $V _ { 1 } \left( x \right)$ 和 $V _ { 2 } \left( x \right)$ .  
(b) 证明:如果 $\psi _ { n } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ 是 $\hat { H } _ { 1 }$ 的本征态，其本征值为 $E _ { n } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ ，那么 ${ \hat { A } } \psi _ { n } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ )是 $\hat { H } _ { 2 }$ 的本征态，且本征值也为 $E _ { n } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ . 同样证明，如果 $\psi _ { n } ^ { ( 2 ) } \left( x \right)$ 是 $\hat { H } _ { 2 }$ 的本征态，其本征值为$E _ { n } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ ，那么 ${ \hat { A } } ^ { \dagger } \psi _ { n } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ 是 $\hat { H } _ { 1 }$ 的本征态，且本征值也为 $E _ { n } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ . 因此，这两个哈密顿量有着完全相同的能谱.  
通常情况下，通过选择 $W \left( x \right)$ 使 $\hat { H } _ { 1 }$ 的基态满足

$$
\hat {A} \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} (x) = 0,
$$

同时 $E _ { 0 } ^ { ( 1 ) } = 0$ . 利用这些条件，用基态波函数 $\psi _ { 0 } ^ { ( 1 ) } \left( x \right)$ 表示出超势 $W \left( x \right)$ .(实际上，

$\hat { A }$ 使 $\psi _ { 0 } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ 逗灭本身就意味着 $\hat { H } _ { 2 }$ 的本征态要比 $\hat { H } _ { 1 }$ 少一个，缺少的本征值为 $E _ { 0 } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ .)(d) 考虑狄拉克 $\delta$ 函数势阱，

$$
V _ {1} (x) = \frac {m \alpha^ {2}}{2 \hbar^ {2}} - \alpha \delta (x),
$$

(常数 ma² $\frac { m \alpha ^ { 2 } } { 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } }$ 包含在内，因此 $E _ { 0 } ^ { ( 1 ) } = 0 .$ ) 它只有一个束缚态(方程 (2.132))

$$
\psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} (x) = \frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} | x | \right].
$$

利用 (a) (c) 部分的结果以及习题 2.23(b)，求超势 $\bar { W } \left( x \right)$ 和伴势 $V _ { 2 } \left( x \right)$ . 你可能会认识这个伴势，显然它没有束缚态.这两个系统之间的超对称性解释了一个事实，即它们的反射系数和透射系数是相同的(见教材 2.5.2 节的最后一段).

解答 刨由 $\hat { A } ^ { \dagger } \hat { A } = \frac { \hat { p } ^ { 2 } } { 2 m } + V _ { 1 } \left( x \right)$ ，等式左边等于

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \hat {A} = \left(- \mathrm {i} \frac {\hat {p}}{\sqrt {2 m}} + W\right) \left(\mathrm {i} \frac {\hat {p}}{\sqrt {2 m}} + W\right) \\ = \frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} - \mathrm {i} \frac {\hat {p} W}{\sqrt {2 m}} + \mathrm {i} \frac {W \hat {p}}{\sqrt {2 m}} + W ^ {2} \\ = \frac {\hat {p} ^ {2}}{2 m} + W ^ {2} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\sqrt {2 m}} \left[ W, \hat {p} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

结合等式右边，得

$$
V _ {1} \left(x\right) = W ^ {2} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\sqrt {2 m}} \left[ W, \hat {p} \right],
$$

以 $W \left( x \right)$ 表示出 $V _ { 1 } \left( x \right)$ 需要将上式对易括号中的结呆具体化，引入试探函数 $f \left( x \right)$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} [ W, \hat {p} ] f (x) = \left[ W, - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \right] f (x) \\ = \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar W \frac {\partial}{\partial x} + \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial x} W\right) f (x) \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar W \frac {\partial f}{\partial x} + \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial x} (W f) \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar W \frac {\partial f}{\partial x} + \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial W}{\partial x} f + \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial f}{\partial x} W \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial W}{\partial x} f. \\ \end{array}
$$

移除试探函数 $f \left( x \right)$ ，可得 $[ W , { \hat { p } } ] = \mathrm { i } \hbar { \frac { \partial W } { \partial x } }$ ;因此，

$$
V _ {1} (x) = W ^ {2} + \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\sqrt {2 m}} \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial W}{\partial x} = W ^ {2} - \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial W}{\partial x}.
$$

由于 ${ \hat { A } } { \hat { A } } ^ { \dagger } = \left( { \hat { A } } ^ { \dagger } { \hat { A } } \right) ^ { * } = W ^ { 2 } - { \frac { \mathrm { i } } { \sqrt { 2 m } } } \left[ W , { \hat { p } } \right]$ ，所以

$$
V _ {2} (x) = W ^ {2} - \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\sqrt {2 m}} [ W, \hat {p} ] = W ^ {2} + \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial W}{\partial x}.
$$

(b) :如果 $\psi _ { n } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ 是 $\hat { H } _ { 1 }$ 的本征态 其本征值为 $E _ { n } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ ，即

$$
\hat {H} _ {1} \psi_ {n} ^ {(1)} = \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \hat {A} \psi_ {n} ^ {(1)} = E _ {n} ^ {(1)} \psi_ {n} ^ {(1)}.
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {H} _ {2} \hat {A} \psi_ {n} ^ {(1)} = \hat {A} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \hat {A} \psi_ {n} ^ {(1)} = \hat {A} \left(\hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \hat {A} \psi_ {n} ^ {(1)}\right) \\ = \hat {A} E _ {n} ^ {(1)} \psi_ {n} ^ {(1)} = E _ {n} ^ {(1)} \hat {A} \psi_ {n} ^ {(1)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以 ${ \hat { A } } \psi _ { n } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ 是 $\hat { H } _ { 2 }$ 的本征态，且本征值也为 $E _ { n } ^ { ( 1 ) }$ .

同样，如果 $\psi _ { n } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ 是 $\hat { H } _ { 2 }$ 的本征态，其本征值为 $E _ { n } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ ，即

$$
\hat {H} _ {2} \psi_ {n} ^ {(2)} = \hat {A} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \psi_ {n} ^ {(2)} = E _ {n} ^ {(2)} \psi_ {n} ^ {(2)},
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {H} _ {1} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \psi_ {n} ^ {(2)} = \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \hat {A} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \psi_ {n} ^ {(2)} = \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \left(\hat {A} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \psi_ {n} ^ {(2)}\right) \\ = \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} E _ {n} ^ {(2)} \psi_ {n} ^ {(2)} = E _ {n} ^ {(2)} \hat {A} ^ {\dagger} \psi_ {n} ^ {(2)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以 ${ \hat { A } } ^ { \dagger } \psi _ { n } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ 是 $\hat { H } _ { 1 }$ 的本征态，且本征值也为 $E _ { n } ^ { ( 2 ) }$ .

(c) $W \left( x \right)$ 在此条件下的表达式，

$$
\hat {A} \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} (x) = \left(\frac {\mathrm {i} p}{\sqrt {2 m}} + W\right) \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} (x) = \left[ \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\sqrt {2 m}} \left(\frac {- \mathrm {i} \hbar \partial}{\partial x}\right) + W \right] \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} (x) = 0,
$$

整理，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} + W\right) \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} (x) = 0, \\ \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} (x)}{\partial x} + W \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} (x) = 0, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，

$$
\begin{array}{l} W \left(x\right) = - \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \ln \left[ \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} \left(x\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} | x | \right] \right] \\ = - \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \left[ \ln \left(\frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar}\right) - \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} | x | \right] \\ = \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \mathrm {s i g n} (x) \\ = \frac {\alpha}{\hbar} \sqrt {\frac {m}{2}} \mathrm {s i g n} (x). \\ \end{array}
$$

(d) (c) 的结论，

$$
\begin{array}{l} W \left(x\right) = - \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \ln \left[ \psi_ {0} ^ {(1)} \left(x\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar} \exp \left(- \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} | x |\right) \right] \\ = - \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \left[ \ln \left(\frac {\sqrt {m \alpha}}{\hbar}\right) - \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} | x | \right] \\ = \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \frac {m \alpha}{\hbar^ {2}} \mathrm {s i g n} (x) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {\alpha}{\hbar} \sqrt {\frac {m}{2}} \operatorname {s i g n} (x).
$$

将上式代入 (a) 中的结论 $\left( V _ { 2 } \left( x \right) = W ^ { 2 } + \frac { \hbar } { \sqrt { 2 m } } \frac { \partial W } { \partial x } \right)$ 得

$$
V _ {2} \left(x\right) = \frac {m \alpha^ {2}}{2 \hbar^ {2}} + \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 m}} \sqrt {\frac {m}{2}} \frac {\alpha}{\hbar} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} x} \left[ \mathrm {s i g n} \left(x\right) \right] = \frac {m \alpha^ {2}}{2 \hbar^ {2}} + \alpha \delta \left(x\right),
$$

其中

$$
\operatorname {s i g n} \left(x\right) = - 1 + 2 \theta \left(x\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} - 1, & (x <   0), \\ + 1, & (x > 0). \end{array} \right.
$$

那么根据习题 2.23(b)，可知 $\frac { \mathrm { d } \theta } { \mathrm { d } x } = \delta \left( x \right)$ 将其代入._.,.... $V _ { 2 } \left( x \right) = \frac { m \alpha ^ { 2 } } { 2 \hbar ^ { 2 } } + \alpha \delta \left( x \right)$ ma² 中，得

$$
V _ {2} (x) = \frac {m \alpha^ {2}}{2 \hbar^ {2}} + \alpha \delta (x).
$$

串串习题 算符不仅可以由其操作定义(对它作用的矢量进行操作)，而且也可以由域定义(算符作用的矢量集合).在有限维矢量空间中，域就是整个空间，我们不必担心它，但是，对希尔伯特空间中的大多数算符，域是有限的，特别地，在 $\hat { Q }$ 的域中，只允许函数 ${ \hat { Q } } f \left( x \right)$ 留在希尔伯特空间中. (正如在习题 3.2 中发现的，可以将求导算符从 $L ^ { 2 }$ 中移出 .)厄米算符的作用与它自伴算符的作用是一样的(习题 3.5).但是实际上要表示可观测量有更多的要求: $\hat { Q }$ 和 $\hat { Q } ^ { \dag }$ 的域必须是相同的.这种算符称为自伴算符，②

(a) 在有限区间。 $0 \leqslant x \leqslant a$ 内，考虑动量算符， $\hat { p } = - \mathrm { i } \hbar \mathrm { d } / \mathrm { d } x .$ 考虑到无限深方势阱，域可以定义为函数集 $f \left( x \right)$ ，且 $f ( 0 ) = f ( a ) = 0 ($ (不言而喻 $f \left( x \right)$ 和 ${ \hat { p } } f \left( x \right)$ 都在 $L ^ { 2 } \left( 0 , a \right)$ 中).证明: $\hat { p }$ 是厄米的，即 $\langle g | \hat { p } f \rangle = \left. \hat { p } ^ { \dagger } g | f \right.$ 和 $\hat { p } ^ { \dagger } = \hat { p }$ 它是自伴算符吗?提示 只要 $f \left( 0 \right) = f \left( a \right) = 0 , g \left( 0 \right)$ 或 $g \left( a \right)$ 没有限制一一 $\hat { p } ^ { \dag }$ 的域要比 $\hat { p }$ 的域大很多@  
假设对于某些固定的复数 $\lambda$ ，扩展 $\hat { p }$ 的域以包含 $f \left( a \right) = \lambda f \left( 0 \right)$ 的所有形式的函数.必须给 $\hat { p } ^ { \dag }$ 什么样的限制条件才能使得 $\hat { p }$ 是厄米的?λ 取什么值时， $\hat { p }$ 是自伴算符?讨论:严格地讲，有限区间内没有动量算符一一或者更确切地说，有无限多，没有方法去确定哪一个是"正确的". (在习题 中，我们是通过无限区间来避免这个问题的.)  
(c) 对于半无限区间。 $0 \leqslant x < \infty$ ，情况又会如何?在这种情况下，动量自伴算符存在吗? (!)

解答 (a) 由题意，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle g | \hat {p} f \rangle = \int_ {0} ^ {a} g ^ {*} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial x}\right) f \mathrm {d} x \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \int_ {0} ^ {a} g ^ {*} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} f}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar g ^ {*} f | _ {0} ^ {a} + \mathrm {i} \hbar \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(\frac {\mathrm {d} g ^ {*}}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) f \mathrm {d} x, \\ \end{array}
$$

由边界条件限制 $f \left( 0 \right) = f \left( a \right) = 0$ ，且忽略 $g \left( x \right)$ 在边界处的行为，则上式为

$$
\langle g | \hat {p} f \rangle = \int_ {0} ^ {a} \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d} g}{\mathrm {d} x}\right) ^ {*} f \mathrm {d} x = \langle \hat {p} g | f \rangle .
$$

所以 $\hat { p }$ 是厄米的.但 $\hat { p }$ 不是自伴的，因为 $\hat { p } ^ { \dag }$ 的域与 $\hat { p }$ 的域不一样， $\hat { p } ^ { \dag }$ 的域需要 $g \left( x \right)$ 满足限制条件$- \mathrm { i } \hbar g ^ { * } f | _ { 0 } ^ { a } = 0$ .

(b) $\hat { p }$ 是厄米的，需要边界项 $g ^ { * } f | _ { 0 } ^ { a } = 0$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} g ^ {*} f \big | _ {0} ^ {a} = g ^ {*} (a) f (a) - g ^ {*} (0) f (0) \\ = g ^ {*} (a) \lambda f (0) - g ^ {*} (0) f (0) \\ = \left[ \lambda g ^ {*} (a) - g ^ {*} (0) \right] f (0) \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

这里 $f \left( 0 \right)$ 不一定非得等于 O. 所以就要求 $\lambda \mathrm { g } ^ { \ast } \left( a \right) - \boldsymbol { g } ^ { \ast } \left( 0 \right) = 0$ 或 $\begin{array} { r } { \mathrm { g } \left( a \right) = \frac { 1 } { \lambda ^ { * } } g \left( 0 \right) } \end{array}$ 成立.

如果 $\begin{array} { r } { \frac { 1 } { \lambda ^ { * } } = \lambda } \end{array}$ ，也就是说 $\lambda = \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } \varphi }$ P，其中 $\phi$ 为实数，那么这两个域相等.

(c) 对处于半无限区间 $L ^ { 2 } \left( 0 , \infty \right)$ )内的函数 $f \left( x \right)$ 和 $g \left( x \right) , f \left( \infty \right) = g \left( \infty \right) = 0 .$

为消除边界条件项 $g ^ { * } f | _ { 0 } ^ { \infty } = \mathrm { g } ^ { * } \left( \infty \right) f \left( \infty \right) - g ^ { * } \left( 0 \right) f \left( 0 \right) .$ ，需要 $g ^ { * } \left( 0 \right) f \left( 0 \right) = 0$ 也可以是 $f \left( 0 \right) =$ $0 , g ( 0 )$ 不做限制，或者 $g \left( 0 \right) = 0 , f \left( 0 \right)$ 不做限制.没有办法使两个域完全等价所以，自伴动量算符在半无限区间内不存在.

# 事*习题 3.49

写出动量空间中自由粒子的舍时薛定谱方程，井求解.

答案: $\exp \left( { - \mathrm { i } p ^ { 2 } t / 2 m \hbar } \right) \varPhi \left( p , 0 \right) .$

(b) 求运动的高斯挟包(习题 $2 . 4 2 ) \varPhi \left( p , 0 \right)$ ，井构造该情况下的 $\varPhi \left( p , t \right)$ . 给出$\left| \phi \left( p , t \right) \right| ^ { 2 }$ 注意它不依赖于时间.  
(c) 通过适当评估包含 $\varPhi$ 的积分，并计算( $\langle p \rangle$ 和 $\langle p ^ { 2 } \rangle$ 然后将答案和习题 .4的结果作对比  
(d) 证明: $\left. H \right. = \left. p \right. ^ { 2 } / 2 m + \left. H \right. _ { 0 }$ {其中脚标 $^ { 6 6 } 0 ^ { 9 }$ 表示高斯定态)，并讨论该结果.

解答 (a) 对自由粒子 $V \left( x \right) = 0$ ，其含时薛定诗方程为

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \varPsi}{\partial t} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \varPsi}{\partial x ^ {2}},
$$

一般波函数是能量本征函数的叠加

$$
\varPsi (x, t) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \varPhi (p, t) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {d} p,
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \bar {\Psi}}{\partial t} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\partial \Phi}{\partial t} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {d} p,
$$

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} \varPsi}{\partial x ^ {2}} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \varPhi \cdot \left(- \frac {p ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {d} p,
$$

代入薛定诗方程，得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {i} \hbar}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\partial \Phi}{\partial t} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {d} p = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(- \frac {p ^ {2}}{\hbar^ {2}}\right) \Phi \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {d} p,
$$

$$
\frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \varPhi}{\partial t} - \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \varPhi\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {d} p = 0,
$$

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \varPhi}{\partial t} = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \varPhi .
$$

积分求解，得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} \varPhi}{\varPhi} = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m \hbar \mathrm {i}} \mathrm {d} t,
$$

$$
\ln \varPhi = - \frac {\mathrm {i} p ^ {2}}{2 m \hbar} t + C,
$$

$t = 0$ 时， $\boldsymbol { \varPhi } \left( p , t \right) = \boldsymbol { \varPhi } \left( p , 0 \right)$ 积分常数 $C = \ln \varPhi \left( p , 0 \right)$ ，所以

$$
\Phi (p, t) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p ^ {2} t / (2 m \hbar)} \Phi (p, 0).
$$

(b) 由习题 .4

$$
\psi (x, 0) = A \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x ^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} l x}, \quad A = \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4},
$$

$$
\Phi (p, 0) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi \hbar}} \left(\frac {2 a}{\pi}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- a x ^ {2}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} l x} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p x / \hbar} \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{(2 \pi a \hbar^ {2}) ^ {1 / 4}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (l - p / \hbar) ^ {2} / (4 a)},
$$

所以

$$
\varPhi (p, t) = \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p ^ {2} t / (2 m \hbar)} \varPhi (p, 0) = \frac {1}{(2 \pi a \hbar^ {2}) ^ {1 / 4}} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} p ^ {2} t / (2 m \hbar)} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (l - p / \hbar) ^ {2} / (4 a)},
$$

$$
\left| \Phi (p, t) \right| ^ {2} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi a} \hbar} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (l - p / \hbar) ^ {2} / (2 a)}.
$$

$$
\langle p \rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p | \varPhi (p, t) | ^ {2} d p = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi a} \hbar} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p e ^ {- (l - p / \hbar) ^ {2} / (2 a)} d p.
$$

令 ${ \frac { p } { \hbar } } - l = y$ ，则 $p = \hbar \left( y + l \right) , \mathrm { d } p = \hbar \mathrm { d } y$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle p \rangle = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi a} \hbar} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \hbar (l + y) \mathrm {e} ^ {- y ^ {2} / (2 a)} \hbar \mathrm {d} y \\ = \frac {\hbar}{\sqrt {2 \pi a}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} (y + l) \mathrm {e} ^ {- y ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {d} y \left(y \mathrm {e} ^ {- y ^ {2} / (2 a)} \text {为 奇 函 数}\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar l}{\sqrt {2 \pi a}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- y ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {d} y \\ = \frac {\hbar l}{\sqrt {2 \pi a}} \sqrt {2 a \pi} = \hbar l, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle p ^ {2} \rangle = \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p ^ {2} | \Phi (p, t) | ^ {2} d p \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi a} \hbar} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} p ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- (l - p / \hbar) ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {d} p \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{\sqrt {2 \pi a}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} (y + l) ^ {2} \mathrm {e} ^ {- y ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {d} y \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{\sqrt {2 \pi a}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \left(y ^ {2} + 2 y l + l ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- y ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {d} y \\ = \frac {2 \hbar^ {2}}{\sqrt {2 \pi a}} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left(y ^ {2} + l ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {e} ^ {- y ^ {2} / (2 a)} \mathrm {d} y \\ = \frac {2 \hbar^ {2}}{\sqrt {2 \pi a}} \left(\frac {2 a}{4} \sqrt {2 a \pi} + l ^ {2} \sqrt {\frac {a \pi}{2}}\right) \\ = (a + l ^ {2}) \hbar^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(d)

$$
\langle H \rangle = \left\langle \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{2 m} \langle p ^ {2} \rangle = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} (a + l ^ {2}) = \frac {a \hbar^ {2}}{2 m} + \frac {1}{2 m} \langle p \rangle^ {2},
$$

由习题 .4 ，对于高斯稳态，

$$
\langle H \rangle_ {0} = \frac {1}{2 m} \langle p ^ {2} \rangle_ {0} = \frac {a \hbar^ {2}}{2 m},
$$

所以对运动的高斯波包，其能量为稳态的能量加上披包运动时的动能 $\langle p \rangle ^ { 2 } / ( 2 m )$ .
